# VMark feature ledger

A hand-inspected inventory of every feature and capability in VMark. Tracked in the repository since 2026-09-27 so CI can hold it to the code; it is not loaded into any agent's context automatically. First built on 2026-09-07 at `75e4c5d86` (v0.9.65); **fully re-verified on 2026-09-27 at `12c98051e` (v0.9.84)**, when every block was re-read against the code, stale material was deleted rather than annotated, and features added since were inspected; then **updated at `2675ad132`** after branch `fix/feature-ledger-findings` fixed the security, capture, workflow-engine and guide findings: every open finding re-checked (269 before, 180 after, new ones included) and every block the branch touched re-read; the areas the branch's audit fixes touched (1, 3, 4, 5 and 9–15) were **re-verified at `bf8b69ceb`**, with every block and finding those fixes reached re-read. Each area records the commit it was verified against, so a reader can see how far behind it is.

This is the **qualitative half** of the ledger. Everything derivable is generated by `pnpm gen:feature-ledger` into `dev-docs/feature-metrics.md` (untracked — regenerate it locally): per-feature code size, tests, coverage, frozen debt and churn; the block counts; the at-a-glance index; and **ledger freshness** — how many commits have touched each feature's files since its blocks were verified. This file cites those tables and never restates them.

## How the ledger stays true

| Join | Checked by | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Every production source file has exactly one owner — a spine feature or declared infrastructure | `pnpm lint:feature-map` (`scripts/check-feature-map.mjs`) | `check:static`, CI |
| Spine paths exist, doc pages exist, flag defaults match `defaults.ts` | same gate (the generator's `spineErrors`) | `check:static`, CI |
| Every block names a spine feature, and every spine feature has a block | same gate | `check:static`, CI |
| Every path a block cites in `code`/`docs`/`tests` exists | same gate | `check:static`, CI |
| Every block carries every field; status tags come from the vocabulary; ids are unique | same gate | `check:static`, CI |
| Every area names a `Verified:` commit that resolves | same gate | `check:static`, CI |

The spine is `scripts/feature-map.json`: one row per feature a user would name, the paths it owns (most specific path wins), its gating setting and its guide page. Shared plumbing is listed in its `infrastructure.paths`, deliberately — "not a feature" and "nobody looked" must stay distinguishable.

## How to read a block

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `id` | stable kebab-case slug, unique across the whole ledger |
| `feature` | the spine feature (`scripts/feature-map.json`) this block belongs to; one feature may have several blocks |
| `summary` | what the user (or an AI client) gets; *Not user-facing* marks an internal seam recorded because it decides user-visible behaviour |
| `capabilities` | the concrete sub-capabilities, at the granularity a user could name |
| `status` | one or more tags from the vocabulary below — tags only; qualifications live in `notes` |
| `gate` | the settings key and its shipped default, a cargo feature, a platform `cfg`, or `always on` |
| `surfaces` | how it is reached: menu id, shortcut, toolbar, context menu, command palette, settings pane, status bar, MCP operation, CLI, drag-drop, or automatic |
| `code` | the entry points that matter, as full repo paths |
| `rust` | Tauri command names or Rust modules involved, or `none` |
| `docs` | the `website/guide` page that actually mentions it, verified by grep, or `none` |
| `tests` | what pins it, and the gaps the inspection noticed |
| `notes` | what is true now: limitations, open issue references, platform caveats, what is still wrong |

A claim the inspector could not confirm is marked **unverified** in place rather than dropped or guessed. History (what was fixed, when, by which work item) is not recorded in blocks: it is in `git log`, and a block full of history is how the first edition went stale.

## Status vocabulary

| Tag | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `shipped-on` | reachable in a default install |
| `shipped-off` | complete but behind a default-off setting (dark) |
| `partial` | reachable, but a named sub-capability is missing or reaches only one surface |
| `unwired` | code exists, is usually tested, and nothing in production reaches it |
| `dev-only` | debug builds, developer mode, or a developer command line only |
| `macos-only` | the native half exists only on macOS; other platforms get a stub or nothing |
| `windows-linux-gap` | works on macOS; degraded or absent elsewhere in a way the block names |
| `deprecated` | kept for compatibility; a replacement is named |

## Sources and method

- **Code, read directly** at `12c98051e`, again at `2675ad132` for every block and finding the fix branch touched, and at `bf8b69ceb` for every block and finding the audit fixes reached: `src/`, `src-tauri/src/`, `server/mcp/src/`, `server/content/src/`, `e2e/`, `src-tauri/capabilities/*.json`, `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`.
- **Change since the first edition**: `git log 75e4c5d86..12c98051e` (174 commits) per feature's owned paths.
- **Docs**: every `website/guide/*.md` claim a block cites was grep-checked against the page.
- **Governance state**: `.claude/rules/60-ai-governance.md` §12 for the dark-feature verdicts; `scripts/check-deleted-names.mjs` for what was deliberately retired.
- **Not consulted**: telemetry (none exists), closed-issue counts (see the metrics generator's header for why a count would mislead), and any running app — this is a reading of the code, not a manual test pass.

## Open findings

Everything below was verified true at `2675ad132`, and every entry citing a file the audit fixes changed was re-checked at `bf8b69ceb`. Each entry names the area whose blocks carry the detail and at least one evidence path. Nothing here is a recommendation, and nothing resolved is kept — when a finding is fixed, delete its line in the same change. 154 open.

### Security boundaries (14)

- (area 1) Media sources refuse URI schemes, `~/` and directory-naming paths but not absolute paths (`src/plugins/shared/mediaSecurity.ts`, `src/services/media/resolveMediaSrc.ts`). The asset scope is now `$HOME/**`, `/Volumes/**`, `/mnt/**`, `/media/**` (plus `C:`–`F:` on Windows) and runtime grants (`src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`, `src-tauri/tauri.windows.conf.json`), so a document can display any readable file in the home folder or on a mounted volume by absolute path. `website/guide/privacy.md` §"What VMark can read on disk" describes the scope; `website/guide/media-support.md` §"Security" does not mention absolute paths.
- (area 6) `export_pdf` (`src-tauri/src/pdf_export/commands.rs`, `validate_output_path`) writes to any absolute `.pdf` path whose parent exists, and `export_via_pandoc` (`src-tauri/src/pandoc/commands.rs`) to any path with an allowlisted extension (`docx`, `epub`, `tex`, `odt`, `rtf`, `txt`) and no `..`; neither is tied to the save-panel choice, and `src-tauri/build.rs` declares no per-command ACL, so any webview can overwrite such a file. The PDF window itself no longer holds an fs write grant (`src-tauri/capabilities/pdf-export.json`).
- (area 8) SSRF residual: the DNS pre-flight (`src-tauri/src/browser/ai_transactions_preflight.rs`) covers only navigations the AI issues; redirect targets, in-page link clicks and subresources are URL-text checks plus content rules for literal private addresses (`src-tauri/src/browser/ai_content_rules.rs`), and resolved addresses are not pinned, so DNS rebinding is not re-checked. Stated in `browser.md`.
- (area 8) `browser_eval` authorizes against a caller-supplied `operation` label (`src-tauri/src/browser/commands_auth.rs`); `console_clear` uses this to drain the page's DOM buffer under `operation: "read"` (`src/services/mcpBridge/v2/browserConsole.ts`). Accepted on the reasoning that every caller is in-process TypeScript.
- (area 8) UX conformance is a handwritten assertion: `src/lib/browser/__tests__/uxPolicyLedger.ts` states per surface whether the native delegates conform, and its test checks inventory and reasons only — nothing executes `nav_delegate_macos.rs` / `dialogs_macos.rs` against it.
- (area 9) The direct picker path sends document text unfenced (`fillTemplate` in `src/services/genieInvocation/extraction.ts`); `<<<DOCUMENT-DATA-…>>>` fencing applies to workflow genie steps only (`src-tauri/src/workflow/untrusted.rs`). `ai-genies.md` §"Untrusted content fencing" states this accurately; recorded as a boundary note, not a docs defect.
- (area 10) `open_workspace` one-shot approvals are not bound to the authenticated bridge principal (Codex F-10 still owed): the handler uses the constant `ONE_SHOT_CLIENT_ID = "mcp-session"`, so any connected client can consume a grant another client asked for; multi-window targeting is also deferred (`src/services/mcpBridge/v2/workspaceOpenFolder.ts`).
- (area 10) Accepted limitations kept visible: TOCTOU window between `mcp_bridge_check_path` and the fs call (`src-tauri/src/mcp_bridge/path_guard.rs`); a same-UID process can read the bridge token (`src-tauri/src/mcp_bridge/token_file.rs`); no server-side rate limit on the bridge (the token bucket is sidecar-side, `server/mcp/src/bridge/rateLimiter.ts`).
- (area 11) The Slidev export output path is unrestricted: `content_server_slidev_export` (`src-tauri/src/content_server/slidev_commands.rs`) forwards any `outputPath` the webview sends, and `POST /api/slidev/export` checks only that the extension matches the format (`server/content/src/server/createServer.ts`), so a webview caller can have a `.pdf`/`.png`/`.pptx` written to any path the user can write. The deck itself is contained to the workspace.
- (area 12) Off Unix, `action/save-file` falls back to path-based calls with a stated check-then-write race (`src-tauri/src/workflow/commit.rs`, `src-tauri/src/workflow/ensure_dir.rs`); the dir-handle anchoring (`src-tauri/src/workflow/commit_dir.rs`, `src-tauri/src/workflow/dir_fd.rs`) is `#[cfg(unix)]` only, and no Windows test runs. Restore Files writes and deletes through the same anchoring and inherits the fallback (`src-tauri/src/workflow/snapshot_write.rs`): off Unix its deletion of run-created files is `symlink_metadata` + canonical-parent check, then `remove_file`, so a parent swapped for a junction in between is followed.
- (area 14) `grant_asset_access` grants asset:// read of any file whose name and canonical target carry a media extension, anywhere on disk, to any webview JS caller; the fs scope is not extended, but which media file is not bounded (the module header states this) — `src-tauri/src/asset_access.rs`.
- (area 14) `open_file_in_new_window`, `open_workspace_in_new_window` and `open_workspace_with_files_in_new_window` grant (`grant_fs_read`) any absolute path from webview JS that canonicalizes to a regular file with a registered extension (md, txt, json, yaml, toml, html, svg, …), anywhere on disk, with the same write/remove reach — `src-tauri/src/window_manager/commands.rs`, `src-tauri/src/window_manager/path_validation.rs`.
- (area 15) `store:default` is granted to the document and Settings windows (`src-tauri/capabilities/default.json`, `src-tauri/capabilities/settings.json`); `tauri-plugin-store` resolves a store path against app data with `PathBuf` join semantics, so webview JS can load and save a store at any absolute path, writing a JSON object to any file the user can write.
- (area 15) `src-tauri/capabilities/automation-bridge.json` grants `mcp-bridge:default` (macOS and Linux) in release builds too, where the debug automation plugin is not registered (`src-tauri/src/app_plugins.rs`).

### Code nothing reaches (18)

- (area 3) The YAML branches of `createSourceEditorExtensions` (`source.yamlLint`, `source.workflowPreview`, `source.workflowCompletion`, `source.workflowCursorSync`, `source.gotoExtension`) in `src/services/assembly/sourceEditorExtensions.ts` run only when a `.yml` / `.yaml` is associated with markdown through Set File Type: the markdown `SourceEditor` is mounted only by `src/lib/formats/adapters/markdownSurface.tsx`, and an ordinary YAML file gets the yaml adapter's own extras (`src/lib/formats/adapters/yamlWorkflowExtensions.ts`) and `vmark-workflow` preview in the split pane instead.
- (area 4) Fourteen Find in Files strings — `contentSearch.placeholder`, `.searching`, `.minChars`, `.resultCount`, `.noResults`, `.noWorkspace`, `.ariaLabel`, `.caseSensitive`, `.wholeWord`, `.regex`, `.markdownOnly`, `.hintNavigate`, `.hintOpen`, `.hintClose` — are called from `src/components/ContentSearch/ContentSearch.tsx` and `src/components/ContentSearch/ContentSearchToggles.tsx` with the `editor` namespace but exist in no `src/locales/*/editor.json` (only `contentSearch.label` does), so the overlay renders its inline English defaults in all 10 locales.
- (area 5) Reopen Closed Tab restores a document tab without its content: `reopenClosedTabForActiveContext` (`src/services/workspaces/reopenClosedTab.ts`) only calls `restoreTab`, `cleanupTabState` (`src/services/windowClose/tabCleanup.ts`) freed the document state at close, and none of the `initDocument` callers covers this path. Established by reading; not reproduced in a running app. `src/services/workspaces/reopenClosedTab.test.ts` asserts only tab records.
- (area 5) Hot-exit capture runs only on an updater restart or from the dev tools — `hot_exit_capture` is invoked only by `src/services/persistence/hotExit/restartWithHotExit.ts` (via `src/hooks/useUpdateChecker.ts`) and `src/pages/settings/HotExitDevTools.tsx`; nothing in `src-tauri/src/quit.rs` or `src/services/windowClose/` captures.
- (area 5) Rust's `InspectedSession` (`src-tauri/src/hot_exit/commands.rs`) reports `lossy_repair` / `repair_summary` and says the frontend must quarantine the main file before clearing it; no file under `src/` reads either field, so `restartWithHotExit.ts` clears a lossy-repaired session after a successful restore.
- (area 5) Browser records are serialized into each workspace config's `sessionTabs` (`src/services/workspaces/workspaceSession.ts` → `serializeSessionTabs`) but `documentPathsForRestore` in `src/services/persistence/sessionTabs.ts` skips them and no other reader uses the workspace field; browser pages restore through `src/services/persistence/windowBrowserSession.ts`. The `sessionTabs.ts` comment still says browser restore is pending ("until then").
- (area 6) `getRenderedHtml` in `src/export/useExportOperations.ts` is exported with no caller anywhere in `src/`, `server/`, `e2e/` or `scripts/`.
- (area 6) The temp HTML the Export PDF window reads (`src/pages/PdfExportPage.tsx` → `readTextFile`) is never deleted after use; it persists until the one-hour stale sweep runs on a later `write_temp_html` (`src-tauri/src/temp_html.rs`).
- (area 7) `@xterm/addon-serialize` is installed and deliberately unused, pinned by `src/components/Terminal/__tests__/serializeAddonDependency.test.ts` as the enabler for deferred scrollback persistence.
- (area 9) A workflow genie's run is registered unowned: `runWorkflowGenie` (`src/hooks/useGenieInvocation.ts`) calls `dispatchWorkflowRun` (`src/services/workflow/dispatchWorkflowRun.ts`) with no `owner`, so `setExecution` (`src/stores/workflowPreviewSlice.ts`) leaves `preview.runTabId` null, and only an owning tab's panel paints statuses, offers Cancel (`src/components/Editor/WorkflowPanel/useWorkflowRunControls.ts`) or looks up the snapshot for Restore Files (`src/components/Editor/WorkflowPanel/useRunSnapshot.ts`) — so no UI shows the run's step graph, cancels it, or restores the snapshot the engine took. `website/guide/workflow-genies.md` says the run "behaves exactly as described" in `workflows.md` (live step graph, Run/Cancel).
- (area 11) `src/services/coherence/mergeAuditService.ts` (+ `coherence_merge_audit`) and `src/services/operators/operatorService.ts` (+ three operator commands) are tested and reached by nothing; frozen in `scripts/test-only-modules-baseline.json` pending D3.
- (area 11) The registered Tauri command `coherence_status` has no caller — the MCP `status` action calls `perform_status` directly (`src-tauri/src/coherence/commands_ipc.rs`, `src-tauri/src/mcp_bridge/coherence_answers.rs`).
- (area 11) No packaged build ships the content-server runtime (`BUNDLED_CLI_RESOURCE = None` in `src-tauri/src/content_server/bundle_manifest.rs`; `.github/workflows/release-smoke.yml` asserts `cli=missing`), so Knowledge Base and Slidev are developer-mode only (D1 option c).
- (area 11) The `provision` phases in `src/stores/contentServerStore.ts` and the progress view in `src/components/KnowledgeBasePanel/KnowledgeBasePanelViews.tsx` are never produced: nothing in Rust downloads, verifies or installs a content-server runtime (the unreached signed-upgrade modules were deleted).
- (area 13) 13 shortcut ids have no `shortcuts.label.*` key in any `src/locales/*/settings.json` (`graphvizDiagram, contentSearch, toggleSidebar, markdownSplit, readOnly, showInvisibles, validateMarkdown, lintNext, lintPrev, newBrowserTab, nextTab, prevTab, commandPalette`) and 4 have no `shortcuts.description.*` key (`closePane, focusOtherPane, syncPaneScroll, lastUsedTab`), so those rows are English in all 10 locales (fallback in `src/stores/settingsShortcutLabels.ts`).
- (area 13) The Shortcuts pane cannot clear a binding: `src/pages/settings/KeyCapture.tsx` only assigns a captured chord, so the store's `""` unbinding (`src/stores/settingsStore/shortcuts.ts`) is reachable only through an imported JSON file.
- (area 14) `useLintStore.runLint` and `runYamlLint` (`src/stores/documentStore/lint.ts`) have no production caller; only `src/stores/__tests__/lintStore.test.ts` reaches them.
- (area 15) `register_dock_recent` exists only on macOS, but `registerDockRecent` (`src/stores/workspaceStoreHelpers.ts`) calls it on every platform and swallows the rejection.


### User-facing behaviour with no guide page (1)

- (area 13) Shortcut import errors use a raw `window.alert` (`handleImport` in `src/pages/settings/ShortcutsSettings.tsx`), and a rejected `update_menu_accelerators` is only logged (`shortcutsWarn` in `src/stores/settingsStore/shortcuts.ts`) — neither reaches the app's dialog/toast surface.

### Platform coverage (20)

- (area 1) The table caret-scroll freeze runs only on WebKit (`src/plugins/tableScroll/scrollFreeze.ts` `isWebKit`); it is a no-op on WebView2.
- (area 2) Editor context-menu clipboard outside macOS: `src/services/editor/clipboardBridge.ts` uses `document.execCommand` for Cut/Copy/Select All and a plain-text read for Paste, so context-menu Paste loses HTML/image fidelity on Windows/Linux (keyboard `Mod+V` is unaffected).
- (area 4) Rail known limitation, still true: on macOS two case-differing spellings of one folder are two workspaces (`website/guide/workspace-rail.md` §"Known limitation"); rail-plan gap G2 (cross-window orphan on Move to New Window) remains deferred (`src/services/workspaces/finalizeInstanceRemoval.ts`).
- (area 5) Linux/Windows document metadata (ACLs) is not carried across an atomic replace; only Unix permission bits and macOS xattrs are preserved — `src-tauri/src/atomic_replace.rs` (`preserve_target_xattrs` no-op off macOS).
- (area 6) PDF tables can split mid-row on macOS and Linux because WebKit ignores `break-inside: avoid` in print (`src/export/pdfPrintCss.ts`); Windows (Chromium) honours it.
- (area 6) Print success notice: macOS and Linux report `completed`/`cancelled`, Windows always `unknown` (`src-tauri/src/pdf_export/renderer/outcome.rs`), so a Windows print never confirms.
- (area 6) PDF page numbers cannot be drawn in CJK or Cyrillic (base-14 Helvetica / WinAnsi, `src-tauri/src/pdf_export/page_numbers.rs`); the verbose format degrades to `n / total` on every platform.
- (area 7) Shell integration is unavailable on Windows (settings row hidden in `src/pages/settings/TerminalSettings.tsx`; `bash.exe` unmatched in `src-tauri/src/shell_integration.rs`), so `isShellBusy()` is always false there and workspace cd-follow can type into a foreground program; the same applies to fish everywhere.
- (area 7) #1375 (still open on GitHub): the WebKitGTK/fcitx5 orphan-compositionend ASCII drop is fixed in `src/components/Terminal/imeGateMachine.ts`, but the flicker and misplaced candidate window reported in the same issue are not addressed (commit `56acb870e`).
- (area 8) macOS-only end to end: `src-tauri/src/browser/surface_stub.rs` refuses every entry point with `UnsupportedPlatform`, and no WebView2 or webkit2gtk backend exists. On macOS ≤ 13 human browsing is not isolated in its own store (`src-tauri/src/browser/browser_store_policy.rs`, `MIN_IDENTIFIED_STORE_MACOS = 14`).
- (area 9) Off macOS `Mod-y` is both editor redo (`src/services/keybinding/undoRedoChords.ts`) and the default `aiPrompts` shortcut (`src/stores/settingsStore/shortcutDefinitions.ts`); which wins inside the editor is unverified.
- (area 10) The bridge token file's 0600 mode is Unix-only; Windows relies on the per-user app-data directory's inherited ACLs (`src-tauri/src/mcp_bridge/token_file.rs`).
- (area 11) The coherence group lock (`flock`) and ledger directory `fsync` are Unix-only; Windows skips both (`src-tauri/src/coherence/workspace_files.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/ledger_lines.rs`).
- (area 13) Two platform sniffs bypass `src/utils/platform.ts`: `src/pages/settings/AdvancedSettings.tsx` uses `isMacPlatform` from `src/utils/shortcutMatch.ts` (regex also matches iPad/iPhone), and `src/pages/settings/FilesImagesSettings.tsx` uses `navigator.platform.includes("Mac")` inline.
- (area 13) `rebuild_menu` cannot tell a malformed user chord from a platform failure; both surface as `internal` (known gap stated in `src-tauri/src/menu/commands.rs`).
- (area 14) Installed-font enumeration is macOS-only; Windows and Linux return an empty list — `src-tauri/src/system_fonts.rs`.
- (area 14) Trusted HTML preview on Windows uses `http://vmark-trusted.localhost`, derived from wry source and never exercised by a Windows run — `src/lib/formats/adapters/htmlTrust.ts`.
- (area 15) On Windows/Linux, `is_openable_supported` (`is_file()` + extension) drops a folder passed as a launch argument, so a folder cannot be opened as a workspace from the command line there (`src-tauri/src/supported_files.rs`, `src-tauri/src/app_setup.rs`, `src-tauri/src/single_instance.rs`).
- (area 15) `PREDEFINED_ICONS` matches English muda titles only, so predefined menu items get no SF Symbol in the other nine locales (`src-tauri/src/macos_menu_icons.rs`).
- (area 15) The close-to-tray icon has never run on a real Windows desktop; `src-tauri/src/close_to_tray/tray.rs` compiles only for Windows and has no unit test (decision logic is tested).

### Settings that do more or less than their name (10)

- (area 3) Source-mode `Mod+D` / `Mod+Shift+L` are literal keys in `src/services/assembly/sourceEditorKeymap.ts`, not shortcut-store entries, so they cannot be rebound in Source mode (the in-block variant `selectAllOccurrencesInBlock` can).
- (area 4) Content search's ".md" toggle is labelled "Markdown Files Only" (`src/components/ContentSearch/ContentSearchToggles.tsx`, default on) but restricts to every registered format flagged `contentSearchIndexed` — txt, yaml, and json/toml/mermaid/svg/html when their categories are on (`src/stores/uiStore/contentSearchSlice.ts`).
- (area 6) HTML export embeds only the Latin and mono font settings (`src/export/exportToHtmlFolder.ts` passes `latinFont` and `monoFont`); the CJK font setting is never embedded, and a `custom:<family>` font (#1429) or a curated family outside the Google Fonts map in `src/export/fontEmbedder.ts` silently falls back to the system stack.
- (area 7) `terminal.osc52Clipboard` ("Remote Clipboard") controls writes only; reads are refused unconditionally (`src/components/Terminal/setupOsc52.ts`). Documented in both guide pages; recorded because the setting name suggests a two-way channel.
- (area 9) Detect cannot find a CLI installed mid-session: `detect_ai_providers` returns `DETECTION_CACHE` for the process lifetime (`src-tauri/src/ai_provider/detection.rs`), while `website/guide/ai-providers.md` §"How CLI Detection Works" presents Detect as a fresh `$PATH` scan.
- (area 13) "Assign Anyway" on a conflict (`src/pages/settings/KeyCapture.tsx`) leaves both shortcuts on the same chord; for menu accelerators only one fires, and router ties are refused as `AmbiguousBindingError` (`src/services/keybinding/bindingRegistry.ts`).
- (area 13) Developer tools (`advanced.developerMode`) also reveals the Knowledge Base menu item, palette command and `Ctrl-Shift-4` (`src/services/contentServer/availability.ts`); the in-app description (`src/locales/en/settings.json`, `advanced.devTools.description`) mentions only the dev tools below it.
- (area 14) `lint.check` has no `when` predicate (`src/services/commands/lintCommands.ts`), and `runActiveLint` runs the markdown link check for every non-YAML tab with a path (`src/services/lint/runActiveLint.ts`), so a JSON/TOML/CSV file gets markdown link-check over its raw text while its own format contributes no linter.
- (area 14) The PDF export dialog seeds CJK letter spacing at `0.05em` regardless of `appearance.cjkLetterSpacing` (default off), while it seeds fonts from settings (`src/export/PdfExportDialog.tsx`).
- (area 14) `advanced.customLinkProtocols` is unioned with the defaults on every hydration, so removing `obsidian` / `vscode` / `dict` / `x-dictionary` in Settings lasts only until restart (now stated in `website/guide/settings.md` §"Link Protocols", but the UI still offers the removal) — `src/stores/settingsStore.ts`.

### Test gaps (32)

- (area 1) `src/services/media/closeCleanup.ts` has no test of its own (its callers mock it: `src/services/windowClose/windowCloseFlow.test.ts`). `src/components/Editor/MediaView/useMediaAsset.ts` (grant lifecycle, reload key) and `src/plugins/tableOfContents/TocNodeView.ts` have no dedicated tests. `src/plugins/safeBlockSplit/` has no test beside it (its pins are in `src/test/splitBlockSelection.test.ts`).
- (area 2) `src/plugins/search/__tests__/search.test.ts` mocks `@/stores/searchStore`, a module that no longer exists (state moved to `src/stores/uiStore/searchSlice.ts`); the mock is dead, and `src/plugins/search/tiptap.test.ts` still names a test "view subscribes to searchStore".
- (area 3) No dedicated tests for `src/services/commands/paneCommands.ts` (only loaded by `src/services/commands/registrationFlag.test.ts`), `src/contexts/PaneContext.tsx`, `src/services/editor/markdownSplitToggle.ts` or `src/hooks/useFormatSettingsBridge.ts` (mocked in `src/hooks/lifecycle/__tests__/useWorkspaceLifecycle.test.ts`); `src/components/Editor/DocumentSplit/useSplitDrag.ts` is exercised only through `src/components/Editor/DocumentSplit/SplitDivider.test.tsx`.
- (area 4) No test references `src/services/workspaces/workspaceConfigGuard.ts`, `src/hooks/useActiveWorkspaceScope.ts` or `src/services/files/resolveDirtyBatch.ts`; `src/services/files/applyModifyPolicy.ts` is reached by one indirect test only (`src/stores/documentStore/__tests__/externalWriterGate.test.ts`).
- (area 5) `src/services/navigation/finderOpenDispatch.ts` (its header calls the dispatch half "the one with rules worth testing on its own") and `src/components/StatusBar/useStatusBarTabDrag.ts` have no dedicated test.
- (area 5) `src/services/files/applyModifyPolicy.ts` (header: "tested as one"), `src/services/files/resolveDirtyBatch.ts` and `src/services/windowClose/fsRenameHandlers.ts` have no dedicated test file; only indirect hook tests and the `externalWriterGate.test.ts` source scan reach them.

## Area 1 — Editing — WYSIWYG blocks and content

Verified: `bf8b69ceb`

Every WYSIWYG block plugin below is registered in `src/services/assembly/tiptapExtensions.ts` (the extension array; the three block-media nodes come in through `src/services/assembly/blockMediaExtensions.ts`) in the canonical order of `src/services/assembly/compositionOrder.ts`, drift-guarded by `assertCanonicalCoverage` in `src/services/assembly/extensionOrdering.ts`. The export surface registers its own subset in `src/export/createExportExtensions.ts`. Smart Select All (`src/plugins/smartSelectAll/**`) is spine-owned by "Lists, blocks & task items" but described by block `smart-select-all` in Area 2.

### GitHub alert / admonition blocks
- id: alert-blocks
- feature: Info boxes, collapsible sections & inline TOC
- summary: Wrap or insert GitHub-style callouts (NOTE, TIP, IMPORTANT, WARNING, CAUTION) as a real block node in WYSIWYG.
- capabilities: five alert types; `alertType` validated and normalized on parse and on render; a non-empty selection wraps the whole top-level blocks it spans (`wrapSpannedBlocks`); an empty selection inserts a blank alert after the current block at a depth-aware position; source-line attr for cursor sync; also registered for export
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: toolbar Insert group (`insert-alert-note|tip|important|warning|caution`); Insert menu ids `info-note|info-tip|info-important|info-warning|info-caution`; shortcuts `insertNote` Alt-Mod-n, `insertTip` Mod-Alt-Shift-t, `insertWarning` Mod-Shift-w, `insertImportant` Mod-Alt-Shift-i, `insertCaution` Mod-Shift-u
- code: `src/plugins/alertBlock/tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/alertBlock/alert-block.css`, `src/plugins/shared/wrapBlocks.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/blockInsertPos.ts`, `src/plugins/toolbarActions/wysiwygAdapter.ts`
- rust: none (menu ids only, `src-tauri/src/menu/localized/insert_submenus.rs`)
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Info Boxes"
- tests: `src/plugins/alertBlock/__tests__/alertBlock.test.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/wrapBlocks.test.ts`
- notes: Source-mode rendering is `src/plugins/codemirror/sourceAlertDecoration.ts` (Area 3). The guide section lists the five types but not the insert shortcuts or the wrap-selection behaviour.

### Collapsible details blocks
- id: details-blocks
- feature: Info boxes, collapsible sections & inline TOC
- summary: `<details>/<summary>` collapsible sections with a clickable summary header in WYSIWYG.
- capabilities: two node types (`detailsBlock` wrapper + `detailsSummary` header); input rule on `<details>` or `:::details` alone on a line; clicking the summary toggles the `open` attr; a selection wraps the top-level blocks it spans; declines (inserts nothing) on a non-empty selection it cannot wrap (AllSelection, top-level NodeSelection); default summary text and open state come from `blockTemplates` (translated); also registered for export
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: toolbar `insert-details`; Insert menu id `collapsible-block`; shortcut `insertCollapsible` Alt-Mod-d; input rule; click-to-toggle
- code: `src/plugins/detailsBlock/tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/detailsBlock/details-block.css`, `src/plugins/shared/blockTemplates.ts`, `src/utils/markdownPipeline/plugins/detailsBlock.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Collapsible Sections"
- tests: `src/plugins/detailsBlock/__tests__/detailsBlock.test.ts`, `src/utils/markdownPipeline/plugins/detailsBlock.test.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/blockTemplates.test.ts`
- notes: Source twin `src/plugins/codemirror/sourceDetailsDecoration.ts` (Area 3). The guide section is one sentence and mentions neither the `:::details` input rule, the shortcut, nor wrap-selection.

### Inline table of contents
- id: table-of-contents
- feature: Info boxes, collapsible sections & inline TOC
- summary: A `[TOC]` block renders as a live, clickable heading list that updates as the document changes.
- capabilities: `toc` atom block node, selectable and draggable; NodeView re-renders from the document's headings (dirty-check includes heading positions) with stable slugs; click a heading to scroll to it; static `renderHTML` with `aria-label`; NodeView also runs in the export surface; round-trips to `[TOC]`; created by typing `[TOC]` alone in a paragraph (case-insensitive) or by the Insert-menu action, which places it after the current block in WYSIWYG and writes the `[TOC]` line in Source
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: Insert menu id `insert-toc`; action/shortcut id `insertToc` (no default key); typing `[TOC]`; no toolbar button
- code: `src/plugins/tableOfContents/tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/tableOfContents/TocNodeView.ts`, `src/plugins/tableOfContents/toc-block.css`, `src/utils/markdownPipeline/plugins/tocBlock.ts`, `src/plugins/toolbarActions/wysiwygAdapterBlockInsert.ts`, `src/plugins/toolbarActions/sourceInsertActions.ts`, `src/utils/headingSlug.ts`
- rust: none (menu id in `src-tauri/src/menu/localized/insert_menu.rs`)
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Inline Table of Contents"; `website/guide/shortcuts.md` (Table of Contents row, customizable)
- tests: `src/plugins/tableOfContents/__tests__/tocPipeline.test.ts`, `src/utils/markdownPipeline/plugins/tocBlock.test.ts`; conformance fixture `toc-block` in `src/utils/markdownPipeline/__tests__/conformance/fixtures.ts`; no dedicated test for `TocNodeView.ts` or `insertTocBlock`
- notes: `[TOC]` inside other text is deliberately not converted. `features.md` names both entry points (typing `[TOC]`, Insert → Table of Contents).

### Task lists / checkboxes
- id: task-lists
- feature: Lists, blocks & task items
- summary: Markdown task items render as real `<input type="checkbox">` inside list items; clicking or a shortcut toggles them.
- capabilities: `checked` attr on `listItem`; DOM checkbox NodeView (a11y); click toggles with stopPropagation; keyboard toggle at cursor; Enter splits the item and resets the new item to unchecked (`splitTaskListItem` in listContinuation); convert/unconvert the selection to a task list (`tiptapTaskListUtils`); also registered for export
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on

### List editing behaviors
- id: list-editing
- feature: Lists, blocks & task items
- summary: Enter, Backspace and clicks behave like a real list editor rather than ProseMirror defaults.
- capabilities: Enter continues the list, exits an empty item, resets task `checked` on split; Backspace on an empty item deletes it so the list stays contiguous (#790), lifts a SOLE empty item to a paragraph, and lifts a non-empty item one level at content start; a click on an empty `<li>` places the caret inside it (pos-inside-empty-li, and DOM-target-in-li via `posAtDOM`)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic (keymap, `handleDOMEvents.keydown`, `handleClick`)
- code: `src/plugins/listContinuation/tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/listBackspace/tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/listClickFix/tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/listHelpers.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: none (behavioural; not documented as a named feature)
- tests: `src/plugins/listContinuation/tiptap.test.ts`, `src/plugins/listBackspace/tiptap.test.ts`, `src/plugins/listClickFix/tiptap.test.ts`, `src/plugins/listClickFix/__tests__/listClickFix.test.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/listHelpers.test.ts`
- notes: `listBackspace` uses `handleDOMEvents.keydown` because Tiptap's core keymap binds Backspace earlier and `joinBackward` splits lists. StarterKit's ListKeymap stays enabled alongside.

### Block boundary escape, safe split and blank-line guard
- id: block-boundary-guards
- feature: Lists, blocks & task items
- summary: The caret can always leave a list, blockquote or table at a document edge, Enter never throws on a cross-block selection, and captured blank-line counts are not multiplied or reset by edits and undo.
- capabilities: ArrowUp/ArrowDown at the first/last list item or blockquote at a document edge inserts an escape paragraph (table escape runs first: tableUI priority 1050 > blockEscape 1040); IME-guarded; `safeBlockSplit` routes Enter on a selection spanning two blocks to ProseMirror's `splitBlock` (works around an upstream Tiptap `splitBlock` defect present in the pinned 3.31.3); `blankLinesGuard` nulls `blankLinesBefore` on newly created blocks, skips programmatic loads (`preventUpdate`) and stands down on undo/redo batches (`isHistoryBatch`)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic (keymaps + appendTransaction)
- code: `src/plugins/blockEscape/tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/listEscape/listEscape.ts`, `src/plugins/blockquoteEscape/blockquoteEscape.ts`, `src/plugins/safeBlockSplit/tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/blankLinesGuard/blankLinesGuard.ts`, `src/plugins/blankLinesGuard/tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/historyBatch.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: none
- tests: `src/plugins/blockEscape/blockEscape.test.ts`, `src/plugins/listEscape/listEscape.test.ts`, `src/plugins/blockquoteEscape/blockquoteEscape.test.ts`, `src/plugins/blankLinesGuard/blankLinesGuard.test.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/historyBatch.test.ts`; `safeBlockSplit` has no test in its own directory — its regression pins live in `src/test/splitBlockSelection.test.ts`
- notes: `listEscape` and `blockquoteEscape` are reached only through `blockEscape/tiptap.ts`; they are not registered directly (by design).

### Tables
- id: tables
- feature: Tables
- summary: Table editing in WYSIWYG — insert, row/column CRUD, per-cell and whole-table alignment, column resize, fit-to-width, and horizontal scroll for wide tables.
- capabilities: insert table (empty cells); add row above/below and column left/right; delete row/column/table; per-column alignment left/center/right; align-all variants; format table (align columns); drag-to-resize columns (CSS width only, not persisted to markdown); per-table fit-to-width toggle (DOM class, ephemeral, hidden while the global setting is on); global fit-to-width setting; `Tab`/`Shift-Tab` between cells; `Mod-Enter` adds a row below and `Mod-Shift-Enter` a row above; ArrowUp/ArrowDown escape at table edges; scroll-wrapper NodeView; WebKit-only caret-scroll freeze; alignment stored as a cell attribute (`AlignedTableCell`/`AlignedTableHeader`)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `markdown.tableFitToWidth = false` (fit-to-width only; the rest is always on); caret-scroll freeze is a no-op outside WebKit (`isWebKit` in `scrollFreeze.ts`)
- surfaces: toolbar Table group (15 items); menu ids `insert-table`, `add-row-before|after`, `add-col-before|after`, `delete-row|col|table`, `align-*`, `format-table`, `fit-tables`; right-click table context menu (imperative DOM, includes per-table fit toggle); shortcuts `insertTable` Mod-Shift-t, `formatTable` Alt-Mod-t, `fitTables` (no default key); palette command `view.toggleFitTables`; Settings → Markdown → Layout "Fit tables to width"
- code: `src/plugins/tableUI/tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/tableUI/tableActions.tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/tableUI/TiptapTableContextMenu.ts`, `src/plugins/tableUI/columnResize.ts`, `src/plugins/tableUI/tableEscape.ts`, `src/plugins/tableUI/tableDom.ts`, `src/plugins/tableScroll/{plugin,scrollFreeze,scrollGuard}.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/tableFitToWidth.ts`, `src/components/Editor/alignedTableNodes.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Tables" (incl. `Tab`/`Shift + Tab`, edge escape, `Mod + Enter` / `Mod + Shift + Enter`); `website/guide/popups.md` §"Table Context Menu" (right-click in both modes, WYSIWYG-only Fit to Width / Natural Width); `website/guide/settings.md` §"Layout"; `website/guide/shortcuts.md`
- tests: `src/plugins/tableUI/{tiptap,columnResize,tableDom,tableEscape,tableEscape.edge-cases,tableActions.tiptap,TiptapTableContextMenu}.test.ts`, `src/plugins/tableUI/__tests__/TiptapTableContextMenu.test.ts`, `src/plugins/tableScroll/{plugin,scrollFreeze,scrollGuard}.test.ts`, `src/components/Editor/__tests__/tableFitToWidthCss.test.ts`
- notes: `TableWithScrollWrapper.configure({ resizable: false })` — Tiptap's own column resizing is off in favour of the CSS-only resize. Column widths and per-table fit are lost on reload by design. The scroll wrapper cites #754 (scrollToSelection loop). `tableUI/tiptap.ts` header says plain Enter adds a row; the keymap binds `Mod-Enter` and `Mod-Shift-Enter`, and plain Enter is not bound.

### Math (inline + block)
- id: math
- feature: Math / LaTeX
- summary: KaTeX-rendered inline `$…$` and display `$$…$$` math, editable in place with a live preview.
- capabilities: `math_inline` atom with an inline NodeView (preview ↔ input modes, floating `MathPreviewView` while typing, arrow-key exit); in a scrolling editor an inline formula renders only once its top-level block comes within 2000px of the viewport, nearest first under a 6 ms per-frame budget, showing its source until then (`nearViewport`; the off-screen export surface still renders everything, print flushes the live editor first, navigation scrolls settle while renders are due, and a tab remount restores the block the reader left at the top); repeated sources render once and are cloned (2,000-entry LRU); KaTeX's font faces preload at editor creation; every `.katex-base` is unpositioned inside inline math so it is not a render layer, while `.katex` stays positioned as the containing block of the hidden MathML copy; display math as a `$$math$$` sentinel code block rendered by codePreview with click-to-edit; `mathPopup` — popup with live KaTeX preview, its store injected as a port; KaTeX lazy-loaded; LaTeX error parsing into readable messages; `blockMathKeymap` (Escape / click-outside revert, Mod-Enter commit); `\tag{}` numbers sit at the block's right edge (unscoped `.katex-display { width: 100% }` in `src/styles/katexFixes.css`, loaded by app and export)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: toolbar `insert-math`; Insert menu id `math-block`; shortcut `mathBlock` Alt-Mod-Shift-m; shortcut `inlineMath` Alt-Mod-m (no menu id; routed via `editorPlugins.tiptap.ts` → `runEditorAction("insertInlineMath")`); click / double-click to edit
- code: `src/plugins/latex/tiptapInlineMath.ts`, `src/plugins/latex/MathInlineNodeView.ts`, `src/plugins/latex/scheduleInlineMathRender.ts`, `src/plugins/latex/inlineMathRenderCache.ts`, `src/plugins/latex/katexFontPreload.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/nearViewport.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/editorScrollRoot.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/renderMarks.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/renderQueue.ts`, `src/plugins/latex/inlineMathEditingRegistry.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/katexLoader.ts`, `src/plugins/latex/latexErrorParser.ts`, `src/plugins/mathPopup/MathPopupView.ts`, `src/plugins/mathPreview/MathPreviewView.ts`, `src/plugins/codePreview/blockMathKeymap.ts`, `src/plugins/codePreview/renderers/renderLatex.ts`, `src/stores/mathPopupStore.ts`, `src/stores/blockMathEditingStore.ts`, `src/styles/katexFixes.css`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Mathematical Equations"; `website/guide/popups.md` §"Math Popup"; `website/guide/large-files.md` §"Known limitations" (inline math renders near the viewport)
- tests: `src/plugins/latex/{tiptapInlineMath,MathInlineNodeView,MathInlineNodeView.deferred,inlineMathRenderCache,katexFontPreload,latexErrorParser,inlineMathEditingRegistry,displayMathTag}.test.ts`, `src/plugins/latex/__tests__/MathInlineNodeView.test.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/{nearViewport,nearViewport.prosemirror,editorScrollRoot,renderQueue,katexLoader}.test.ts`, `src/plugins/latex/inlineMathLayers.webkit.test.ts`, `src/plugins/codePreview/displayMathTag.webkit.test.ts`, `src/plugins/mathPopup/tiptap.test.ts`, `src/plugins/mathPopup/__tests__/{MathPopupView,mathPopupStore}.test.ts`, `src/plugins/mathPreview/MathPreviewView.test.ts`, `src/plugins/codePreview/blockMathKeymap.test.ts`, `src/plugins/codePreview/renderers/renderLatex.test.ts`
- notes: `katexLoader.ts` clears its cached load promise on rejection, so a failed KaTeX chunk load is retried on the next render, as the mermaid, graphviz and markmap loaders do. The math toolbar button emits the `MATH_BLOCK_LANGUAGE` sentinel (serializes to `$$`) rather than a ```latex fence (`wysiwygAdapterBlockInsert.ts`). Fixed #1376 / #1402 (tag overlap). A formula once rendered is never un-rendered, so the positioned-element count still grows as a reader moves deeper into a math-heavy document (#1473). Where the engine has no CSS scroll anchoring (WKWebView before macOS 27, WebKitGTK), a formula rendered above the viewport moves the visible text by the height it gained.

### Trusted HTML preview (standalone .html/.htm)
- id: trusted-html-preview
- feature: Trusted HTML preview (standalone .html)
- summary: A standalone HTML file can be explicitly authorized, per file per session, to run its own scripts in an origin-isolated frame served from `vmark-trusted://`.
- capabilities: Enable (after a warning) / Reload (republish after edit) / Revoke; "may not match the current source" marker when the frame may be stale; grant token from the single CSPRNG minter (`secret_token`); exact-path grants only, never persisted, never inferred from extension or path, refused for unsaved documents; caps `MAX_DOC_BYTES` 16 MiB, `MAX_GRANTS` 64, `MAX_TOTAL_BYTES` 64 MiB; each grant is owned by the authorizing window and revoked on `WindowEvent::Destroyed`; iframe `sandbox="allow-scripts"` only, empty `allow`; own CSP (`default-src 'none'`, no network); Windows served from `http://vmark-trusted.localhost`
- status: shipped-off
- gate: `formats.htmlPreview = false` — the HTML format adapter (and so the trust bar) registers only when HTML preview is enabled; then per-file explicit confirmation. The default preview stays DOMPurify + empty `sandbox` + `default-src 'none'` meta CSP.
- surfaces: `HtmlTrustBar` above the HTML preview ("Enable trusted preview…", "Revoke trust", Reload); Settings → Formats (HTML preview toggle)
- code: `src/services/trustedHtml/trustedHtmlBridge.ts`, `src/stores/htmlTrustStore.ts`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/htmlTrust.ts`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/useHtmlTrust.ts`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/HtmlTrustBar.tsx`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/HtmlPreview.tsx`
- rust: `src-tauri/src/trusted_html/{mod,state,protocol,commands}.rs`; commands `trusted_html_grant`, `trusted_html_publish`, `trusted_html_revoke`; scheme registered in `src-tauri/src/lib.rs`; CSP `frame-src 'self' vmark-trusted: http://vmark-trusted.localhost` in `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`; window teardown in `src-tauri/src/window_manager/window_events.rs`
- docs: `website/guide/formats.md` §"Trusted HTML preview (opt-in)"
- tests: `src-tauri/src/trusted_html/{commands,protocol,state,tauri_pin}.test.rs` (`tauri_pin` fails on a Tauri bump, forcing re-verification of the invoke-key property); `src/services/trustedHtml/trustedHtmlBridge.test.ts`, `src/stores/htmlTrustStore.test.ts`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/{htmlTrust.test.ts,HtmlTrustBar.test.tsx,HtmlPreview.test.tsx}`
- notes: issue #1273. `protocol.rs` records that the IPC boundary rests on one undocumented Tauri internal plus the invoke key, with no ACL second layer. `trusted_html/mod.rs` still says a grant ends only on revoke or process exit and that a destroyed webview leaves its grants resident — both contradicted by `revoke_window` in `window_events.rs`; `state.rs` and `commands.rs` name `../app_setup.rs` as the caller, which it is not.

### Footnotes

### Code blocks (gutter, language picker, copy, run)
- id: code-blocks
- feature: Focus / typewriter / view toggles
- summary: WYSIWYG code blocks get syntax highlighting, an optional line-number gutter, a copy button, a searchable language chip, and a Run button for shell fences.

- code: `src/plugins/codeBlockLineNumbers/tiptap.ts`, `src/plugins/codeBlockLineNumbers/nodeView.ts`, `src/plugins/codeBlockLineNumbers/nodeViewActions.ts`, `src/plugins/codeBlockLineNumbers/dropdown.ts`, `src/plugins/codeBlockLineNumbers/languages.ts`, `src/services/terminal/runInTerminal.ts`
- rust: none directly (Run routes through the terminal, Area 7)
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"Layout"
- tests: `src/plugins/codeBlockLineNumbers/{tiptap,hljsSyntax}.test.ts`, `src/plugins/codeBlockLineNumbers/__tests__/{CodeBlockNodeView,codeBlockRunButton,copyButton,languageApply,languages.registry,nodeViewActions}.test.ts`
- notes: spine-owned by Area 2's view toggles. The gutter setting is independent of the `lineNumbers` shortcut (Alt-Mod-l), which toggles the Source/Split gutter (#1082). Header: line numbers are recounted on every DOM mutation. `tiptap.ts` header still says it "reads view.lineNumbers setting" from `stores/settingsStore.ts`; it imports no store.

### Smart code paste

### Block media nodes (image / video / audio / embeds)
- id: block-media-nodes
- feature: Media (image/video/audio)
- summary: Standalone images, videos, audio players and provider embeds render as block nodes with their own node views; inline images get their own NodeView.
- capabilities: `block_image`, `block_video`, `block_audio` registered as a group bound to the owning tab (`ownerTabId`, so a relative `src` resolves against THIS document in a split, #1081); `atom` single-selectable units; arrow keys navigate in/out; Enter on a selected media node creates a paragraph below; async src resolution with loading/error classes and a disk-change reload key (#1328); relative paths may climb with `..` (#1433); video `controls` + `preload: "metadata"`; `video_embed` for YouTube (nocookie), Vimeo and Bilibili with a responsive wrapper and click overlay; inline `image` with its own NodeView (click selects, double-click opens the popup); an inline image may carry marks, so a link or emphasis around it round-trips
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: toolbar `insert-image`/`insert-video`/`insert-audio`; Insert menu ids `image`/`video`/`audio`; shortcut `insertImage` Shift-Mod-i (video/audio unbound); double-click opens the media popup; right-click on an image opens the image context menu
- code: `src/plugins/blockImage/{tiptap,BlockImageNodeView}.ts`, `src/plugins/blockVideo/{tiptap,BlockVideoNodeView}.ts`, `src/plugins/blockAudio/{tiptap,BlockAudioNodeView}.ts`, `src/plugins/videoEmbed/{tiptap,VideoEmbedNodeView}.ts`, `src/plugins/imageView/{tiptap,plugin,resolveSrc}.ts`, `src/services/assembly/blockMediaExtensions.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/mediaNodeViewHelpers.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/mediaSecurity.ts`, `src/services/media/resolveMediaSrc.ts`, `src/utils/videoProviderRegistry.ts`
- rust: `grant_asset_access` (`src-tauri/src/asset_access.rs`)
- docs: `website/guide/media-support.md`; `website/guide/features.md` §"Images", §"Video & Audio"
- tests: `src/plugins/blockImage/tiptap.test.ts`, `src/plugins/blockImage/__tests__/{blockImage,BlockImageNodeView}.test.ts`, `src/plugins/blockVideo/__tests__/{blockVideo,BlockVideoNodeView}.test.ts`, `src/plugins/blockAudio/__tests__/{blockAudio,BlockAudioNodeView}.test.ts`, `src/plugins/imageView/{tiptap,resolveSrc}.test.ts`, `src/plugins/videoEmbed/__tests__/{tiptap,tiptap.paste,urlParser,VideoEmbedNodeView}.test.ts`, `src/plugins/shared/mediaSecurity.test.ts`, `src/services/media/resolveMediaSrc.test.ts`
- notes: no resize exists — images render at natural size. `BlockImageNodeView.ts` known limitation: no lazy loading. Media paths refuse URI schemes, `~/` and directory-naming paths but not absolute paths (`src/plugins/shared/mediaSecurity.ts`). The asset scope is `$HOME/**`, `/Volumes/**`, `/mnt/**`, `/media/**` (plus `C:`–`F:` via `src-tauri/tauri.windows.conf.json`) and runtime grants (`src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`), so an absolute path anywhere in those roots renders, and an image beside a document opened alone from outside them does not render until its folder is opened as a workspace (`website/guide/privacy.md` §"What VMark can read on disk"). `mediaSecurity.ts` still describes the scope as `**`. Clicking a link around an image is handled by linkPopup (Area 2).

### Asset folder management (dedup, auto-resize, orphan cleanup)
- id: asset-management
- feature: Media (image/video/audio)
- summary: Media copied into a document's assets folder is deduplicated by content hash, optionally downscaled, and unreferenced files can be cleaned up.
- capabilities: hash-based dedup registry (`image-hashes.json`), skipped for files above 50 MB; canvas downscale to a max dimension before save (Off / 800 / 1200 / 1920 / 2560); relative paths for portability; orphan scan consulting sibling documents (disk or live unsaved buffers), every open buffer, other VMark windows over IPC, and a workspace content search; fail-closed (`scanComplete: false` protects everything); deletions go to the system Trash (`move_paths_to_trash`); manual cleanup with preview + re-scan before delete; optional cleanup on tab/window/app close
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `image.autoResizeMax = 0` (off), `image.copyToAssets = true`, `image.cleanupOrphansOnClose = false`
- surfaces: Settings → Images (`src/pages/settings/FilesImagesSettings.tsx`); Format → Text Cleanup → Clean Up Unused Images (menu id `cleanup-images`, palette `image.cleanupOrphans`); automatic on close when enabled
- code: `src/services/media/{imageOperations,mediaOperations,imageResize,imageHashRegistry,orphanAssetCleanup,orphanCleanupPrompt,closeCleanup,siblingReferences,liveDocumentContents,crossWindowRefs,workspaceReferenceCheck,clipboardImagePath}.ts`, `src/utils/imageReferenceScanner.ts`, `src/utils/imageHash.ts`
- rust: `src-tauri/src/live_docs.rs` (`collect_live_document_refs`, `live_docs_response`); `search_workspace_content_checked`
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"Images"; `website/guide/features.md` §"Image Cleanup (Format → Text Cleanup → Clean Up Unused Images…)" (preview + confirm, Trash, live buffers in other windows, fail-closed)
- tests: `src/services/media/{imageOperations,mediaOperations,imageResize,imageHashRegistry,orphanAssetCleanup,orphanCleanupPrompt,liveDocumentContents,crossWindowRefs,workspaceReferenceCheck,clipboardImagePath}.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/live_docs.test.rs`, `src/test/adversarialMediaSources.ts`; `closeCleanup.ts` has no test of its own (callers mock it) and `siblingReferences.ts` is covered only through `orphanAssetCleanup.test.ts`
- notes: Outside workspace mode there is no workspace index to consult.

### Media file viewer (image / video / audio tabs)
- id: media-file-viewer
- feature: Media (image/video/audio)
- summary: Opening an image, video or audio file shows it read-only in a tab; the same render core backs Quick Look.
- capabilities: `media` format adapter (every media extension except `svg`) routed to a full-width `MediaViewer` rather than a text editor; file never read as text; asset URL via `grant_asset_access` + `convertFileSrc`; cache-busting reload key from the document id so an on-disk change re-renders (#1328); fallback panel with open-with-default-app and reveal for undecodable formats; media tabs never become dirty

- notes: `grant_asset_access` extends only the asset-protocol scope, never the fs scope, and only for a path whose name and canonical target both carry a media extension; any readable media file anywhere on disk is still grantable from the webview — the module header records this.

## Area 2 — Editing — inline behaviour, popups, toolbar, find

Verified: `2675ad132`

Every WYSIWYG block below is registered in `buildExtensionList` (`src/services/assembly/tiptapExtensions.ts`) and ordered by `WYSIWYG_COMPOSITION_ORDER` (`src/services/assembly/compositionOrder.ts`); settings and stores reach plugins only through the `plugins/shared/host*` seams bound once in `src/bootstrap.tsx` (block *plugin-host-seams*). Source-mode popups and Source Peek are described in Area 3 (*source-popups*, *source-peek*); the footnote and math popups in Area 1 (*footnotes*, *math*); code-block line numbers in Area 1 (*code-blocks*).

### Inline mark input rules (CJK-aware bold/italic)
- id: mark-input-rules
- feature: Inline marks & formatting
- summary: Typing `**text**`, `*text*`, `__text__` or `_text_` turns into bold/italic as you type, and the same rules apply to pasted text — with lookbehind regexes so CJK text without spaces (`你好**世界**`) also triggers.
- capabilities: `**`/`__` bold input and paste rules; `*`/`_` italic input and paste rules; CommonMark intraword-`_` rejection extended to all Unicode letters and numbers (Cyrillic, Greek, Arabic snake_case); Han/Hiragana/Katakana/Bopomofo allowed as emphasis boundaries; URL-range exclusion so `__init__.py` or `_drafts_/post` inside a pasted URL never emphasizes
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on (StarterKit `bold`/`italic` are disabled and replaced by `CJKBold`/`CJKItalic`)
- surfaces: automatic (typing, paste)
- code: `src/plugins/markInputRules/tiptap.ts`; `src/plugins/markInputRules/urlSafePasteRule.ts`; `src/services/assembly/tiptapExtensions.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Basic Styles" lists bold/italic but not the as-you-type input rule
- tests: `src/plugins/markInputRules/__tests__/markInputRules.test.ts`; `src/plugins/markInputRules/__tests__/urlSafePasteRule.test.ts`
- notes: The CJK boundary is a deliberate divergence from CommonMark, documented in the module header.

### Inline code cursor boundary
- id: inline-code-boundary
- feature: Inline marks & formatting
- summary: Typing at the left edge of an inline `code` span inserts inside the span rather than outside it.
- capabilities: `appendTransaction` sets `storedMarks` to include the code mark at the left boundary; skipped during IME composition
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic
- code: `src/plugins/inlineCodeBoundary/plugin.ts`; `src/plugins/inlineCodeBoundary/tiptap.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Editing Details"
- tests: `src/plugins/inlineCodeBoundary/__tests__/inlineCodeBoundary.test.ts`
- notes: The IME skip cites upstream ProseMirror #1476 (setting `storedMarks` mid-composition corrupts mark state). `src/plugins/tabIndent/shiftTabEscape.ts` relies on the `storedMarks` this plugin sets for single-cursor Shift+Tab escape; multi-cursor reads `nodeAfter.marks` instead.

### Highlight, underline, subscript and superscript marks
- id: inline-marks-extended
- feature: Inline marks & formatting
- summary: Four marks beyond StarterKit — `==highlight==`, underline, `~sub~` and `^sup^` — each with a rebindable shortcut, a menu item and a toolbar button.
- capabilities: `highlight` mark (`<mark>`); `underline` mark (`<u>`, replaces StarterKit's, which is disabled); `subscript` (`<sub>`) and `superscript` (`<sup>`) marks; shortcuts `highlight` `Mod-Shift-m`, `underline` `Mod-u`, `subscript` `Alt-Mod-=`, `superscript` `Alt-Mod-Shift-=` routed through `runEditorAction`; own CSS each; no attributes (no highlight colours); also in the export extension set
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: shortcut ids `highlight`, `underline`, `subscript`, `superscript` (menu ids of the same names); Universal Toolbar Inline group; command palette
- code: `src/plugins/highlight/tiptap.ts`; `src/plugins/underline/tiptap.ts`; `src/plugins/subSuperscript/tiptap.ts`; `src/plugins/editorPlugins.tiptap.ts`
- rust: menu items in `src-tauri/src/menu/localized/format_menu.rs` with the matching accelerators
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Basic Styles"; `website/guide/shortcuts.md`
- tests: `src/plugins/highlight/tiptap.test.ts`; `src/plugins/underline/__tests__/underline.test.ts`; `src/plugins/subSuperscript/__tests__/subSuperscript.test.ts`
- notes: Markdown round-trip of these marks lives in the markdown pipeline (Area 3), not in these extensions, which only declare schema and HTML parse/render.

### Case transforms and line operations
- id: text-transforms
- feature: Inline marks & formatting
- summary: Change the case of the selection and move, duplicate, delete, join, sort or de-blank lines, in both editors.
- capabilities: UPPERCASE, lowercase, Title Case (Unicode word boundaries), toggle case; move line up/down; duplicate line; delete line; join lines; remove blank lines; sort lines ascending/descending (Source only)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: shortcut ids `transformUppercase` (`Ctrl-Shift-u`, `Alt-Shift-u` off macOS), `transformLowercase` (`Ctrl-Shift-l` / `Alt-Shift-l`), `transformTitleCase` (`Ctrl-Shift-t` / `Alt-Shift-t`), `transformToggleCase` (unbound), `moveLineUp` `Alt-Up`, `moveLineDown` `Alt-Down`, `duplicateLine` `Shift-Alt-Down`, `deleteLine` `Mod-Shift-k`, `joinLines` `Mod-j`, `removeBlankLines` (unbound), `sortLinesAsc` `F4`, `sortLinesDesc` `Shift-F4`; Format → Transform and Edit → Lines menus; command palette
- code: `src/utils/caseTransformations.ts`; `src/utils/textTransformations.ts`; `src/plugins/toolbarActions/wysiwygAdapterFormatting.ts`; `src/plugins/toolbarActions/sourceTextTransforms.ts`; `src/plugins/editorPlugins.tiptap.ts`
- rust: menu items in `src-tauri/src/menu/localized/format_menu.rs` (transforms) and `src-tauri/src/menu/localized/edit_menu.rs` (lines)
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Text Transformations", §"Line Operations"; `website/guide/shortcuts.md` §"Line Operations", §"Text Transformations"
- tests: `src/utils/caseTransformations.test.ts`; `src/utils/textTransformations.test.ts`
- notes: Sort lines is Source-only (`src/plugins/toolbarActions/adapterActions.ts` lists it as a source-only action); `features.md` §"Line Operations" and `shortcuts.md` §"Line Operations" say so, but `shortcuts.md` §"F-Key Quick Reference" lists `F4` / `Shift + F4` without the caveat. `caseTransformations.ts` is reached only through the re-export in `textTransformations.ts`.

### Link popup (edit existing link)
- id: link-popup
- feature: Inline popups
- summary: Clicking a link opens its edit popup while the caret stays in the document; `Mod+Click` follows the link instead — to a heading, the browser, or a local file in a new tab.
- capabilities: pointer-opened popup does not take focus (typing, Backspace and copy/paste keep acting on the document) and closes once edits make its snapshot stale or the caret moves to another link; `Mod+K` opens it with focus in the URL field; URL edit, open, copy, delete; link span includes a marked inline image (`[text ![a](p.png)](A.md)`); `Mod+Click` routing via `openLinkTarget` — `#fragment` heading jump, external URL in the browser, absolute or relative file path in a new tab landing on the `#fragment` heading; UNC/network and drive-relative paths refused; a DOM listener marks link-anchor clicks handled so the opener plugin does not also open them
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; extra schemes via `advanced.customLinkProtocols = ["obsidian","vscode","dict","x-dictionary"]`
- surfaces: mouse click / `Mod+Click`; shortcut id `link` (`Mod-k`); Universal Toolbar Link group; editor context menu Edit/Copy/Remove Link
- code: `src/plugins/linkPopup/tiptap.ts`; `src/plugins/linkPopup/LinkPopupView.ts`; `src/plugins/linkPopup/findLinkMarkRange.ts`; `src/plugins/linkPopup/linkRange.ts`; `src/plugins/linkPopup/operations.ts`; `src/plugins/linkPopup/linkPopupDom.ts`; `src/stores/linkPopupStore.ts`; `src/services/navigation/linkOpen.ts`; `src/services/assembly/linkExtension.ts`
- rust: none directly (external URLs go through the Tauri opener plugin)
- docs: `website/guide/popups.md` §"Link Popup", §"Edit Existing Link", §"Source Mode" (Cross-File Links tip)
- tests: `src/plugins/linkPopup/tiptap.test.ts`; `src/plugins/linkPopup/tiptapPointer.test.ts`; `src/plugins/linkPopup/findLinkMarkRange.test.ts`; `src/plugins/linkPopup/linkRange.test.ts`; `src/plugins/linkPopup/operations.test.ts`; `src/plugins/linkPopup/LinkPopupView.test.ts`; `src/plugins/linkPopup/__tests__/LinkPopupView.test.ts`; `src/stores/linkPopupStore.test.ts`; `src/services/navigation/linkOpen.test.ts`
- notes: `linkOpen.ts` blocks `file:` and `javascript:` below any `customLinkProtocols` entry, which it treats as untrusted localStorage data. `vmarkLinkExtension` carries `title`, `referenceId` and `referenceType` so reference-style links are not rewritten inline on the first edit. Source mode shares `openLinkTarget` (Area 3 *source-popups*).

### Link create popup

### Wiki link popup
- id: wiki-link-popup
- feature: Inline popups
- summary: Hovering a `[[wiki link]]` for 300 ms opens a popup to edit its target, browse for a file, open, copy or remove it.

- surfaces: mouse hover; shortcut id `wikiLink` (`Alt-Mod-k`) inserts a wiki link

- notes: The module header also promises click-to-edit and `Mod+Click` to open the target; the plugin binds only mouseover/mouseout and no WYSIWYG click handler for wiki links was found, so both are unverified. The wiki-link node itself is in `src/plugins/markdownArtifacts/wikiLink.ts` (Area 1).

### Heading picker (bookmark links)

### Plugin host seams
- id: plugin-host-seams
- feature: Inline popups
- summary: Not user-facing: narrow interfaces through which plugins read settings, document, shortcuts, search, view toggles and live views, and ask the host for chrome, without importing the app's stores.
- capabilities: `hostSettings` (tab size, HTML allowlist, CJK formatting, paste mode, hard-break style); `hostDocument` (window label, active file path, content, dirty flag, format id); `hostPopups` (media popup, heading picker, editor context menu, image path, universal-toolbar dismiss — no-op defaults); `hostShortcuts` (`getShortcut` + `onChange`, default unbound); `hostSearch` (find-bar query/flags/index/replace text, match reporting; default closed bar); `hostViewModes` (`focusMode`, `typewriterMode`, `diagramPreview` + `onChange`, default off); `hostEditors` (live Source/WYSIWYG views and Source cursor context); `popupPorts` (state contracts for popups with Source twins); `pasteSettings` (paste vocabulary owned plugin-side)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; bound once by `bindPluginHostSettings()` from `src/bootstrap.tsx`
- surfaces: none
- code: `src/plugins/shared/{hostSettings,hostDocument,hostPopups,hostShortcuts,hostSearch,hostViewModes,hostEditors,popupPorts,pasteSettings}.ts`; `src/services/assembly/bindHostSettings.ts`; `src/services/assembly/hostAdapters.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: none (`.claude/adr/ADR-015-extension-model.md`; `.claude/rules/00-engineering-principles.md`)
- tests: `src/plugins/shared/{hostSettings,hostDocument,hostPopups,hostShortcuts,hostSearch,hostViewModes,hostEditors,pasteSettings}.test.ts`; `src/services/assembly/bindHostSettings.test.ts`
- notes: `hostEditors.ts` names `toolbarActions/dispatch.ts` as "the only consumer", but `src/plugins/codemirror/sourceLint.ts` and `src/plugins/codemirror/sourceCursorContext.ts` also consume it. `plugins/shared/` also holds non-seam helpers owned here by the spine (`historyBatch.ts`, `blockSpan.ts`, `fenceScanner.ts`, `mediaSecurity.ts`, …) whose features are elsewhere.

### Paste pipeline (smart / markdown / HTML / code / plain)
- id: paste-pipeline
- feature: Paste & copy pipeline
- summary: Pasting turns clipboard content into the right markdown structures — HTML from browsers and Word becomes clean markdown, markdown-looking text becomes rich nodes, code-looking text becomes a code block, and a URL pasted over a selection becomes a link.
- capabilities: smartPaste — URL over a non-empty selection wraps it in a link (skips code and already-linked ranges); markdownPaste — plain text detected as markdown is parsed into nodes, Tauri-clipboard fallback, 200 000-char and 5 000-node caps; htmlPaste — substantial HTML → `htmlToMarkdown`, 100 KB cap with plain-text fallback, and in `plain` mode inserts `text/plain`; codePaste — scored code detection wraps the paste in a code block with a language guess (`smart` mode only); Paste as Plain Text (`Mod+Shift+V`); all skip code blocks and multi-cursor selections
- status: shipped-on, windows-linux-gap
- gate: `markdown.pasteMode = "smart"` (`"smart" | "plain" | "rich"`; `rich` hands HTML to Tiptap untouched); `markdown.pasteMarkdownInWysiwyg = "auto"` (`"auto" | "off"`); `markdown.preserveLineBreaks` affects the parse
- surfaces: `Mod+V` (automatic); shortcut id `pastePlainText` `Mod-Shift-v`; editor context menu Paste; Settings → Markdown → Paste & Input
- code: `src/plugins/smartPaste/tiptap.ts`; `src/plugins/markdownPaste/tiptap.ts`; `src/plugins/htmlPaste/tiptap.ts`; `src/plugins/codePaste/tiptap.ts`; `src/utils/htmlToMarkdown.ts`; `src/utils/markdownPasteDetection.ts`; `src/utils/cleanPastedMarkdown.ts`; `src/utils/codeDetection/index.ts`; `src/utils/pasteUtils.ts`; `src/plugins/shared/pasteSettings.ts`; `src/services/assembly/pasteOptions.ts`; `src/services/editor/clipboardBridge.ts`; `src/pages/settings/MarkdownSettings.tsx`
- rust: `trigger_webview_edit` (`src-tauri/src/webview_edit.rs`) — macOS context-menu Paste goes down the native responder chain
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Editor Modes" ("Smart paste"); `website/guide/settings.md` §"Paste & Input"; `website/guide/shortcuts.md` (Paste Plain Text)
- tests: `src/plugins/{smartPaste,markdownPaste,htmlPaste,codePaste}/tiptap.test.ts`; `src/utils/htmlToMarkdown.test.ts`; `src/utils/markdownPasteDetection.test.ts`; `src/utils/cleanPastedMarkdown.test.ts`; `src/utils/codeDetection.test.ts`; `src/utils/pasteUtils.test.ts`; `src/services/assembly/pasteOptions.test.ts`; `src/plugins/shared/pasteSettings.test.ts`
- notes: Chain priority is smartPaste → markdownPaste → htmlPaste → codePaste, pinned by `WYSIWYG_COMPOSITION_ORDER`. `triggerPastePlainText` returns early on a multi-range selection, so Paste as Plain Text with several cursors does nothing. Outside macOS the context-menu Paste falls back to a plain-text clipboard read (`clipboardBridge.ts`), losing HTML/image fidelity; keyboard `Mod+V` is unaffected. `htmlPaste/tiptap.ts`'s header says it is active only in `smart` mode, but it also owns the `plain`-mode insert. Source-mode smart paste lives in `src/plugins/codemirror/smartPaste.ts` (Area 3).

### Copy format and copy-on-select
- id: markdown-copy
- feature: Paste & copy pipeline
- summary: Copy can put markdown syntax in the clipboard's plain-text slot instead of flattened text, and can auto-copy whatever you select.
- capabilities: `clipboardTextSerializer` override serializing the selection through `serializeMarkdown`; copy-on-select on mouseup (terminal style)
- status: shipped-off

- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Copy Format"; `website/guide/settings.md` §"Behavior"
- tests: `src/plugins/markdownCopy/tiptap.test.ts`
- notes: `terminal.copyOnSelect` is a separate terminal setting (Area 7) — do not conflate.

### Text drag-and-drop within the editor
- id: text-drag-drop
- feature: Paste & copy pipeline
- summary: Drag a text selection with the mouse to move it elsewhere in the document, with a visible drop cursor.
- capabilities: mousedown inside the selection with a 5 px threshold; live drop-cursor line; one undoable transaction; position tracking via `tr.mapping`; cancel on Escape or window blur
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: mouse drag
- code: `src/plugins/textDragDrop/tiptap.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/popups.md` §"Moving Text by Dragging"
- tests: `src/plugins/textDragDrop/tiptap.test.ts`
- notes: A mouse-event re-implementation, not HTML5 drag-and-drop, because Tauri's native drag-drop handler intercepts browser drag events. No platform `cfg`; identical behaviour on Windows/Linux is unverified.

### Auto-pair brackets and quotes
- id: auto-pair
- feature: Auto-pair & tab escape
- summary: Typing an opening bracket or quote inserts the closer; typing the closer skips over it; Backspace on an empty pair deletes both.
- capabilities: ASCII `() [] {} "" '' `` ``; CJK brackets `（）【】「」『』《》〈〉`; CJK curly quotes `“” ‘’`; skip-over; empty-pair Backspace; Shift+Tab jump back before the opener; backtick state machine (1st = code mark on, 2nd = off, 3rd = code block); disabled inside code blocks and inline code; IME-guarded
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `markdown.autoPairEnabled = true`; `markdown.autoPairCJKStyle = "auto"` (`"off" | "auto"`); `markdown.autoPairCurlyQuotes = true`; `markdown.autoPairRightDoubleQuote = false`
- surfaces: automatic (typing); Settings → Editor
- code: `src/plugins/autoPair/tiptap.ts`; `src/plugins/autoPair/handlers.ts`; `src/plugins/autoPair/keyHandler.ts`; `src/plugins/autoPair/backtickToggle.ts`; `src/plugins/autoPair/pairs.ts`; `src/plugins/autoPair/utils.ts`; `src/services/assembly/autoPairConfig.ts`; `src/pages/settings/EditorSettings.tsx`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Auto-Pair & Tab Escape"; `website/guide/tab-navigation.md` §"Bracket & Quote Escape", §"Settings"
- tests: `src/plugins/autoPair/{autoPair,handlers,pairs,utils}.test.ts`; `src/plugins/autoPair/__tests__/{tiptap,backtickCodeToggle,handlersExtra,shiftTabJump}.test.ts`; `src/services/assembly/autoPairConfig.test.ts`
- notes: `normalizeRightDoubleQuote` takes effect only when CJK pairs and curly quotes are both on. `pairs.ts` says curly quotes are "opt-in" and "Disabled by default" because they conflict with Chinese IME smart quotes, yet `autoPairCurlyQuotes` ships `true`. Uses `handleDOMEvents.keydown` so it runs before Tiptap's list-indent shortcut. Source-mode auto-pair is `src/plugins/codemirror/markdownAutoPair.ts` (Area 3).

### Smart Tab navigation and Tab indent
- id: tab-indent
- feature: Auto-pair & tab escape
- summary: Tab and Shift+Tab stay in the editor and do the contextually right thing — escape a mark or link, move between table cells, indent or outdent list items, or insert spaces.
- capabilities: escape from bold/italic/code/strike/link forward (Tab) and backward (Shift+Tab); next/previous table cell with a new row at the last cell; list sink/lift; space-insertion fallback and Shift+Tab outdent of leading spaces by `general.tabSize`; per-cursor escape under multi-cursor
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; `general.tabSize` supplies the fallback width
- surfaces: automatic (Tab / Shift+Tab)
- code: `src/plugins/tabIndent/tiptap.ts`; `src/plugins/tabIndent/tabEscape.ts`; `src/plugins/tabIndent/shiftTabEscape.ts`; `src/services/assembly/tiptapExtensions.ts`

- tests: `src/plugins/tabIndent/{tabEscape,tabEscape.edge-cases,tabEscape.integration,tabEscape.multi-cursor,tabIndent,tiptap}.test.ts`; `src/plugins/tabIndent/__tests__/shiftTabEscape.test.ts`; `src/plugins/tabIndent/__tests__/tabEscapeCode.test.ts`
- notes: `ESCAPABLE_MARK_NAMES` is `bold, italic, code, strike, link`; highlight, underline, subscript and superscript are not escapable. Source mode has no Shift+Tab bracket or mark escape (`tab-navigation.md` §"Comparison: WYSIWYG vs Source Mode").

### Focus mode
- id: focus-mode

### Show invisibles
- id: show-invisibles

- surfaces: View menu id `show-invisibles`; shortcut id `showInvisibles` `F3`; Settings → Editor → Whitespace
- code: `src/plugins/showInvisibles/tiptap.ts`; `src/hooks/useTiptapSettingsSync.ts`; `src/services/commands/viewCommands.ts`; `src/pages/settings/WhitespaceSettings.tsx`; `src/plugins/codemirror/showInvisibles.ts`

### Universal Toolbar
- id: universal-toolbar
- feature: Universal toolbar
- summary: A single-line, keyboard-driven formatting toolbar in the bottom lane, identical in WYSIWYG and Source.
- capabilities: `Mod+Shift+B` shows and focuses it, pressed again returns focus to the editor; seven groups in pinned order Block → Inline → List → Table → Blockquote → Link → Insert, each a dropdown; Left/Right between groups, Enter/Space to open, arrows inside; two-step Escape; session memory of the last focused button; smart initial focus; per-item enabled/active state from the shared enable rules; AI Genies button opening the genie picker; displaces the StatusBar and closes the FindBar
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; visibility in `uiStore.universalToolbarVisible`
- surfaces: shortcut id `formatToolbar` `Mod-Shift-b`; View menu id `universal-toolbar`; toolbar buttons and dropdowns

- tests: `src/components/Editor/UniversalToolbar/{UniversalToolbar,GroupDropdown}.test.tsx`; `src/components/Editor/UniversalToolbar/{toolbarGroups,toolbarFocus,toolbarNavigation,useToolbarKeyboard,toolbarI18n}.test.ts`; `src/hooks/useUniversalToolbar.test.ts`; `src/services/editor/universalToolbarToggle.test.ts`
- notes: Keyboard and View-menu toggles share `universalToolbarToggle.ts` (#1204). Icons are branded `SafeIconSvg`, so only the validated icon registry reaches `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`. `#1063` marks the effects that react to external store state.

### Find and Replace (in-document)

Doc-change rebuilds debounce 200 ms, query changes 150 ms; replace re-scans before writing. The `FindBar.tsx` header still says the bar appears at the top of the editor; it is in the bottom lane. `src/plugins/search/__tests__/search.test.ts` mocks `@/stores/searchStore`, which no longer exists. "Find in Files" is Area 4.

- id: multi-cursor

## Area 3 — Source mode, split view, multi-format support

Verified: `bf8b69ceb`

Every document tab resolves to a format through `src/lib/formats/registry.ts`; `src/components/Editor/Editor.tsx` mounts the format's surface through `src/components/Editor/FormatSurface.tsx`. Markdown gets `src/lib/formats/adapters/markdownSurface.tsx` (WYSIWYG, Source or the markdown split); every other text format gets `src/components/Editor/SplitPaneEditor/SplitPaneEditor.tsx`. The markdown Source editor's extensions are composed once in `src/services/assembly/sourceEditorExtensions.ts` in the order `src/services/assembly/compositionOrder.ts` declares.

### Editor mode switching (WYSIWYG / Source / Markdown Split)
- id: editor-mode-switching
- feature: Editor modes (WYSIWYG/Source/Split)
- summary: For markdown documents the user picks one of three mutually exclusive surfaces — rich-text WYSIWYG, raw CodeMirror source, or source beside a live read-only WYSIWYG preview — and the choice is remembered per tab and mirrored in the native View menu.
- capabilities: F6 toggles WYSIWYG⇄Source; Shift+F6 toggles Markdown Split; "WYSIWYG Mode" radio item leaves whichever mode is on, including a large-file forced Source; per-tab mode restored on tab switch (`useTabModeSync`); every switch records a cross-mode undo checkpoint; native View-menu radio group with checkmarks and enable state; content flushed and popups closed before each swap; a forced-Source tab never enters the split; `.github/workflows/*.yml` paths refuse the WYSIWYG toggle with a toast; on split-pane formats F6 / Shift+F6 drive that tab's Source⇄Split / Preview⇄Split instead
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; `markdown.splitViewByDefault = false` seeds the live split once per window mount
