### Markdown Source mode (CodeMirror 6 editor)
- id: source-mode-codemirror
- feature: Editor modes (WYSIWYG/Source/Split)
- summary: The raw-markdown editing surface built on CodeMirror 6, with GFM-aware highlighting, its own keymap, cursor and scroll sync with WYSIWYG, and the markdown-aware plugin set in `src/plugins/codemirror/`.
- capabilities: markdown syntax highlighting (tables, tasks, strikethrough, sub/superscript); per-tab cursor and scroll restore on remount (#1249); echo-loop guard; multi-range selection reported to the status bar; read-only compartment; IME composition and scroll-jitter guards; live reconfiguration via compartments (wrap, line numbers, invisibles, `<br>` hiding, auto-pair, read-only, keymap); outline click → scroll and caret → active heading; lint gutter from the shared lint store; pending-navigation consumption (Find in Files, terminal links, lint jumps); Finder image drag-drop; hidden mode that skips store writes when both editors are kept alive; registers as the active source view only while its pane is focused and not hidden, and is forgotten by identity when focus moves to another pane; only the active source view publishes the cursor context
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on for markdown tabs; mounted only by `MarkdownEditorSurface`
- surfaces: automatic when mode = source or split; mode switching per `editor-mode-switching`
- code: `src/components/Editor/SourceEditor.tsx`; `src/services/assembly/sourceEditorExtensions.ts`; `src/services/assembly/sourceEditorKeymap.ts`; `src/services/assembly/compositionOrder.ts`; `src/plugins/codemirror/index.ts`; `src/lib/formats/markdownLanguageSupport.ts`; `src/hooks/useSourceEditorSync.ts`; `src/hooks/useSourceEditorContentSync.ts`; `src/hooks/useSourcePaneFocus.ts`; `src/components/Editor/sourceFocusRestore.ts`; `src/components/Editor/sourcePendingNav.ts`; `src/utils/cursorSync/**`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Source Mode", §"Reading Position"; `website/guide/large-files.md`
- tests: `src/components/Editor/SourceEditor.test.tsx`, `src/components/Editor/sourceFocusRestore.test.ts`, `src/components/Editor/sourcePendingNav.test.ts`, `src/services/assembly/sourceEditorExtensions.test.ts`, `src/services/assembly/extensionOrdering.test.ts`, `src/plugins/codemirror/imeGuard.test.ts`, `src/plugins/codemirror/imeScrollGuard.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useSourceEditorSync.test.ts` (both sync hooks), `src/hooks/useSourcePaneFocus.test.tsx`, `src/plugins/codemirror/sourceCursorContext.test.ts`

The YAML branches of `createSourceEditorExtensions` (`source.yamlLint`, `source.workflowPreview`, `source.workflowCompletion`, `source.workflowCursorSync`, `source.gotoExtension`) run only when a `.yml` / `.yaml` is associated with markdown through Set File Type — `SourceEditor` is mounted only by `markdownSurface.tsx`; ordinary YAML gets the yaml adapter's own extras and `vmark-workflow` preview in the split pane. `src/plugins/codemirror/sourceWorkflowPreview.ts` (area 12) now parses on open and says so in its header.

### Source-mode text editing helpers (lists, tables, pairs, tabs)
- id: source-editing-helpers
- feature: Editor modes (WYSIWYG/Source/Split)
- summary: Markdown-aware typing in Source mode — list continuation and smart indent, table Tab navigation and cell highlight, auto-pairing of markdown emphasis characters, and protection against deleting structural characters.

CJK) and walks `[text]`→`(url)`→out; fallback Tab inserts `tabSize` spaces; blank lines between list items hidden visually; `<br />` lines hidden when `markdown.showBrTags` is off; visual-line Up/Down and smart Home under word wrap

- surfaces: automatic (keymap); Settings → Editor (tab size, auto-pair), Settings → Editor → Whitespace (`<br>` tags)

### Source-mode decorations and inline previews
- id: source-decorations

### Source-mode cursor context, toolbar/menu actions and shortcuts
- id: source-context-actions

### Source-mode search (find/replace)
- id: source-search
- feature: Find & replace (in-document)
- summary: The app's find bar drives CodeMirror's search extension in markdown Source mode — live match counting, next/previous, replace and replace-all, with case, whole-word and regex options.
- capabilities: match counting with CodeMirror's own search cursor (the engine Next/Previous/Replace use, so `^`/`$`, Whole Word in regex mode and a typed `\n` count as they match); debounced recount on document change that keeps the current match by position (the WYSIWYG rule); find next / previous; replace next / all; case-sensitive, whole-word and regex flags; open-find-bar / find-next / find-previous bound as non-mutating handlers
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; `search()` included with no CodeMirror panel and no `searchKeymap`
- surfaces: FindBar (Mod+F)
- code: `src/hooks/useSourceEditorSearch.ts`; `src/utils/sourceEditorSearch.ts`; `src/services/search/sourceSearchCounter.ts`; `src/plugins/codemirror/sourceShortcutsHelpers.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Search & Replace"
- tests: `src/hooks/useSourceEditorSearch.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useSourceEditorSearch.replaceResume.test.ts`, `src/utils/sourceEditorSearch.test.ts`, `src/services/search/sourceSearchCounter.test.ts`, `src/components/Editor/SourceEditor.search.test.tsx`, `e2e/journeys/07-find-bar.mjs`

### Split-pane editor for non-markdown formats (Source / Split / Preview)
- id: split-pane-formats
- feature: Editor modes (WYSIWYG/Source/Split)
- summary: Every non-markdown text format opens in a two-pane surface — an editable CodeMirror source pane beside a format-specific preview — with a Source · Split · Preview toggle remembered per tab.
- capabilities: per-tab view mode seeded from a global default; resizable divider clamped to [0.2, 0.8] (ArrowLeft/Right, Home, End); lazily loaded language pack in a compartment; lazily loaded per-format extra extensions; validator-backed lint gutter plus a clickable `ValidationGutter` that jumps to line:column; read-only banner with "Enable editing" and "Open in external editor"; store↔view sync that never pollutes undo; deferred preview rendering with a separate live content for actions; reduced context menu (clipboard + Select All); while its pane is focused the source pane registers as the window's active source view under its own tab and drops any markdown cursor context another pane published, so readers such as the workflow diagnostics banner's line jump, `JobNode` Escape and the IME guard find it and the toolbar cannot format the other pane's document; the registration is released by identity when split focus moves to another pane, and on unmount only if still its own
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `formats.defaultViewMode = "split"`; per-tab `viewMode` and `editingEnabled`
- surfaces: in-surface `ViewModeToggle`; F6 / Shift+F6 via `applySplitPaneViewShortcut`; divider; read-only banner buttons; Settings → Formats → Default view mode
- code: `src/components/Editor/SplitPaneEditor/{SplitPaneEditor,SourcePane,ViewModeToggle,ReadOnlyBanner,ValidationGutter}.tsx`; `src/components/Editor/SplitPaneEditor/{sourcePaneExtensions,usePreviewModel}.ts`; `src/services/keybinding/splitPaneViewShortcut.ts`; `src/hooks/useSourcePaneFocus.ts` (`bindSplitSourceView`); `src/lib/formats/sourceLanguage.ts`; `src/services/assembly/sourceLanguageBinding.ts`
- rust: `external_editor::open_in_external_editor`
- docs: `website/guide/formats.md` §"View modes (Source / Split / Preview)", §"Editing rules"; `website/guide/settings.md` §"Default view mode"
- tests: `src/components/Editor/SplitPaneEditor/{SplitPaneEditor,SourcePane,ViewModeToggle,ReadOnlyBanner,ValidationGutter,ValidationGutter.i18n}.test.tsx`, `src/components/Editor/SplitPaneEditor/sourcePaneExtensions.test.ts`, `src/components/Editor/SplitPaneEditor/usePreviewModel.test.tsx`, `src/services/keybinding/splitPaneViewShortcut.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useSourcePaneFocus.test.tsx`, `src/lib/formats/sourceLanguage.test.ts`, `e2e/journeys/12-nonmd-format-dispatch.mjs`, `e2e/journeys/13-workflow-split-pane.mjs`
- notes: The split fraction is component state — not persisted across remounts or restarts. `SourcePane` is deliberately thinner than the markdown `SourceEditor`: no focus/typewriter, no invisibles, no smart paste, no auto-pair, no markdown decorations, no popups, no cross-mode undo (CodeMirror `history()` + `historyKeymap` + `searchKeymap`).

### Multi-format registry and file-type dispatch
- id: format-registry
- feature: Multi-format support
- summary: A single registry decides which editor a file gets. Markdown is an allowlist, not a default — an unrecognized pathed file always opens as plain text.
- capabilities: registration invariants checked before commit (id pattern, duplicate id, extension must be a real lookup key, collisions, wysiwyg needs a surface and no `loadLanguage`, import thunks callable, read-only-default requires `prompt-on-close`); dispatch order untitled → markdown, user association (most-specific key first), built-in extension map, plain-text fallback; missing markdown or txt fails loudly; lookup keys full filename → dotfile stem → bare extension; atomic registry rebuild when a category toggle flips, with every tab's format recomputed and the surface remounted; localized save-dialog filters; lazy surface and language thunks; Rust↔TS extension-list parity
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on (registry); per-category toggles gate individual adapters
- surfaces: automatic (file open); Settings → Formats; palette `format.setPlainText` / `format.setMarkdown` / `format.resetType`
- code: `src/lib/formats/{registry,registryBootstrap,formatValidation,formatPathKeys,types,lazySurfaces,saveFilters,index}.ts`; `src/components/Editor/Editor.tsx`; `src/components/Editor/FormatSurface.tsx`; `src/services/formats/formatSettingsBridge.ts`; `src/hooks/useFormatSettingsBridge.ts`
- rust: `src-tauri/src/supported_files.rs` (`SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS`, `has_supported_extension`, `is_openable_supported`) — gates CLI args, Finder opens, `validate_openable_path`, macOS quarantine strip
- docs: `website/guide/formats.md` §"Enabling formats", §"How VMark decides a file's type", §"Override: \"Set File Type\""; `website/guide/settings.md` §"Format support"
- tests: `src/lib/formats/{registry,registryBootstrap,formatValidation,formatPathKeys,dispatch,index,lazySurfaces,saveFilters,extSync}.test.ts`, `src/components/Editor/Editor.test.tsx`, `src/components/Editor/FormatSurface.test.tsx`, `src/services/formats/formatSettingsBridge.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/supported_files.test.rs`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/__tests__/parserRobustness.test.ts`
- notes: `supported_files.rs` keeps a `cfg_attr(target_os = "macos", allow(dead_code))` on the CLI-args filter — Finder dispatches through `RunEvent::Opened` there. `useFormatSettingsBridge` has no dedicated test (the service it wraps does).

### Format adapters (what each file type gets)
- id: format-adapters
- feature: Multi-format support
- summary: Registered adapters cover markdown, plain text, YAML, JSON, TOML, Mermaid, SVG, HTML, nine code-viewer languages over twelve extensions, and binary media — each with its own language pack, validator, preview and menu policy.
- capabilities: markdown (`.md .markdown .mdown .mkd .mdx`, WYSIWYG); txt (`.txt`, source only); yaml (`.yaml .yml`, tree preview + GitHub Actions schema renderer + workflow source extras, plus a `vmark-workflow` schema for engine workflows — YAML that parses, with a top-level `steps:` list in which a step's own `uses:` names `genie/` / `action/` / `webhook/`, and no `jobs:` — that shows the engine's Run/Cancel panel while `advanced.workflowEngine` is on and the plain tree otherwise, switching live with the setting, except that a tab whose run is still live keeps the panel and its Cancel after the setting goes off or the file stops parsing); json (`.json .jsonl`, tree + `package.json` renderer); toml (`.toml`, tree + `Cargo.toml` / `pyproject.toml` renderers); mermaid (`.mmd`, live diagram); svg (sanitized inline render); html (`.html .htm`, empty-sandbox iframe + DOMPurify + CSP, plus opt-in per-file trusted preview on an isolated origin); code viewers (`ts tsx js jsx py rs go css sh bash rb lua`, read-only by default); media (images/video/audio except `.svg`, never read as text); syntax colouring for plain files by filename regardless of the code-viewer toggle
- status: shipped-on, shipped-off
- gate: `formats.dataFormats = false`; `formats.diagrams = false`; `formats.htmlPreview = false`; `formats.codeViewers = false`; markdown, txt, yaml and media always registered; the yaml `vmark-workflow` Run panel needs `advanced.workflowEngine = false` turned on
- surfaces: Settings → Formats toggles; automatic on open; read-only banner for viewers; `HtmlTrustBar` for trusted HTML
- code: `src/lib/formats/adapters/{markdown,json,yaml,toml,mermaid,svg,html,HtmlPreview,HtmlTrustBar,DepList,cargoToml,packageJson,pyprojectToml,yamlWorkflowRenderer,yamlEngineRenderer}.tsx`; `src/lib/workflow/detection.ts` (engine-workflow shape, area 12); `src/lib/formats/adapters/{txt,code,media,htmlTrust,useHtmlTrust,jsonViewStyles,yamlWorkflowExtensions}.ts`; `src/components/Editor/MediaViewer/MediaViewer.tsx`
- rust: `src-tauri/src/trusted_html/protocol.rs` (trusted HTML scheme); asset protocol for media
- docs: `website/guide/formats.md` §"At a glance", §"Schema-aware previews" (incl. "VMark workflow"), §"Security model for HTML", §"Trusted HTML preview (opt-in)"; `website/guide/media-support.md`; `website/guide/workflow-viewer.md`; `website/guide/workflows.md` (engine-workflow files); `website/guide/settings.md` §"Format support"
- tests: `src/lib/formats/adapters/*.test.ts`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/*.test.tsx` (incl. `src/lib/formats/adapters/yamlEngineRenderer.test.tsx`), `src/lib/formats/adapters/__tests__/darkThemeStyles.test.tsx`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/__tests__/parserRobustness.webkit.test.ts`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/__tests__/trustedHtmlIsolation.webkit.test.ts`
- notes: markdown/txt/yaml/media are on; data, diagram, HTML and code-viewer adapters ship off. `.zig` is deliberately out of scope (no maintained language pack). Workflow authoring aids load as individually degradable chunks (`yamlWorkflowExtensions.ts`). HTML preview's OWASP sign-off (WI-3.4) is pending, and the adapter header, the settings description, the in-preview notice (`preview.signOffPending`) and `formats.md` §"Security model for HTML" all say so. The engine renderer's chunk is not fetched while `advanced.workflowEngine` is off, except to keep a live run's Cancel reachable in the tab that owns it. `formats.md` §"Schema-aware previews" does not list the engine-workflow schema. Trusted HTML preview is disabled on Windows (area 15 platform notes).

### Open in external editor
- id: external-editor
- feature: Multi-format support
- summary: A read-only code-viewer tab offers "Open in external editor", which launches the user's configured editor (or the env-var chain) on the file, detached.
- capabilities: GUI override with a Browse file picker; fallback chain `$VMARK_EXTERNAL_EDITOR → $VISUAL → $EDITOR → platform default`; macOS `.app` bundle rewrite to `open -a`; login-shell PATH; checks and spawn run on a blocking task and the child is not awaited; failures toasted
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `formats.externalEditor = ""` (empty = env chain); button shown only when `kind === "viewer"` and `readOnlyDefault` and editing is not enabled
- surfaces: `ReadOnlyBanner` button; Settings → Formats → External editor (text field + Browse)
- code: `src/components/Editor/SplitPaneEditor/SplitPaneEditor.tsx`; `src/components/Editor/SplitPaneEditor/ReadOnlyBanner.tsx`; `src/pages/settings/FormatsSettings.tsx`
- rust: `external_editor::open_in_external_editor` (async, `spawn_blocking`, legacy `Result<(), String>`)
- docs: `website/guide/formats.md` §"Open in external editor", §"Security gate"; `website/guide/settings.md` §"External editor"
- tests: `src-tauri/src/external_editor.test.rs`, `src/components/Editor/SplitPaneEditor/ReadOnlyBanner.test.tsx`, `src/components/Editor/SplitPaneEditor/SplitPaneEditor.test.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/FormatsSettings.test.tsx`
- notes: Platform defaults — macOS `open -t`, Windows `notepad.exe`, else `xdg-open`; `maybe_open_app_bundle` is macOS-only. Known limitation (`external_editor.rs` header): env-var editor commands split on whitespace with no quoting, so an executable path containing a space breaks; the GUI override (single token) is the workaround. Security gate: the override (`src-tauri/src/external_editor/override_guard.rs`) must be one token with no shell metacharacters and no leading `-`, and either a bare name in `KNOWN_EDITORS` or an existing absolute path without `..` whose spelled and resolved names are not in `RUNS_ITS_ARGUMENT` (shells, interpreters, launchers, terminal emulators — `src-tauri/src/external_editor/program_names.rs`); the env-var chain is not restricted. The file path is canonicalized and checked against `is_openable_supported`. Both rules are in `website/guide/formats.md` §"Security gate".

### Split documents (two documents side by side)
- id: split-documents-panes
- feature: Side-by-side documents (panes)
- summary: A window can show two different documents side by side, each a full editor, with a draggable divider, focus tracking, optional synchronized scrolling and per-workspace layout restore.

- gate: always on (split disabled per window by default)

### Large-file handling
- id: large-files
- feature: Multi-format support
- summary: VMark sizes a file before reading it and routes accordingly — normal open under 1 MB, automatic Source mode from 1 MB, a confirmation dialog from 5 MB, and a hard refusal at 50 MB.
- capabilities: four size tiers from a metadata stat with no content read; one pre-read gate used by every open path; native warn and refusal dialogs; per-tab forced-Source marker with a reason, overriding the window mode; status-bar "Opened in Source mode" with a "Switch to WYSIWYG" link that clears the marker and switches; indeterminate open spinner with a size label, CSS-delayed so fast opens never flash it; forced-Source tabs never enter the markdown split
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `largeFile.autoSourceMode = true`; `largeFile.warnAbove5MB = true`; the 50 MB refusal is fixed
- surfaces: automatic on open; native dialogs; status bar; Settings → Editor → Large files group
- code: `src/utils/fileSizeThresholds.ts`; `src/services/navigation/largeFileRouting.ts`; `src/services/dialogs/largeFilePrompts.ts`; `src/stores/documentStore/largeFileSession.ts`; `src/stores/documentStore/fileLoad.ts`; `src/components/StatusBar/SourceModeUpgrade.tsx`; `src/components/StatusBar/FileLoadIndicator.tsx`; `src/lib/formats/markdownLargeFile.ts`; `src/pages/settings/EditorSettings.tsx`
- rust: `files::ops::get_file_size_bytes` (`src-tauri/src/files/ops.rs`)
- docs: `website/guide/large-files.md`; `website/guide/features.md` §"Large Files"; `website/guide/settings.md` §"Editor" → §"Large Files"
- tests: `src/utils/fileSizeThresholds.test.ts`, `src/services/navigation/largeFileRouting.test.ts`, `src/services/dialogs/largeFilePrompts.test.ts`, `src/stores/largeFileSessionStore.test.ts`, `src/stores/fileLoadStore.test.ts`, `src/lib/formats/markdownLargeFile.test.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/SourceModeUpgrade.test.tsx`, `src/components/StatusBar/FileLoadIndicator.test.tsx`; dev-only bench `src/bench/largeFile.bench.ts` (skipped unless `VMARK_BENCH_LARGE_FILE` is set)
- notes: Known limitations (`large-files.md`): thresholds are bytes, a proxy for block count; WYSIWYG has no deferred or incremental rendering. A size-check error resolves as "small" so the normal read path reports once.

### Markdown pipeline (parse and serialize)
- id: markdown-pipeline
- feature: Markdown pipeline (parse/serialize)
- summary: Not user-facing: the remark/MDAST pipeline every markdown open, save, paste and export runs through — markdown → MDAST → ProseMirror and back — with VMark's dialect extensions and linear-time guards against hostile input.
- capabilities: `parseMarkdown` / `serializeMarkdown` entry points with null-safe input and error wrapping; four parse dialects (`document`, `source-position`, `details-body`, `inline-summary`) built from one plugin table; GFM, math, frontmatter, wiki links, `<details>`, `[TOC]`, sub/superscript/highlight marks and reference resolution as remark plugins; setext headings disabled; pre-parse container-nesting refusal above `MAX_NESTING_DEPTH` = 1000; inline fast paths that settle hostile `]`, backtick and emphasis runs in linear time; list-item preparation linear in document size (#1473); serializer options for soft-break preservation, hard-break style and blank-line preservation; attention/escape serialization that keeps marks beside CJK punctuation and whole code points
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; serializer behaviour follows `markdown.preserveLineBreaks`, `markdown.hardBreakStyleOnSave`, `markdown.preserveBlankLines`
- surfaces: automatic
- code: `src/utils/markdownPipeline/{index,adapter,parser,serializer,dialect,dialectDescriptors,dialectQueries,nestingDepth,mdastToProseMirror,proseMirrorToMdast}.ts`; `src/utils/markdownPipeline/parser/fastPaths/**`; `src/utils/markdownPipeline/plugins/**`; `patches/micromark@4.0.2.patch`; `patches/micromark-extension-gfm-autolink-literal@2.1.0.patch`; `patches/mdast-util-from-markdown@2.0.3.patch`; `patches/mdast-util-find-and-replace@3.0.2.patch`; `patches/mdast-util-to-markdown@2.1.2.patch`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/formats.md` §"Markdown dialect" (dialect and the 1000-level nesting limit); `website/guide/large-files.md` (nesting limit)
- tests: `src/utils/markdownPipeline/__tests__/**` (conformance, characterization corpus, fidelity, spec, round-trip property tests, pathological scaling), `src/utils/markdownPipeline/parser/fastPaths/inlineFastPaths.test.ts`, `src/utils/markdownPipeline/parser/*.test.ts`
- notes: Five `pnpm` patches cover costs no extension API reaches; a Dependabot bump of `micromark`, `micromark-extension-gfm-autolink-literal`, `mdast-util-from-markdown`, `mdast-util-find-and-replace` or `mdast-util-to-markdown` fails install until its patch is re-made. The `mdast-util-from-markdown` patch has a recorded unpatched reference (`listPreparation.differential.test.ts`); upstream's own list fix (syntax-tree/mdast-util-from-markdown#51) batches per list and does not replace it. The `mdast-util-find-and-replace` patch has one too (`findAndReplace.differential.test.ts`). `nested-strong-emph` and `nested-brackets` remain super-linear (upstream algorithm), and so does one paragraph of tens of thousands of inline siblings: `remarkResolveReferences` walks every node with `unist-util-visit`, whose index lookup is an `indexOf` over the siblings (linear on realistic documents, so not patched). The spine names `website/guide/formats.md` as this feature's page, but that page says nothing about the pipeline.

## Area 4 — Workspace, sidebar, file management

Verified: `bf8b69ceb`

A window's workspace lives in `src/stores/workspaceStore.ts` (window-scoped, rehydrated through `src/stores/workspaceStorePersist.ts`) with its on-disk config written by `src-tauri/src/workspace/mod.rs`. Which folders get a recursive fs + asset grant is decided in Rust by `src-tauri/src/workspace/grants/mod.rs`; the webview asks through `src/services/workspaces/workspaceAccess.ts`. When `general.workspaceRailMode` is on, `src/stores/workspaceInstancesStore.ts` holds several workspaces per window and `src/services/workspaces/switchWorkspaceInstance.ts` performs the context switch. The sidebar (`src/components/Sidebar/Sidebar.tsx`) follows the active tab's kind. Opening files from Finder, the CLI, drag-drop and links is block `file-open-routing` in area 15.

### Workspace (folder as project root)
- id: workspace-folder
- feature: Workspace management
- summary: Open a folder as the window's workspace root; it drives the file explorer, Quick Open, content search, terminal cwd and per-workspace session restore.
- capabilities: Open Workspace through the folder picker Rust shows (`pick_workspace_folder`, can create folders, one dialog at a time — a second is refused with `conflict` and reported, not read as a cancel), which grants the pick recursively (fs + asset scopes) and records it in `<appData>/workspace-grants.json` only once the grant has taken (a grant that does not take fails the pick); recorded roots re-granted at launch (bounded wait, resolved concurrently so one stale mount holds back no other root, a root that now resolves elsewhere is skipped); Rust re-issues a grant only for a recorded root or a folder inside one and refuses any other webview-supplied path; `openWorkspaceByPath` grants nothing itself, so each opener settles access first (the picker grants its pick, Open Recent and the MCP open ask Rust, a window's startup workspace and a hot-exit restore wait for their re-grant); Open Recent and the MCP open send a folder that is neither recorded nor readable by the static scope to the picker opened at it; Close Workspace persists the session first, and with the rail on runs the rail's own instance close (dirty-checked tab close, successor promoted); per-window workspace state; per-workspace config on disk (excludeFolders, showHiddenFiles, showAllFiles, lastOpenTabs, sessionTabs, ai, identity/trust); one-shot legacy `.vmark/` → appData migration; 8-byte → 16-byte hash filename migration; rehydrated config normalized on merge; optimistic config writes rolled back on a failed write; cross-window config rehydrate; one reentry guard shared by open, open-recent and close
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: menu `open-folder` (Mod+Shift+O, shortcut id `openFolder`), `close-workspace`; palette `workspace.openFolder`, `workspace.close`; Welcome screen "Open Workspace…"; Finder folder open (area 15 `file-open-routing`); MCP `vmark.workspace.open_workspace` (approval-gated, see `workspace-approval`)
- code: `src/services/commands/workspaceCommands.ts`; `src/services/workspaces/{openWorkspaceByPath,openWorkspaceWithConfig,workspaceAccess,workspaceConfig,workspaceConfigGuard,workspaceSession}.ts`; `src/stores/{workspaceStore,workspaceStorePersist,workspaceConfigDefaults,workspaceStoreHelpers}.ts`; `src/hooks/useWorkspaceBootstrap.ts`; `src/hooks/useWorkspaceSync.ts`; `src/utils/workspaceIdentity.ts`
- rust: `workspace::read_workspace_config`, `workspace::write_workspace_config`, `workspace::validation::validate_workspace_dir`, `quarantine::strip_workspace_quarantine_cmd`; `workspace::grants::commands::allow_workspace_access` (`src-tauri/src/workspace/grants/commands.rs`), `workspace::grants::picker::pick_workspace_folder` and `request_workspace_confirmation` (`src-tauri/src/workspace/grants/picker.rs`), `workspace::grants::restore_at_launch` (`src-tauri/src/workspace/grants/launch.rs`) and `grant_chosen_root` (`src-tauri/src/workspace/grants/mod.rs`), list file and write fence in `src-tauri/src/workspace/grants/{registry,protect}.rs`; the recursive grant in `workspace::grants::scope::grant_workspace_scope` (`src-tauri/src/workspace/grants/scope.rs`)
- docs: `website/guide/workspace-management.md` §"Opening a Workspace", §"Workspace Settings", §"Session Restore"; `website/guide/features.md` §"Workspace Support"; `website/guide/privacy.md` §"What VMark can read on disk"; `website/guide/troubleshooting.md` §"Folder Access and `forbidden path` Errors"
- tests: `src/services/commands/workspaceCommands.test.ts`, `src/services/workspaces/{openWorkspaceByPath,openWorkspaceWithConfig,workspaceAccess,workspaceConfig,workspaceSession}.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/workspace/grants/{commands,commands_policy,launch,mod,picker,protect,registry,scope}.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/capabilities.test.rs` (pins every capability's fs scope and the asset scope), `src/stores/{workspaceStore,workspaceStore.blankRoot,workspaceStoreConfig,workspaceStorePersist,workspaceConfigDefaults,workspaceStoreHelpers}.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useWorkspaceBootstrap.test.tsx`, `src/hooks/useWorkspaceSync.test.ts`, `src/utils/workspaceIdentity.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/workspace/mod.test.rs`, inline `mod tests` in `src-tauri/src/workspace/validation.rs`; e2e helper `e2e/lib/workspace.mjs`
- notes: Config is written to `<appData>/workspaces/<sha256-16>.json`, never into the project folder, and also cached per window in localStorage. Known limitation (`workspace/mod.rs` header): hash collisions are possible in theory. An app-minted config with `excludeFolders: []` is repaired to the defaults (`.git`, `node_modules`) (#1187). Access is settled before any read so workspaces outside the static scope (`$HOME/**`, `/Volumes/**`, `/mnt/**`, `/media/**`, `C:\`–`F:\` on Windows) work (#1252); a refusal for a folder the static scope covers is the ordinary answer and does not abort the open. A runtime grant is not read-only — it satisfies every fs command the capability permits, write and remove included. The grant list is fenced from webview writes (fs plugin, `atomic_write_file`, `create_file_exclusive`, store-plugin format) and `run_workflow` refuses a workspace root that contains it, but per the `workspace/grants/mod.rs` header not from any process running as the user; an edit takes effect at next launch. The `workspaceStore.ts` header still says config is "stored in localStorage … not on disk".

### Workspace rail — multiple workspaces per window
- id: workspace-rail
- feature: Workspace rail (multi-workspace)
- summary: Experimental vertical strip on the window's left edge holding several workspaces at once; clicking a glyph performs a full context switch (tabs, sidebar tree, split panes, outline state, closed-tab history, terminal sessions).
- capabilities: activate/switch instance (stash outgoing, restore incoming, generation-bumped); drag-reorder; drag a glyph outside the window → move to a new window; hover ⧉ → duplicate to a new window; right-click menu Close / Duplicate / Move to New Window, clamped to the viewport on resize and relayout; synthetic "Loose Files" instance for files outside every root; per-instance glyph colour derived from the workspace name; display-name disambiguation; per-instance sidebar width and view, folder-open and scroll state, outline collapse/filter/scroll, pane layout, terminal sessions; per-instance closed-tab history behind File → Reopen Closed Tab
- status: shipped-off, partial
- gate: `general.workspaceRailMode = false` (migrated forward from legacy `advanced.workspaceRailMode`)
- surfaces: Settings → Files & Images → Workspace → Workspace rail; rail buttons (`data-rail-action="activate"|"duplicate"`); rail context menu; drag-drop; menu `reopen-closed-tab` (shortcut id `reopenClosedTab`, no default key)
- code: `src/components/WorkspaceRail/{WorkspaceRail,WorkspaceRailContextMenu}.tsx`; `src/components/WorkspaceRail/{workspaceRailHandlers,workspaceRailMenuLayout}.ts`; `src/services/featureFlags/workspaceRailFeatureFlag.ts`; `src/utils/workspaceRailGlyphs.ts`; `src/services/workspaces/{switchWorkspaceInstance,workspaceOwnershipKernel,closeWorkspaceInstance,finalizeInstanceRemoval,workspaceWindowActions,instanceOperationLock,reopenClosedTab,restoreInstanceContext,hydrateWorkspaceInstanceContext}.ts`; `src/stores/workspaceInstancesStore.ts`; `src/stores/workspaceInstanceUiStore.ts`; `src/hooks/useWorkspaceRailSeed.ts`; `src/pages/settings/WorkspaceSettingsGroup.tsx`
- rust: `workspace::transfer::detach_workspace_to_new_window`, `claim_workspace_transfer`, `ack_workspace_transfer`, `cancel_workspace_transfer` (`src-tauri/src/workspace/transfer.rs`)
- docs: `website/guide/workspace-rail.md` (whole page, marked Experimental); `website/guide/settings.md` §"Workspace"
- tests: `src/components/WorkspaceRail/{WorkspaceRail,WorkspaceRailContextMenu}.test.tsx`, `src/components/WorkspaceRail/{workspaceRailHandlers,workspaceRailMenuLayout}.test.ts`, `src/services/workspaces/{switchWorkspaceInstance,workspaceOwnershipKernel,closeWorkspaceInstance,closeWorkspaceInstance.pinned,finalizeInstanceRemoval,instanceOperationLock,reopenClosedTab,restoreContextOrdering,workspaceSwitchInterplay,workspaceWindowActions,workspaceWindowActions.claim,workspaceWindowActions.duplicate}.test.ts`, `src/stores/{workspaceInstancesStore,workspaceInstanceUiStore}.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useWorkspaceRailSeed.test.ts`, `src/services/featureFlags/workspaceRailFeatureFlag.test.ts`, `src/utils/workspaceRailGlyphs.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/workspace/transfer.test.rs`, `e2e/journeys/35-terminal-rail-scoping.mjs`; `hydrateWorkspaceInstanceContext.ts` and `restoreInstanceContext.ts` have no dedicated tests
- notes: Documented known limitation: on macOS two case-differing spellings of one folder are two workspaces. Deferred gap G2 (cross-window orphan on move) is still open (`finalizeInstanceRemoval.ts`, `workspaceWindowActions.ts`). Close cancellation is deliberately not atomic. `data-rail-action` / `data-instance-id` are an automation contract for `e2e/lib/rail.mjs`. The per-instance operation lock is advisory and webview-local.

### File explorer tree
- id: file-explorer-tree
- feature: Sidebar file tree & outline
- summary: Virtualized workspace file tree (react-arborist) rooted at the workspace folder, listed in one Rust call and refreshed from the filesystem watcher.
- capabilities: single click opens a file (Enter starts inline rename); folder open state persisted across view switches and per rail instance; scroll-offset restore; active-file highlight; workspace-name header row; ResizeObserver-measured height; Expand All / Collapse All; Show Hidden Files and Show All Files (workspace-scoped, persisted); `general.showFileExtensions` decides how names are spelled; always-skipped dirs (`.git`, `node_modules`, `.obsidian`, `.svn`, `__pycache__`, `.DS_Store`, `.vscode`, `.idea`, `target`, `.next`, `dist`, `.superpowers`) plus user `excludeFolders` are listed but not descended; truncation past 50,000 nodes / 64 levels; unreadable subdirectory shown empty and flagged, unreadable root surfaces an error; debounced back-off rescan scheduler; rescan on window focus; ARIA `navigation` landmark; Space opens Quick Look (area 13 block `quick-look`)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: workspace mode only; `showHiddenFiles` / `showAllFiles` per-workspace config, both default false; `general.showFileExtensions = true`
- surfaces: sidebar Files view and header buttons; shortcuts `toggleHiddenFiles` Mod+Shift+. (Ctrl+H off macOS), `toggleAllFiles` Mod+Shift+A; palette `explorer.toggleHiddenFiles`, `explorer.toggleAllFiles`; Settings → Files & Images → File Browser

- rust: `files::tree_walk::list_directory_tree` (`src-tauri/src/files/tree_walk.rs`); `files::tree::compute_is_hidden` (`src-tauri/src/files/tree.rs`); `watcher::start_watching` / `stop_watching`

- notes: The listing sends the root once and each node by name; `treeListingPaths.ts` rebuilds the absolute paths exactly as `Path::join` spelled them. Windows hidden detection also honours `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_HIDDEN` / `SYSTEM` (`#[cfg(windows)]`); dot-prefix everywhere. Symlinks are never descended. The skip list is shared with content search (`content_search::matching::ALWAYS_SKIP`).

### Content search — Find in Files
- id: content-search
- feature: Find in files (content search)
- summary: Spotlight-style overlay that searches file contents across the workspace, lists results grouped by file, and opens the matched file at the matching line with the find bar pre-filled.
- capabilities: debounced search-as-you-type (300 ms, minimum 3 characters); toggles case-sensitive / whole-word / regex / ".md" (on by default); per-file grouped results with line numbers and highlighted snippets; keyboard and click selection; exclude-folders read at execution time; request-id ordering plus workspace-generation guard; results in directory-walk order (subdirectories sorted); a `_checked` variant reporting scan completeness, used by the orphan-image verifier
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on (needs a workspace root)
- surfaces: shortcut `contentSearch` Mod+Shift+H (menu `find-in-files`, Edit → Find); palette `view.contentSearch`
- code: `src/components/ContentSearch/{ContentSearch,ContentSearchToggles,ContentSearchResults,contentSearchUtils}.tsx`; `src/components/ContentSearch/useContentSearchScheduler.ts`; `src/stores/uiStore/contentSearchSlice.ts`; `src/services/navigation/contentSearchNavigation.ts`
- rust: `content_search::search_workspace_content`, `content_search::search_workspace_content_checked` (`src-tauri/src/content_search.rs`; the walk in `src-tauri/src/content_search_walk.rs`, the per-file scan in `src-tauri/src/content_search_file.rs`, line matching in `src-tauri/src/content_search_match.rs`)
- docs: `website/guide/workspace-management.md` §"Workspace Content Search"; `website/guide/shortcuts.md`
- tests: `src/components/ContentSearch/__tests__/ContentSearch.test.tsx`, `src/components/ContentSearch/__tests__/contentSearchUtils.test.tsx`, `src/components/ContentSearch/{ContentSearchResults,ContentSearchToggles}.test.tsx`, `src/components/ContentSearch/useContentSearchScheduler.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/content_search.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_search_fixture.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_search_walk.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_search_file.test.rs`
- notes: Rust guarantees — 5 s deadline with partial results, 1000-match / file-count / file-size caps, 200-character line cap, NUL-byte binary skip, symlinks and dotfiles skipped (dot-directories are descended unless on the skip list), regex size and DFA limits, character (not byte) offsets. The ".md" toggle ("Markdown Files Only") actually restricts to every registered format flagged `contentSearchIndexed` (markdown, txt, yaml, plus json/toml/mermaid/svg/html when enabled); off searches every non-hidden, non-binary file. The overlay's placeholder, status, toggle and hint strings (`contentSearch.placeholder`, `.searching`, `.minChars`, `.resultCount`, `.noResults`, `.noWorkspace`, `.ariaLabel`, `.caseSensitive`, `.wholeWord`, `.regex`, `.markdownOnly`, `.hintNavigate`, `.hintOpen`, `.hintClose`) exist in no locale file, so they render their inline English defaults in every language.

### Recent files and recent workspaces
- id: recents
- feature: Workspace management
- summary: Two persisted MRU lists (max 10 each) behind the native Open Recent menus, the Welcome screen, Quick Open's recent tier, and the macOS Dock's Recent Documents.
- capabilities: add/remove/clear per list; cross-window read-merge-write by path before every persist; serialized native-menu IPC per channel; on-mount sync to the native menu; dynamic submenu items (`recent-file-N`, `recent-workspace-N`, `clear-recent`, `clear-recent-workspaces`, empty placeholders); macOS Dock Recent Documents registration for every opened file; opening a recent file routes by `general.openInNewTab` and `general.workspaceRailMode`; opening a recent workspace asks Rust for access first — granted opens the canonical root Rust returns, readable by the static scope opens the entry, a gone folder offers removal from the list, an unrecorded folder outside the static scope opens the Rust folder picker at it (the picked folder is what opens; Cancel opens nothing), and a check that cannot run opens the entry only if the static scope reads it; a failed check or a refused picker is reported to the user, not read as a cancel
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; Dock registration is `#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]`
- surfaces: File → Recent Files / Recent Workspaces submenus; palette `file.openRecent`, `file.clearRecent`, `workspace.openRecent`, `workspace.clearRecent`; Welcome screen lists; Quick Open recent tier; macOS Dock right-click
- code: `src/stores/recentsStore.ts`; `src/stores/createRecentsStore.ts`; `src/stores/persistedListMerge.ts`; `src/hooks/useRecentFilesSync.ts`; `src/hooks/useRecentWorkspacesSync.ts`; `src/services/commands/recentFilesCommands.ts`; `src/services/commands/recentWorkspacesCommands.ts`
- rust: `menu::update_recent_files`, `menu::update_recent_workspaces` (`src-tauri/src/menu/dynamic.rs`); `dock_recent::register_dock_recent` (`src-tauri/src/dock_recent.rs`)
- docs: `website/guide/workspace-management.md` §"Recent Workspaces", §"macOS Dock Recent Documents"; `website/guide/troubleshooting.md` §"Folder Access and `forbidden path` Errors"
- tests: `src/stores/recentsStore.test.ts`, `src/stores/persistedListMerge.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useRecentFilesSync.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useRecentWorkspacesSync.test.ts`, `src/services/commands/recentFilesCommands.test.ts`, `src/services/commands/recentWorkspacesCommands.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/dock_recent.test.rs`
- notes: Documented tradeoff (`recentsStore.ts`): a removal in one window can be resurrected by another window's later merge — no tombstones; Clear is a deliberate blind wipe. `registerDockRecent` swallows the rejection off macOS by design.

### Workspace file watching and external-change layer
- id: workspace-events
- feature: Save pipeline & file watching
- summary: A Rust `notify` watcher per window feeds a normalized frontend event bus, which keeps the file tree in sync and drives reload/conflict handling for open documents changed on disk.
- capabilities: one watcher per window keyed by window label, rooted at the workspace root or else the active document's directory (bare Windows drive roots rejected); 200 ms debounce keyed by (watch id, path, kind); noise-dir filtering (`.git`, `node_modules`, `.obsidian`) while `.github` / `.vscode` pass; paths rebased onto the requested root; frontend normalization (scope filter, self-write flag, kind, dedup), coalescing bus, content-hash no-op suppression; per-tab modify policy (restore / no-op / un-diverge / auto-reload / queue conflict) with soft comparison for cloud-sync rewrites; debounced batch queue with requeue on failure; reload-all / keep-all / review-each batch dialog
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic; reload toast; conflict and batch dialogs
- code: `src/hooks/useWindowFileWatcher.ts`; `src/utils/watchRoot.ts`; `src/services/workspaceEvents/{subscribeWorkspaceEvents,workspaceEventBus,normalizeFsEvents,suppressUnchanged,contentHashCache,types}.ts`; `src/services/files/{applyModifyPolicy,externalChangeBatchQueue,fileChangeBatch,resolveDirtyBatch}.ts`; `src/hooks/useExternalFileChanges.ts`
- rust: `watcher::start_watching`, `watcher::stop_watching` (`src-tauri/src/watcher.rs`)
- docs: `website/guide/workspace-management.md` §"External Changes"
- tests: `src/services/workspaceEvents/{subscribeWorkspaceEvents,workspaceEventBus,normalizeFsEvents,suppressUnchanged,contentHashCache}.test.ts`, `src/services/files/{externalChangeBatchQueue,fileChangeBatch}.test.ts`, `src/hooks/{useExternalFileChanges,useExternalFileChanges.deletion,useExternalFileChanges.eolRewrite,useExternalFileChanges.media,useExternalFileChanges.staleConflict}.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useWindowFileWatcher.test.ts`, `src/utils/watchRoot.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/watcher.test.rs`, `e2e/journeys/21-external-change-reloads-clean-doc.mjs`; `applyModifyPolicy.ts` and `resolveDirtyBatch.ts` have no dedicated tests
- notes: Known limitations (`watcher.rs` header): no glob ignore patterns (component-based filtering only), and the debounce is leading-edge, so the frontend can briefly show content one write behind. Watcher start/stop are serialized per window label.

### AI workspace-open approval
- id: workspace-approval
- feature: Workspace management
- summary: A fail-closed consent dialog shown when an MCP client asks to open a folder as a workspace — opening a folder grants the AI a whole file tree, so it needs an explicit human click every time.
- capabilities: one-shot approval bound to the canonical path, the target window and one per-session client id (`ONE_SHOT_CLIENT_ID`, not the authenticated client — see area 10 `mcp-open-workspace-approval`); fail now → approve → AI retries the same call; 5-minute one-shot TTL; at most 32 pending prompts and 8 unconsumed one-shots; equivalent prompts deduplicated; Escape, backdrop and the default-focused Deny all fail closed; no "remember" option; browser view frozen while the prompt is up; after approval, Rust is asked for access before the one-shot is spent — a folder nobody chose that the static scope cannot read opens the Rust folder picker at it, the call fails with `APPROVAL_REQUIRED`, the one-shot survives, and the retry after the pick opens it; another folder dialog already open is `BUSY` (none shown) and a dialog that cannot be shown is `INTERNAL`, both keeping the one-shot; a gone folder is `INVALID_PATH`, an access check that cannot run fails closed (`INTERNAL`)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on while the MCP bridge is active

### Image assets in the workspace
- id: image-assets
- feature: Media (image/video/audio)
- summary: Pasted or dropped images can be copied into the document's assets folder, and assets no document references can be trashed when a document closes.
- capabilities: `image.copyToAssets` copies a dropped or pasted image next to the document; `image.cleanupOrphansOnClose` trashes unreferenced assets when a tab, window or the app closes, keeping any file still referenced by another document in the folder and verifying against a complete workspace content scan; Format → Text Cleanup → Clean Up Unused Images… runs the same check on demand, lists what it found and asks before trashing
- status: shipped-on, shipped-off
- gate: `image.copyToAssets = true`; `image.cleanupOrphansOnClose = false`
- surfaces: Settings → Files & Images → Images; automatic on drop/paste and on close; menu `cleanup-images` (Format → Text Cleanup); palette `image.cleanupOrphans`
- code: `src/services/media/{closeCleanup,workspaceReferenceCheck,orphanAssetCleanup,orphanCleanupPrompt,imageOperations}.ts`; `src/services/windowClose/windowCloseFlow.ts`; `src/services/tabs/tabOperations.ts`; `src/hooks/useImageDragDrop.ts`; `src/stores/dropZoneStore.ts`; `src/services/commands/miscCommands.ts`; `src/pages/settings/FilesImagesSettings.tsx`
- rust: `content_search::search_workspace_content_checked`, `files::ops::move_paths_to_trash`
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"Images"; `website/guide/features.md` §"Image Cleanup (Format → Text Cleanup → Clean Up Unused Images…)"
- tests: `src/services/media/{workspaceReferenceCheck,orphanAssetCleanup,orphanCleanupPrompt,imageOperations}.test.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/windowCloseFlow.test.ts`, `src/stores/dropZoneStore.test.ts`, `src/pages/settings/FilesImagesSettings.test.tsx`; `closeCleanup.ts` has no dedicated test (exercised through the window-close tests)
- notes: `copyToAssets` ships on, `cleanupOrphansOnClose` ships off. The delete path fails closed: a search error, an incomplete scan, or a filename too short to search keeps the file (`workspaceReferenceCheck.ts`). Inline images are served over asset://, whose scope is now the fs static roots plus runtime grants (`src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`), so an image beside a document opened alone from outside those roots does not render until its folder is opened as a workspace (`website/guide/privacy.md`).

## Area 5 — Tabs, windows, document lifecycle, session

Verified: `bf8b69ceb`

Document windows mount their lifecycle through `src/hooks/lifecycle/useWindowLifecycle.ts` (per window) and `src/hooks/lifecycle/MainWindowRunners.tsx` (main window only); window creation and close interception live in `src-tauri/src/window_manager/`, quit coordination in `src-tauri/src/quit.rs`, and the per-tab document state in `src/stores/documentStore/`.

### Tab strip (status-bar tabs)
- id: tab-strip
- feature: Tabs & windows
- summary: The window's open documents render as pills in the bottom status bar, with a "+" new-tab button, per-tab state badges, roving keyboard navigation, and one synthetic pill standing for the whole browser workspace.
- capabilities: click to activate; middle-click or X to close (no X on pinned tabs); dirty `•`, pinned, missing and divergent indicators; label hides the extension per `general.showFileExtensions` while the close button's accessible name keeps the full name; "+" new tab; APG tablist roving focus (Arrow/Home/End, Enter/Space); Alt+Shift+Arrow keyboard reorder; ARIA live region for drag and reorder outcomes; faint underline on the pill shown in the other split pane; one browser-workspace pill grouping every browser page; with the workspace rail on, only the active workspace instance's document tabs are listed (`useVisibleWindowTabs` projection)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; the bar renders only while `uiStore.statusBarVisible` (default true) or while AI has an active status or a browser page is active
- surfaces: status bar; shortcut `toggleStatusBar` = F7 (`view.toggleStatusBar`)
- code: `src/components/StatusBar/StatusBar.tsx`, `src/components/StatusBar/StatusBarTabStrip.tsx`, `src/components/Tabs/Tab.tsx`, `src/components/StatusBar/tabKeyboard.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/paneIndicator.ts`, `src/hooks/useVisibleWindowTabs.ts`, `src/services/tabs/visibleWindowTabs.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/tab-navigation.md` §"Switching between open tabs"
- tests: `src/components/Tabs/Tab.test.tsx`, `src/components/StatusBar/StatusBar.test.tsx`, `src/components/StatusBar/StatusBar.a11y.test.tsx`, `src/components/StatusBar/StatusBarTabStrip.test.tsx`, `src/components/StatusBar/tabKeyboard.test.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/paneIndicator.test.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/tabPillSurface.test.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/tabStripHarnessContract.test.tsx`, `src/hooks/useVisibleWindowTabs.test.ts`; e2e `e2e/journeys/04-tab-lifecycle.mjs`; no dedicated test for `src/services/tabs/visibleWindowTabs.ts` (covered through its consumers)
- notes: the other-pane underline is suppressed while the browser workspace is active, because the native webview paints above all DOM and no document pane is on screen (`paneIndicator.ts`).

### Tab lifecycle (create / close / pin / reorder)
- id: tab-lifecycle

- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: shortcuts `newTab` = Mod-T (`tab.new`), `closeFile` = Mod-W (`tab.close`, menu id `close`); tab context menu; status-bar "+"
- code: `src/stores/tabStore.ts`, `src/stores/tabStoreHelpers.ts`, `src/stores/tabStoreTypes.ts`, `src/services/tabs/tabOperations.ts`, `src/services/commands/tabCommands.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/tabCleanup.ts`, `src/stores/tabRemovalBus.ts`

The same header still says tabs are restored "via workspaceStore.lastOpenTabs"; restore now prefers the additive `sessionTabs` field (`src/services/persistence/sessionTabs.ts`).

### Tab context menu
- id: tab-context-menu
- feature: Tabs & windows
- summary: Right-clicking a tab pill exposes per-tab file and window actions plus four bulk-close variants.
- capabilities: Move to New Window; Pin/Unpin; Open to the Side (document tabs only — omitted for browser tabs); Rename (inline `TabRenameInput`); Copy Path; Copy Relative Path; Reveal in Finder/Explorer; Restore to Disk (only when the file is missing); Revert to Saved (only when dirty, with a confirm); Close; Close Others; Close to the Right; Close All Unpinned; Close All; keyboard navigation skips disabled items
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; items disabled per state — Move to New Window needs a document and is refused for the last tab of `main`; Rename, Copy Path and Reveal need a saved file; Copy Relative Path needs a workspace file; Open to the Side needs a second visible document; Close is disabled on a pinned tab
- surfaces: context menu (right-click a tab pill)
- code: `src/components/Tabs/TabContextMenu.tsx`, `src/components/Tabs/useTabContextMenuActions.ts`, `src/components/Tabs/TabRenameInput.tsx`, `src/stores/tabRenameStore.ts`, `src/services/tabs/bulkCloseSelectors.ts`, `src/services/tabs/closeAllTabs.ts`, `src/services/tabs/closeLiftingPin.ts`, `src/services/tabs/tabDiskActions.ts`, `src/services/tabs/openToTheSide.ts`, `src/services/tabs/moveTabToNewWindow.ts`
- rust: `detach_tab_to_new_window`, `close_window`
- docs: `website/guide/tab-navigation.md` §"The tab context menu", §"Pinned tabs", §"Renaming a file", §"Two documents side by side"
- tests: `src/components/Tabs/TabContextMenu.test.tsx`, `src/components/Tabs/useTabContextMenuActions.test.ts`, `src/components/Tabs/TabRenameInput.test.tsx`, `src/hooks/useMenuPosition.test.tsx`, `src/stores/tabRenameStore.test.ts`, `src/services/tabs/bulkCloseSelectors.test.ts`, `src/services/tabs/closeAllTabs.test.ts`, `src/services/tabs/closeLiftingPin.test.ts`, `src/services/tabs/tabDiskActions.test.ts`, `src/services/tabs/openToTheSide.test.ts`, `src/services/tabs/moveTabToNewWindow.test.ts`
- notes: pinned tabs are excluded from every bulk-close selector because `closeTab` refuses them. **Close All** is the exception: it asks once when any targeted tab is pinned, naming how many, and on confirmation closes unpinned tabs first and then pinned ones right to left, lifting each pin only for that tab's own close and restoring it in place when a save prompt is cancelled (`src/services/tabs/closeAllTabs.ts`, `src/services/tabs/closeLiftingPin.ts`). Closing a workspace closes its pinned tabs through the same helper (`src/services/workspaces/closeWorkspaceInstance.ts`). Pins are not part of the persisted session record (`src/services/persistence/sessionTabs.ts`), so they last until quit (and through an update restart).

### Tab drag: reorder, detach, cross-window transfer
- id: tab-drag-transfer
- feature: Tabs & windows
- summary: Pointer-drag a tab horizontally to reorder it, or ~40 px above or below the strip to detach it into a new window or drop it onto another VMark window, with an undo toast.
- capabilities: direction-locked reorder vs detach (6 px horizontal lock, 40 px vertical threshold); touch hold delay 180 ms; auto-scroll at the strip edges; pinned tabs are not draggable, and an unpinned tab dropped into the pinned zone snaps back with ARIA feedback; spring-loaded focus of the hovered target window; `tab:drop-preview` broadcast so the target window highlights; document content and line convention (CRLF/BOM) carried in the transfer payload; prepare/commit undo that restores what the destination actually holds; source window auto-closes when it empties (not `main`); the last tab of `main` cannot be moved out; browser tabs are refused with an announcement
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: drag-drop on the tab strip; tab context menu "Move to New Window"; undo toast
- code: `src/hooks/useTabDragOut.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/useStatusBarTabDrag.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/tabTransferActions.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/tabDragRules.ts`, `src/contexts/tabTransferHandlers.ts`, `src/utils/transferLineMetadata.ts`, `src/types/tabTransfer.ts`
- rust: `src-tauri/src/tab_transfer.rs`, `src-tauri/src/tab_transfer/drop_target.rs`, `src-tauri/src/tab_transfer/removal.rs`; commands `detach_tab_to_new_window`, `transfer_tab_to_existing_window`, `claim_tab_transfer`, `find_drop_target_window`, `focus_existing_window`, `remove_tab_from_window`
- docs: `website/guide/workspace-management.md` §"Detaching Tabs into New Windows" (both drag directions, drop onto another window, undo toast, refused tabs)
- tests: `src/hooks/useTabDragOut.test.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/tabDragRules.test.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/tabTransferActions.test.ts`; Rust `src-tauri/src/tab_transfer.test.rs`; no dedicated test for `src/components/StatusBar/useStatusBarTabDrag.ts`
- notes: the payload is registered before the window is created and rolled back if creation fails, so a claim on mount cannot find an empty registry; unclaimed transfers are cleared on `WindowEvent::Destroyed` (`src-tauri/src/app_setup.rs`). The transfer registry is a process-global `static` (`TRANSFER_REGISTRY`) rather than `.manage()` state.

### Tab navigation: MRU, next/prev, last-used
- id: tab-navigation-mru
- feature: Tabs & windows
- summary: Move between tabs positionally (next/previous) or by recency — `Ctrl+Tab` toggles back to the tab you were in most recently.

### Reopen closed tab (scoped history)
- id: closed-tab-reopen
- feature: Tabs & windows
- summary: Closed tabs are remembered per workspace-instance scope (plus one window-global browser scope) and reopened most-recent-first from File → Reopen Closed Tab or a user-assigned shortcut.
- capabilities: per-scope cap of 10; monotonic close sequence; detach does not record; scope re-keyed when a loose instance's identity changes; a window's scopes dropped when it closes; captured into hot exit as `closedTabIds`; reopen compares the active context's newest document with the browser scope's newest entry and takes the newer; a closed document whose path is already open in the window is skipped, not duplicated; rail off uses one window-wide scope; empty history is a no-op
- status: partial
- gate: always on
- surfaces: File menu `reopen-closed-tab`; command `tab.reopenClosed` (menu event `menu:reopen-closed-tab`); shortcut `reopenClosedTab` = unbound by default
- code: `src/stores/tabStoreClosedScopes.ts`, `src/stores/tabStoreClosedScopesValidation.ts`, `src/services/workspaces/reopenClosedTab.ts`, `src/stores/tabRemovalBus.ts`, `src/services/commands/tabCommands.ts`

- docs: `website/guide/workspace-rail.md` (Reopen Closed Tab row); `website/guide/shortcuts.md` (Reopen Closed Tab, customizable)

- notes: history stores tab metadata only (stated in the `tabStore.ts` header), so reopening an unsaved tab loses its edits; `reopenClosedTabForActiveContext` only re-adds the tab record via `restoreTab`, `cleanupTabState` freed the document state at close, and no code path reloads the file (confirmed by reading `src/services/workspaces/reopenClosedTab.ts`, `src/stores/tabStore.ts` and every `initDocument` caller; not exercised in a running app), so a reopened document tab likely opens with no document state. The `reopenClosedTab.ts` header still calls it "the Cmd+Shift+T behavior", but no default key is bound (Mod-Shift-T is `insertTable`).

### Multi-window management
- id: multi-window
- feature: Tabs & windows

### Cross-window live document references
- id: live-docs
- feature: Media (image/video/audio)
- summary: Before deleting an orphaned image, a window asks every other document window which image references its live (possibly unsaved) buffer holds, and deletes nothing unless every one of them answered.
- capabilities: `collect_live_document_refs` targets each other document window (`main`, `doc-*`) with `emit_to` (not a broadcast) under a per-request UUID and an expected-label set; each window's responder answers with its own label plus reference keys extracted from its own store; answers are counted by distinct expected label, so a duplicate listener or an echo adds keys but never completes the count; Rust unions the keys and resolves when the set empties, or after an 800 ms deadline; a failed emit counts as a silent window; `complete: false` (or a malformed reply) means "cannot verify" and the caller deletes nothing; only keys cross IPC, never document contents, so payload size is independent of document size
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic — Format → Text Cleanup → Clean Up Unused Images… (menu id `cleanup-images` → command `image.cleanupOrphans`) and the orphan cleanup offered when a document closes
- code: `src-tauri/src/live_docs.rs`, `src/hooks/useLiveDocsResponder.ts`, `src/services/media/crossWindowRefs.ts`, `src/services/media/orphanCleanupPrompt.ts`, `src/services/media/closeCleanup.ts`, `src/hooks/lifecycle/useWindowLifecycle.ts`
- rust: `live_docs::collect_live_document_refs`, `live_docs::live_docs_response` (internal `register_request`, `apply_response`, `take_result`)
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Image Cleanup (Format → Text Cleanup → Clean Up Unused Images…)" (states that an image another window's unsaved buffer uses is kept, and that nothing is deleted when a window does not answer)
- tests: `src-tauri/src/live_docs.test.rs`, `src/hooks/useLiveDocsResponder.test.ts`, `src/services/media/crossWindowRefs.test.ts`
- notes: in-flight requests live in a process-global `static` (`PENDING`) rather than `.manage()` state. Both commands still return the legacy `Result<T, String>`.

### Document open pipeline (file → tab → document)
- id: document-open-pipeline
- feature: Tabs & windows
- summary: Every way of opening a document — dialog, Quick Open, recent files, sidebar, Finder/CLI, drag-drop, links, MCP — funnels through one policy that decides activate / replace / new tab / new window.
- capabilities: reuse an existing tab for the same path; replace a lone clean untitled tab; empty-window detection; an external file's resolved workspace opened before its tab is created (a failed workspace open is reported, the file still opens); pre-read size gate — refuse, warn above 5 MB, or open large files in Source mode with a load indicator; heading-fragment landing after `foo.md#heading`; media files routed to the viewer; files VMark does not open handed to the system default app with a toast on refusal; recents and file-ownership updates; window-scoped `open-file` event (scope carried in the payload); five explicit open outcomes (opened, deduped, refused, closed, failed) so a close during the read is not treated as a failure
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; `largeFile.autoSourceMode = true`, `largeFile.warnAbove5MB = true`
- surfaces: menu ids `open` (`openFile`, unbound), `quick-open` (`quickOpen` = Mod-O), `new` (`newFile` = Mod-N), recent files; shortcut `newTab` = Mod-T; drag-drop; Finder/CLI; sidebar; link click; MCP `workspace` operations
- code: `src/services/navigation/fileOpen.ts`, `src/services/navigation/executeOpenDecision.ts`, `src/services/navigation/replaceTabWithFile.ts`, `src/services/navigation/loadFileIntoTab.ts`, `src/services/navigation/largeFileRouting.ts`, `src/services/navigation/openFragment.ts`, `src/services/navigation/openFileEvent.ts`, `src/services/navigation/openMediaFile.ts`, `src/services/navigation/openWithDefaultApp.ts`, `src/services/navigation/newFile.ts`, `src/services/tabs/replaceableTab.ts`, `src/utils/openPolicy.ts`
- rust: `get_file_size_bytes`, `get_pending_file_opens`
- docs: `website/guide/workspace-management.md` §"Opening a Workspace", §"Multi-Window"; `website/guide/large-files.md`
- tests: `src/services/navigation/fileOpen.test.ts`, `src/services/navigation/__tests__/fileOpen.test.ts`, `src/services/navigation/executeOpenDecision.test.ts`, `src/services/navigation/largeFileRouting.test.ts`, `src/services/navigation/openFragment.test.ts`, `src/services/navigation/openFileEvent.test.ts`, `src/services/navigation/openMediaFile.test.ts`, `src/services/navigation/openWithDefaultApp.test.ts`, `src/services/navigation/loadFileIntoTab.closeDuringRead.test.ts`, `src/services/navigation/newFile.test.ts`, `src/services/tabs/replaceableTab.test.ts`, `src/utils/openPolicy.test.ts`; e2e `e2e/journeys/09-open-from-disk.mjs`, `e2e/journeys/12-nonmd-format-dispatch.mjs`; no dedicated test for `replaceTabWithFile.ts`

### Document state model
- id: document-state
- feature: Save pipeline & file watching
- summary: Per-tab document state — the canonical editor text, the last saved snapshot, the raw disk bytes, dirty/divergent/missing flags, cursor, line metadata, and a per-tab revision id for MCP concurrency.
- capabilities: three-domain field contract (`content` canonical LF/BOM-free, `savedContent` same domain, `lastDiskContent` raw disk bytes) so dirty tracking is a same-domain compare; two ingest doors (`setEditorContent` vs `initDocument`/`ingestExternalContent`) both canonicalising; documents handed out readonly so every change goes through an action; per-tab revision bumped on meaningful edits; `isDivergent`/`isMissing`; per-doc editor mode (ADR-009); hard-break style; forced-Source markers with a reason (`large-file` or `unparseable`); per-tab lint diagnostics; in-flight file-load indicator; all per-tab state released by `cleanupTabState` on close or detach
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic; MCP `document` / `selection` operations read and write through it
- code: `src/stores/documentStore/document.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/documentState.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/ingestState.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/storeContract.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/revision.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/largeFileSession.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore.ts`, `src/hooks/useDocumentState.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/tabCleanup.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: none directly (consequences in `website/guide/workspace-management.md` §"External Changes" and `website/guide/large-files.md`)
- tests: `src/stores/documentStore.test.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/__tests__/readonlyDocumentView.test.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/__tests__/externalWriterGate.test.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/__tests__/dualSnapshotSave.test.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/__tests__/ingestExternalContent.test.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/__tests__/ingestMatrix.test.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/__tests__/canonicalTextInvariant.test.ts`, `src/stores/documentStore/__tests__/revisionCoverage.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useDocumentState.test.tsx`, `src/services/windowClose/tabCleanup.test.ts`
- notes: `setContent` and `loadContent` no longer exist; `externalWriterGate.test.ts` asserts it. The `document.ts` header still says `setContent` "survives only as a deprecated test alias", contradicting `storeContract.ts`. The unparseable fallback that sets the `unparseable` marker lives in `src/services/editor/unparseableDocument.ts` (area 3).

### Save pipeline
- id: save-pipeline
- feature: Save pipeline & file watching
- summary: Saving a document normalizes line endings and hard breaks, re-emits the file's BOM, writes atomically via Rust, updates the saved snapshots, records a history snapshot, and coordinates with the file watcher.
- capabilities: Save / Save As / Move To; per-path serialization so two writes to one file cannot land out of order; per-document save-target claims so a stale auto-save cannot re-point a document after a Save As; pending-save registration before the write, cleared 1000 ms after (late FSEvents); atomic temp + fsync + rename with permission preservation (Unix) and xattr / Finder-tag preservation (macOS); transient Windows `MoveFileExW` sharing refusals retried with backoff, never remove-then-retry; symlink target resolution for document saves; path-traversal, absolute-path and missing-parent validation with typed `CommandError`; `create_file_exclusive` (`O_EXCL`) reservation so a Save-All batch cannot overwrite an existing file; file-ownership conflict refusal; `atomic_write_file` and `create_file_exclusive` resolve the path once and refuse the workspace-grant list (`workspace-grants.json`) by name and through a link, with a typed `permission-denied`; on Unix both then write through the parent folder held open and judged by its identity (`openat`/`renameat`), so a folder or link swapped after the check cannot redirect the write; Windows stays path-based; recents updated (skipped for auto-save); tab ownership reassigned on path change; every save hands the written content to the coherence capture funnel, which alone enforces `general.coherenceCaptureOnSave`
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `general.lineEndingsOnSave = "preserve"`, `markdown.hardBreakStyleOnSave = "preserve"`
- surfaces: menu ids `save` (`save` = Mod-S), `save-as` (`saveAs` = Mod-Shift-S), `move-to` (`moveTo`, unbound); automatic (auto-save, close prompts); MCP `document` writes
- code: `src/services/persistence/saveToPath.ts`, `src/services/persistence/applyPostSaveState.ts`, `src/services/persistence/saveTargetClaim.ts`, `src/services/persistence/serializeByPath.ts`, `src/services/persistence/saveHistorySnapshot.ts`, `src/services/files/fileSave.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/saveDialog.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/reserveBatchDestinations.ts`, `src/utils/pendingSaves.ts`, `src/utils/linebreaks.ts`
- rust: `files::write::atomic_write_file`, `files::create::create_file_exclusive` (grant-list guard via `src-tauri/src/workspace/grants/protect.rs`); held-folder write core (Unix) in `src-tauri/src/files/write/anchored.rs`; path-based core (Windows) in `src-tauri/src/atomic_replace.rs`, `src-tauri/src/atomic_persist.rs`; link resolution in `src-tauri/src/link_target.rs`
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"Whitespace" (Line endings on save)
- tests: `src/services/persistence/saveToPath.test.ts`, `src/services/persistence/saveToPath.ordering.test.ts`, `src/services/persistence/saveTargetClaim.test.ts`, `src/services/persistence/serializeByPath.test.ts`, `src/services/persistence/applyPathReconciliation.test.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/reserveBatchDestinations.test.ts`, `src/services/files/fileSave.test.ts`, `src/utils/pendingSaves.test.ts`, `src/utils/linebreaks.test.ts`; Rust `src-tauri/src/files/write.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/atomic_replace.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/link_target.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/mcp_bridge/token_file.test.rs` (exercises `persist_with_retry`); e2e `e2e/journeys/10-save-to-disk.mjs`, `e2e/journeys/14-multi-doc-save-integrity.mjs`, `e2e/journeys/19-line-ending-preservation.mjs`, `e2e/journeys/20-failed-save-preserves-document.mjs`, `src-tauri/src/atomic_persist.test.rs` (the parent-directory sync)
- notes: a hung history backend holds the save promise open after the file and stores are already updated (named in the `saveToPath.ts` header; a bounded timeout is the proposed fix). Linux and Windows document metadata (ACLs) is not carried across an atomic replace. The native save dialog passes no file-type filters because rfd's filter API hangs or crashes on macOS 26.

### Auto-save
- id: auto-save
- feature: Save pipeline & file watching
- summary: Dirty documents with a file path are written back automatically at a configurable interval.
- capabilities: interval timer (min 1000 ms) restarted when the setting changes; skips untitled, missing and divergent documents; skips while a manual save is in progress; reentry guard against overlapping cycles; flushes the WYSIWYG buffer first; re-reads each tab's state inside the loop; records both save snapshots plus a `lastAutoSave` timestamp; suppresses error toasts and the recents update; "auto-save paused" toast while a document is divergent
- status: shipped-on

- surfaces: automatic; settings pane Files & Images → Saving; status-bar "saved N ago" label

### Save-on-close prompts and Save All & Quit
- id: close-save-prompts
- feature: Quit & save-all behavior
- summary: Closing a tab or a window, or quitting, resolves unsaved documents first — a native Save/Discard/Cancel prompt per document or one aggregate prompt for several, with a single folder pick for multiple untitled files.
- capabilities: single-doc and multi-doc prompts; divergent documents need resolution as well as dirty ones, and the prompt says the file changed on disk; Save As for untitled with a default-folder fallback; batch persist of every pathed document, then one Save As (single) or one folder picker (several) for untitled ones; collision-free destination reservation; Save All and Quit is a mode of the coordinated quit: every document window flushes its mounted editors, saves each dirty or divergent document without prompting (untitled ones still get one Save As or one folder picker), revalidates the dirty set, then closes through the normal close flow; a cancelled or failed save keeps that window open and cancels the quit
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: menu id `save-all-quit` (`saveAllQuit` = Alt-Mod-Shift-Q; File menu, and the app menu on macOS), handled in Rust (`MenuAction::SaveAllQuit`); command palette `file.saveAllQuit` → `save_all_and_quit`; window close button; Mod-W; Mod-Q

- rust: `create_file_exclusive`, `quit::save_all_and_quit`
- docs: `website/guide/tab-navigation.md` §"Closing tabs and windows"; `website/guide/shortcuts.md` (Save All and Quit row); `website/guide/features.md` §"Session Recovery (Hot Exit)"
- tests: `src/services/windowClose/closeSave.test.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/reserveBatchDestinations.test.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/windowCloseFlow.test.ts`, `src/services/files/fileSave.test.ts`, `src/services/files/saveAllQuit.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useWindowClose.saveAllQuit.test.tsx`; e2e `e2e/journeys/15-dirty-file-close-guard.mjs`, `e2e/journeys/14-multi-doc-save-integrity.mjs`
- notes: The `closeSave.ts` header names a `decideOnClose()` util that no longer exists.

### Window close flow
- id: window-close
- feature: Tabs & windows
- summary: Closing a window is a transaction — resolve every dirty or divergent document, confirm pinned tabs, clean up orphans, persist the session, then close natively; nothing is destroyed until the native close is issued.
- capabilities: Rust intercepts `CloseRequested` for document windows (`main`, `doc-*`) and hands the decision to the frontend; shared in-flight promise so duplicate triggers join it, with a new request treated as a retry after 5 min stalled; dirty set revalidated after every await (bounded at 3 rounds, cancels rather than discards); pinned-tabs confirmation; media orphan cleanup; workspace session persisted; store teardown only after `close_window`; per-window closed-tab history dropped; `menu:close` closes the active tab, or the window when it is empty; an `app:quit-requested` arriving mid-close awaits it and sends `cancel_quit` on failure; close milestones logged at INFO in release builds; on destroy, the window's embedded browser, PTY sessions and MCP workspace registration are torn down natively
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: window close button; Mod-W (`menu:close`); Mod-Q; workspace-instance removal
- code: `src/hooks/useWindowClose.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/windowCloseFlow.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/tabCleanup.ts`, `src-tauri/src/window_manager/window_events.rs`, `src-tauri/src/app_setup.rs`
- rust: `close_window`, `window_close_log`; `window_manager::window_events::handle_document_window_close_event`
- docs: `website/guide/tab-navigation.md` §"Closing tabs and windows"; `website/guide/workspace-management.md` §"Empty Workspace Window" (closing the window)
- tests: `src/hooks/useWindowClose.comprehensive.test.tsx`, `src/hooks/useWindowClose.lifecycle.test.tsx`, `src/services/windowClose/windowCloseFlow.test.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/tabCleanup.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/window_manager/window_events.test.rs`
- notes: `prevent_close` hands the outcome to the frontend with no timeout and no fallback, which is why the close is logged in release builds (`#1253`). On Windows with `general.closeToTray` on, the last document window is hidden instead of closed and no save flow runs — see `src-tauri/src/close_to_tray/mod.rs` (area 15, `#1419`).

### Quit coordination and confirm-quit
- id: quit
- feature: Quit & save-all behavior
- summary: Quitting closes every document window through its save flow, one at a time, and an optional gate requires pressing Cmd+Q twice.
- capabilities: `request_quit` → confirm gate (2 s wall-clock window; `app:quit-first-press` shows status-bar feedback for 2 s) → `start_quit` emits `app:quit-requested` to each document window → windows close → `finalize_quit` kills the MCP sidecar, content servers and PTYs → `app.exit(0)`; non-document windows closed immediately; OS-level exit requests bypass the gate; a failed emit cancels the quit rather than hanging; `cancel_quit` clears all state including the first-press timestamp; tray-parked windows are restored before quit asks them to save (Windows); `EXIT_ALLOWED` set only immediately before exit; the setting is mirrored into a Rust `AtomicBool`; macOS keeps the app alive with no document windows, Windows/Linux exit; Save All and Quit (`start_save_all_quit`, no confirm gate) runs the same pipeline with `saveAll: true` in `app:quit-requested`, and a window that cannot save answers `cancel_quit`
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `general.confirmQuit = true`
- surfaces: Mod-Q (app menu Quit → `MenuAction::Quit`); menu id `save-all-quit` (`MenuAction::SaveAllQuit` → `quit::start_save_all_quit`); tray menu "Quit VMark" (Windows, close-to-tray on); settings pane Files & Images → Quit Behavior
- code: `src-tauri/src/quit.rs`, `src-tauri/src/app_setup.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/events/dispatch.rs`, `src/hooks/useConfirmQuitSync.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/useQuitFeedback.ts`, `src/services/files/fileSave.ts`
- rust: `quit::cancel_quit`, `quit::set_confirm_quit`, `quit::save_all_and_quit`; `quit::request_quit` / `quit::start_quit` / `quit::start_save_all_quit` are called from Rust only
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"Quit Behavior"; `website/guide/shortcuts.md` (Save All and Quit)
- tests: `src-tauri/src/quit.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/quit_broadcast.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/events/dispatch.test.rs`, `src/hooks/useConfirmQuitSync.test.ts`, `src/components/StatusBar/useQuitFeedback.test.ts`
- notes: Quit state lives in process-global statics, so `quit.rs` tests run serially under `TEST_LOCK`.

### External file-change detection and conflict resolution
- id: external-change-handling
- feature: Save pipeline & file watching
- summary: When a file changes on disk under an open tab, VMark silently reloads clean documents (with a toast) and offers Save As / Reload / Keep for dirty ones.
- capabilities: clean docs auto-reload; soft content comparison so line-ending, BOM or trailing-newline rewrites by sync daemons are no-ops; dirty docs batch into one debounced (300 ms) dialog with reload-all / keep-all / review-each; own saves filtered via `matchesPendingSave`; rename/remove verified by an existence probe first (Windows `MoveFileEx` and sync daemons emit spurious removes); deleted files marked missing, never auto-closed; divergent docs auto-recover when disk matches the buffer again; after "Keep my changes" `lastDiskContent` is refreshed so an identical cloud rewrite is swallowed; the document is re-read after every await in the dialog; media tabs never UTF-8-read (documentId bump so the viewer re-fetches); a rejected batch is re-queued, not dropped
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic; native dialog; reload / restored toasts; divergent tab badge
- code: `src/hooks/useExternalFileChanges.ts`, `src/services/files/applyModifyPolicy.ts`, `src/services/files/externalChangeBatchQueue.ts`, `src/services/files/resolveDirtyBatch.ts`, `src/services/files/fileChangeBatch.ts`, `src/services/persistence/resolveDirtyFileChange.ts`, `src/services/persistence/reloadFromDisk.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/fsChangeHandlers.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/fsRenameHandlers.ts`
- rust: `watcher::start_watching`, `watcher::stop_watching`
- docs: `website/guide/workspace-management.md` §"External Changes"
- tests: `src/hooks/useExternalFileChanges.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useExternalFileChanges.deletion.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useExternalFileChanges.eolRewrite.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useExternalFileChanges.media.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useExternalFileChanges.staleConflict.test.ts`, `src/services/files/externalChangeBatchQueue.test.ts`, `src/services/files/fileChangeBatch.test.ts`, `src/services/persistence/resolveDirtyFileChange.test.ts`, `src/services/persistence/reloadFromDisk.test.ts`, `src/services/windowClose/fsChangeHandlers.test.ts`; e2e `e2e/journeys/21-external-change-reloads-clean-doc.mjs`; no dedicated test for `applyModifyPolicy.ts`, `resolveDirtyBatch.ts` or `fsRenameHandlers.ts`
