## Area 11 — Coherence, knowledge base, claims

Verified: `bf8b69ceb`

Two unrelated features share this area. The coherence layer is a Rust kernel (`src-tauri/src/coherence/`, one per workspace root, ledger in `<workspace>/.vmark/`) with a thin frontend: the capture funnel (`src/services/coherence/captureFunnel.ts`), the Breakdown and Claims panels (mounted together by `src/components/CoherenceOverlays.tsx`) and their services under `src/services/breakdown/` and `src/services/claims/`. The knowledge base is a separate Node program (`server/content/`) that Rust spawns per workspace (`src-tauri/src/content_server/`) and the Knowledge Base dock (`src/components/KnowledgeBasePanel/`) drives; its entry points are hidden unless Developer Mode is on, because no packaged build ships its runtime. The AI-facing coherence tools are `mcp-tool-coherence-pair` in Area 10.

### Coherence provenance capture (identity block + capture funnel)
- id: coherence-capture
- feature: Coherence & breakdown
- summary: Records writes to workspace files as transformations in a plain-text `.vmark/` ledger and stamps each captured file with a `vmark: id:` frontmatter identity so history survives renames.
- capabilities: one capture-on-save policy for every write path — the webview reads `general.coherenceCaptureOnSave` at the moment of each write and sends `adopt` (on) or `tracked-only` (off), and Rust enforces it in `capture_policy.rs`; `tracked-only` with no ledger records nothing and creates or stamps nothing; `tracked-only` in a workspace whose ledger already exists records writes to documents it tracks (a registered path, or a file that already carries its own `vmark:` id, such as a tracked file moved or copied in), never mints an id for an untracked file, never rewrites a file, and drops an input that would need stamping (the capture falls to `inferred`); capture on human save; capture on genie apply and accepted AI suggestion (live buffer, no disk rewrite); capture on MCP `document.write`, `workspace.save` and `workspace.save_as` with the session-observed read set (`recordMcpRead` → `captureMcpWrite`, confidence `inferred`); each read is pinned via `coherence_head` to the latest revision matching the served content or a dirty buffer's saved base (`src-tauri/src/coherence/head_pin.rs`), a read no revision matches is left out, and a read served under another workspace root is dropped; an identity-bearing file at an unregistered path repoints the registry only when its registered original is gone (a move), a live copy raises a `duplicate-id` diagnostic and a capture hold, and an unreadable original is refused (`src-tauri/src/coherence/adopt_duplicate.rs`); capture on explorer new file (no rewrite) and on history snapshot restore; capture on workflow `save-file` steps in Rust, under the policy the run carried (`run_workflow` `capturePolicy`, `tracked-only` when absent); identity assignment never changes the content hash; watcher-echo suppression via `registerPendingSave`; strict submission-order queue; workspace-root containment in TS and Rust; fire-and-forget — a failed capture never fails the write
- status: shipped-off
- gate: `general.coherenceCaptureOnSave = false` — governs every write path (human save, MCP `document.write`/`workspace.save`/`workspace.save_as`, genie apply, accepted AI suggestion, history restore, explorer new file, workflow `save-file`)
- surfaces: Settings → Files & Images → Saving ("Stamp identity block on save"); otherwise automatic
- code: `src/services/coherence/captureFunnel.ts`, `src/services/coherence/capturePolicy.ts`, `src/services/coherence/mcpCapture.ts`, `src/services/persistence/saveToPath.ts`, `src/plugins/aiSuggestion/widgets.ts`, `src/services/genieInvocation/applyGenieResult.ts`, `src/services/mcpBridge/v2/document.ts`, `src/services/mcpBridge/v2/workspaceSave.ts`, `src/services/mcpBridge/v2/workspaceSaveAs.ts`, `src/components/Sidebar/HistoryView.tsx`, `src/components/Sidebar/FileExplorer/useExplorerOperations.ts`, `src/pages/settings/FilesImagesSettings.tsx`, `src/services/workflow/dispatchWorkflowRun.ts`
- rust: `coherence_capture`, `coherence_head` (`src-tauri/src/coherence/commands_ipc.rs`); `coherence::{capture, capture_policy, capture_input, capture_output, adopt, adopt_duplicate, head_pin, frontmatter, cas, ledger, state, state_lock}`; `src-tauri/src/workflow/coherence_capture.rs`
- docs: `website/guide/coherence.md` §"How it works (30 seconds)", §"Frontmatter identity"; `website/guide/settings.md` (Files & Images → Saving row)
- tests: `src/services/coherence/captureFunnel.test.ts`, `src/services/coherence/capturePolicy.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/capture_policy.test.rs`, `src/services/persistence/saveToPath.test.ts`, `src/services/persistence/saveToPath.ordering.test.ts`, `src/pages/settings/FilesImagesSettings.test.tsx`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/capture.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/frontmatter.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/cas.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/ledger.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/workflow/coherence_capture.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/workflow/commands.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/commands.test.rs` (head pins), `src/services/coherence/mcpCapture.test.ts`, `src/services/mcpBridge/v2/__tests__/workspaceSaveCapture.test.ts`, `src/services/mcpBridge/v2/__tests__/workspaceSaveAsCapture.test.ts`, `src/services/workflow/dispatchWorkflowRun.test.ts`
- notes: Under `tracked-only` the policy is checked against `.vmark/` on disk before the lock, and the lock taken is existing-only (`src-tauri/src/coherence/state_lock.rs`), which declines rather than recreate a `.vmark/` deleted in between. MCP read set bounded to 256 (least-recent evicted), with at most 256 pins in flight; a write awaits its pins at most 500 ms, inside its capture-queue slot. Ledger mutation refuses when a line carries a future format version.

### Scan reconciliation (external edits + git awareness)
- id: coherence-scan
- feature: Coherence & breakdown
- summary: A debounced background scan reconciles files edited outside VMark into the ledger as "observed external" history, and classifies git operations so checkouts and branch switches never mint phantom revisions.
- capabilities: 3 s trailing debounce, one scan in flight, re-run if events land mid-scan; scans triggered only by the window's own watcher, including its rescan reports; git-first classification (navigation vs mutation); `git revert` and merges captured as git-attributed transformations; duplicate-identity detection surfaced as a diagnostic plus capture hold, never auto-fixed; objects marked absent only after a complete walk; a `merge-completed` diagnostic per merge SHA; a markdown file whose size, mtime (and on Unix inode and ctime) are unchanged since a scan read it is not read or hashed again, a file modified within 2 s of being stamped is never trusted; all kernel work on the blocking pool
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on once a workspace root is open (`startCoherenceScanOnChange` via `src/services/runtimeWiring.ts`); each scan carries the capture-on-save policy, and with `general.coherenceCaptureOnSave = false` a workspace without a ledger is not scanned, and a `.vmark/` that vanishes before the lock is not recreated (`scan_on_change` in `src-tauri/src/coherence/capture_policy.rs`, existing-only lock in `src-tauri/src/coherence/state_lock.rs`)
- surfaces: automatic (workspace file-watcher events)
- code: `src/services/coherence/scanOnChange.ts`, `src/services/coherence/capturePolicy.ts`, `src/services/runtimeWiring.ts`
- rust: `coherence_scan`; `coherence::{capture_policy, state_lock, scan, scan_git, scan_walk, scan_cache, scan_report, scan_diagnostics, gitops, gitops_cmd}`
- docs: `website/guide/coherence.md` §"How it works (30 seconds)", §"Git interoperability"
- tests: `src/services/coherence/scanOnChange.test.ts`, `src/services/coherence/scanOnChange.window.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/capture_policy.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/scan.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/scan_git.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/scan_cache.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/gitops.test.rs`
- notes: A policy-declined scan returns an empty (incomplete) report, which can never drive a deletion. `GitObserver` is an injected seam so the observation-unreliable branch is testable (#1207). `gitops_cmd.rs` has no registered command; it is the `git_output` helper `gitops.rs` uses.

### Breakdown view (stale/diverged dependency edges)
- id: breakdown-view
- feature: Coherence & breakdown
- summary: A pull-based panel listing every live, non-fresh dependency edge in the workspace — which derived documents were built from an upstream that has since moved — grouped by downstream artifact.
- capabilities: refresh on open and on demand, never background-polled; edge states `version-stale`, `stale-valid`, `stale-contradicted`, `stale-unknown`, `waived`, `diverged`, `diverged-multi-head`, `unpinnable`; grouping by downstream; 200-row cap on the actionable list with a total count; provenance confidence (`exact`/`inferred`/`unknown`); "previously waived ×N" badge; suppressed group (frozen downstreams, lost or unchanged anchors) collapsed but never hidden; stale-response guards (per-surface tickets and active-workspace check)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on (panel closed by default)
- surfaces: Window menu item `breakdown` (no accelerator); palette `view.toggleBreakdown` ("Breakdown View"); menu→command binding in `src/hooks/useCommandBootstrap.ts`
- code: `src/components/BreakdownPanel/BreakdownPanel.tsx`, `src/components/BreakdownPanel/BreakdownOverlay.tsx`, `src/components/BreakdownPanel/BreakdownRow.tsx`, `src/components/BreakdownPanel/SuppressedGroup.tsx`, `src/components/CoherenceOverlays.tsx`, `src/stores/breakdownStore.ts`, `src/services/breakdown/breakdownService.ts`, `src/services/breakdown/breakdownRefresh.ts`, `src/services/breakdown/refreshGuards.ts`, `src/services/breakdown/breakdownShared.ts`
- rust: `coherence_breakdown`; `coherence::{index_query, index_dag, read_model, read_view, dag}`
- docs: `website/guide/coherence.md` §"The Breakdown view"; `website/guide/features.md` §"Coherence, Knowledge Base & Slidev"
- tests: `src/components/BreakdownPanel/BreakdownPanel.test.tsx`, `src/stores/breakdownStore.test.ts`, `src/services/breakdown/breakdownService.test.ts`, `src/services/breakdown/breakdownShared.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/index.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/read_model.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/read_view.test.rs`
- notes: The registered Tauri command `coherence_status` has no caller at all — the MCP `status` action calls `perform_status` directly in `src-tauri/src/mcp_bridge/coherence_answers.rs`. The actionable set is computed once in Rust so the badge and the list cannot diverge.

### Edge resolution (accept-newer / revise / waive)
- id: coherence-resolve-edge
- feature: Coherence & breakdown
- summary: Three append-only actions on a stale edge — ratify it as still compatible, open the artifact to revise it, or waive the divergence with a required reason.
- capabilities: accept-newer (ratification); revise (opens the downstream via the window-scoped open-file event); waive with a mandatory reason; accept-newer and waive disabled for `diverged-multi-head` and `unpinnable`; errors shown in the panel without blanking the list
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: Breakdown panel row buttons; MCP `coherence_resolve` for delegated agents (Area 10)
- code: `src/services/breakdown/breakdownEdgeService.ts`, `src/components/BreakdownPanel/BreakdownRow.tsx`
- rust: `coherence_resolve`; `coherence::{accept, accept_precondition, operator_accept, lifecycle}`
- docs: `website/guide/coherence.md` §"Actions"; `website/guide/mcp-tools.md` §`coherence_resolve`
- tests: `src/services/breakdown/breakdownService.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/accept.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/accept_precondition.test.rs`
- notes: A waiver is deliberately narrow — one edge, one upstream revision.

### Semantic checking (τ-thresholded AI verdicts)
- id: coherence-semantic-check
- feature: Coherence & breakdown
- summary: On demand, VMark asks the configured AI provider whether an upstream's movement contradicts the derived text, and records the verdict as a badge — valid, contradicted (with a verbatim evidence quote) or unknown.
- capabilities: pull-only per-edge Check; contradiction requires an evidence quote; confidence below τ downgrades to unknown while preserving the model's answer for the log; malformed answers → unknown; a verdict expires when either document moves or the claim set changes; verdicts bound to the exact context and claim snapshot; established claims fed into the prompt; τ sent on every call
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `general.coherenceCheckTau = 0.9`, clamped to `[0.5, 1]`; needs a configured AI provider
- surfaces: Breakdown panel row Check; Settings → Advanced → Coherence
- code: `src/services/breakdown/breakdownEdgeService.ts`, `src/pages/settings/CoherenceSettingsGroup.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/AdvancedSettings.tsx`
- rust: `coherence_check`; `coherence::{check_commands, checker, checker_format}`
- docs: `website/guide/coherence.md` §"Semantic checking, claims, and contexts"; `website/guide/settings.md` §"Coherence"
- tests: `src/pages/settings/CoherenceSettingsGroup.test.tsx`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/checker.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/check_commands.test.rs`
- notes: The UI offers 0.7 / 0.8 / 0.9 / 0.95 plus the current out-of-list value. A request with no `tau` falls back to 0.9 in Rust.

### Canon claims
- id: coherence-claims

### Contexts (named workspace views) & branch contexts

### Provenance recovery
- id: coherence-provenance-recovery
- feature: Coherence & breakdown
- summary: When a derived document is hand-edited its recorded inputs are correctly lost; the Breakdown's "Provenance unknown" group offers to re-attach its most recent prior input set.
- capabilities: lazy "Suggest inputs" (proposals computed, never stored); pre-checked, editable list with roles preserved; "Confirm provenance" re-emits without minting a new revision, so downstreams see no change; documents that never had inputs are not listed; fails loud on multi-head, unknown object or stale head

### Agent delegation (time-limited grants)

### Knowledge Base panel (developer-mode only)
- id: knowledge-base
- feature: Knowledge base & Slidev
- summary: A right-hand dock that starts the local content server for the open workspace and shows it as a browsable knowledge base — hidden unless Developer Mode is on, because no packaged build carries the content-server runtime.
- capabilities: runtime probe on mount and on "Check again" (`content_server_runtime`: Node on the login-shell PATH, content-server `cli.js`), naming what is missing with build-specific copy; start / stop / retry with status `stopped`/`provisioning`/`starting`/`running`/`error`; embedded view via a one-time `/__auth?t=<nonce>` handshake; "Open in browser" with a fresh single-use URL; site/graph toggle; the panel closes itself when Developer Mode is turned off, while a running server is left running; workspace switch marks the store stopped (the app-wide manager keeps the child); a workspace-trust flip restarts the server; bounded crash supervisor (3 auto-restarts per user start, replenished only by a manual start)
- status: shipped-off, dev-only
- gate: `advanced.developerMode = false` (one predicate, `knowledgeBaseAvailableHere`, for the menu item, palette and shortcut); needs `node` on the login-shell PATH and `VMARK_CONTENT_SERVER_CLI` or a provisioned bundle

The `availability.ts` header says hiding the entry points does not close an open panel; `KnowledgeBaseOverlay.tsx` does close it.

### Content server (knowledge base + Slidev host)
- id: content-server
- feature: Knowledge base & Slidev
- summary: A Node HTTP server VMark spawns per workspace on demand that renders the whole workspace as a cross-linked site on loopback and hosts Slidev decks. It is not part of the HTML/PDF export pipeline.
- capabilities: routes `/`, `/note/*`, `/asset/*`, `/graph` + `/api/graph`, `/api/backlinks/*`, `/api/search` (caps 1000 matches / 50 files / 200-char lines), `/__events` (SSE live reload), `/__assets/kb.css` + `kb.js`, `/__health`, `/__mint`, `/__auth`, `/slidev/*` proxy, `POST /api/slidev/preview`, `POST /api/slidev/export`; wiki links `[[Page]]`, `[[dir/Page]]`, `[[Page#Heading]]`, `[[Page|Alias]]` with a visible missing state; typed frontmatter relations and `#tag` edges; chokidar watcher with a debounced index rebuild; remark/rehype render with GFM, KaTeX, alerts and asset resolution; DOMPurify sanitization; CSP and security headers, with `img-src https:` only for a trusted workspace (`--trusted`, a trust change restarts the server); path containment and symlink checks; bidi-control stripping on filenames; one server per workspace keyed by a SHA-256 prefix, on an OS-assigned `127.0.0.1` port discovered via `<appDataDir>/content-server/<key>.port.json`; child stdio drained into the log; Rust supervisor emitting `content-server:exited`; bounded loopback client for every Rust→server call; all children killed at quit
- status: shipped-off, dev-only
- gate: reached only through `knowledge-base` (`advanced.developerMode = false`); runtime resolution `VMARK_CONTENT_SERVER_CLI` → bundled resource (none shipped) → provisioned `base-kb` bundle; `node` from the login-shell PATH
- surfaces: Knowledge Base panel; "Open in browser"
- code: `server/content/src/cli.ts`, `server/content/src/cliMain.ts`, `server/content/src/server/createServer.ts`, `server/content/src/server/runtime.ts`, `server/content/src/server/headers.ts`, `server/content/src/server/search.ts`, `server/content/src/server/assetRoute.ts`, `server/content/src/server/pathContainment.ts`, `server/content/src/index/buildIndex.ts`, `server/content/src/index/watch.ts`, `server/content/src/render/renderMarkdown.ts`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/mod.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/commands.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/start.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/spawn.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/manager.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/supervisor.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/cleanup.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/client.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/port_wait.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/drain.rs`

### Content server: nonce → cookie authentication
- id: content-server-auth
- feature: Knowledge base & Slidev
- summary: The long-lived bootstrap token never appears in a URL; VMark mints a single-use nonce over loopback and sends the browser to `/__auth?t=<nonce>`, which sets an HttpOnly SameSite=Strict session cookie.
- capabilities: `GET /__mint` with `Authorization: Bearer <bootstrapToken>` → nonce (120 s TTL, single use); `GET /__auth?t=` validates, sets the cookie and redirects; later requests authenticate by cookie; constant-time compares; per-workspace cookie name derived from a SHA-256 of the workspace root (cookies are host-scoped, not port-scoped); asset URLs carry `?s=` so the SameSite=Strict iframe can load them; the port file is trusted only when its token matches the child VMark spawned; Bearer-authed control calls for Slidev
- status: shipped-off, dev-only
- gate: always on whenever the content server runs
- surfaces: automatic (embedded view, "Open in browser", Slidev preview URL)
- code: `server/content/src/server/auth.ts`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/http.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/client.rs`, `src/services/contentServer/client.ts`
- rust: `content_server_browser_url`
- docs: `website/guide/knowledge-base.md` §"Privacy & security"
- tests: `server/content/src/server/auth.test.ts`, `server/content/src/server/createServer.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/http.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/client.test.rs`
- notes: Security refs VULN-001 (token in URL), ADR-9.

### Knowledge-base relationship graph
- id: kb-graph

- summary: A native interactive graph of the workspace's documents, tags and typed frontmatter relations, toggled against the served-site view inside the Knowledge Base dock.
- capabilities: doc and tag nodes; edge kinds `link`, `wikiLink`, `tag`, `relation`; unresolved docs flagged; dagre layout; xyflow pan/zoom and minimap; site/graph toggle with `aria-pressed`; refetch when the workspace root changes; lazy-loaded behind `RetryableLazy`, so a failed chunk retries instead of taking the window down

- docs: `website/guide/knowledge-base.md` §"Knowledge base" (Relationship graph bullet)

- notes: The error boundary sits at the graph, not the panel mount, to avoid the React 19 + xyflow layout-effect loop.

### Slidev preview & export
- id: slidev-export
- feature: Knowledge base & Slidev
- summary: A markdown file detected as a Slidev deck can be previewed live in the browser and exported to PDF, PNG or PPTX by running the real Slidev CLI through the content server.
- capabilities: deck detection from frontmatter (`theme:`, `layout:` + slides, explicit `format: slidev`); "Preview slides" starts a per-deck Slidev dev server behind the proxied `/slidev/` path and opens an authenticating browser URL; saves hot-reload the preview; "Export slides" opens a native save dialog with PDF/PNG/PPTX filters and infers the format from the extension (default name from the deck's final path segment); cancellable export (15-minute Rust timeout, 180 s Node timeout); failures — no deck, cancelled export, unsupported extension — are toasts, never a server error state
- status: shipped-off, dev-only
- gate: needs the content server running for the workspace and a deck as the active tab; export needs `playwright-chromium` available to the content server
- surfaces: Knowledge Base dock "Preview slides" / "Export slides" — no menu item, shortcut or palette command
- code: `src/hooks/useSlidevControls.ts`, `src/services/contentServer/slidevDeck.ts`, `src/services/contentServer/client.ts`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/slidev_commands.rs`, `server/content/src/slidev/detect.ts`, `server/content/src/slidev/manager.ts`, `server/content/src/slidev/server.ts`, `server/content/src/slidev/export.ts`
- rust: `content_server_slidev_preview`, `content_server_slidev_export`
- docs: `website/guide/knowledge-base.md` §"Slidev presentations", §"Export"
- tests: `src/hooks/useSlidevControls.test.ts`, `server/content/src/server/createServer.test.ts`, `src/services/contentServer/slidevDeck.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/content_server/slidev_commands.test.rs`, `server/content/src/slidev/detect.test.ts`, `server/content/src/slidev/manager.test.ts`, `server/content/src/slidev/server.test.ts`, `server/content/src/slidev/export.test.ts`
- notes: `@slidev/cli` ^53.0.0. Nothing in VMark downloads Chromium: the Node server shells out to `slidev export` and returns its stderr (e.g. a missing Chromium); `client.ts` still says export "provisions playwright-chromium on first use (Rust side)". The output path is not contained: `content_server_slidev_export` forwards any `outputPath` the webview sends, and the server checks only that the extension matches the format (`server/content/src/server/createServer.ts`), so a `.pdf`/`.png`/`.pptx` can be written anywhere the user can write.

- notes: Option (b) of D1 would need a runtime story (a bundled resource, or a new install path) to produce these phases; until then they are dead UI states.

## Area 12 — Workflows — GitHub Actions viewer and YAML engine

Verified: `bf8b69ceb`

Two unrelated features share the word "workflow", and the yaml adapter (`src/lib/formats/adapters/yaml.tsx`) tells their files apart. A path under `.github/workflows/`, or YAML with top-level `on:` + `jobs:`, is the `gha-workflow` schema: the always-on GitHub Actions viewer, which lazily mounts `GhaWorkflowWorkbench` (canvas + forms + save) in the split pane and loads four source-pane extensions through `loadExtraExtensions`. YAML that parses, has no top-level `jobs:`, and has a top-level `steps:` list in which a step's own `uses:` names `genie/`, `action/` or `webhook/` is the `vmark-workflow` schema (`src/lib/workflow/detection.ts`; `isWorkflowYaml` in `src/lib/workflow/parser.ts` delegates to it): the bespoke engine's Run/Cancel panel when `advanced.workflowEngine` is on (default off, enforced in Rust by `workflow::guards`), a plain YAML tree when it is off unless that tab's run is still live. Both features share one store, `src/stores/workflowStore.ts`, whose slice transitions live in the sibling `src/stores/workflow*Slice.ts`, `workflowEditQueue.ts` and `workflowSerialize.ts`, each with its own test. Workflow genies — the picker path into `run_workflow` — are described once, in Area 9 (`workflow-genies`).

### GitHub Actions workflow workbench (standalone `.yml`)
- id: gha-workflow-workbench
- feature: GHA workflow viewer
- summary: Opening a `.github/workflows/*.yml` file, or any YAML with top-level `on:` and `jobs:`, gives a split view — YAML source on the left, the job-DAG canvas plus a structured forms editor on the right — with no setting to turn on.
- capabilities: path detection under `.github/workflows/` with either `/` or `\` separators (routes even malformed YAML to the workbench, where the parser's best-effort IR carries `GHA-PARSE-*` diagnostics; the renderer's own parse-failed line is a fallback for a parser that throws); content detection (`on:` + `jobs:` at column 0, then a successful YAML parse); a GitHub Actions path or top-level `jobs:` is never claimed by the engine's `vmark-workflow` schema; `RetryableLazy` boundary around the renderer chunk so a failed chunk load stays in the pane and can be retried; per-pane document binding keyed on filePath (`untitled:<tabId>` fallback) that rebinds on pointer/focus entry and follows a Save As without a remount; module-level per-document save lock; forms-editor chunk loaded the first time a workflow is on screen
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on — the yaml adapter is always registered and its `gha-workflow` schema renderer is unconditional
- surfaces: automatic (`.yml`/`.yaml` routed to the yaml adapter, `kind: "split-pane"`)
- code: `src/lib/formats/adapters/yaml.tsx`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/yamlWorkflowRenderer.tsx`, `src/components/Editor/WorkflowPanel/GhaWorkflowWorkbench.tsx`, `src/lib/ghaWorkflow/detection.ts`, `src/components/Editor/SplitPaneEditor/SourcePane.tsx`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/workflow-viewer.md` §"Standalone workflow files"; `website/guide/formats.md` §"GitHub Actions workflow (`.github/workflows/*.yml`)"; `website/guide/workflows.md` §"Workflow file or GitHub Actions file?"
- tests: `src/lib/formats/adapters/{yaml.test.ts,yamlWorkflowRenderer.test.tsx,yamlWorkflowChunkFailure.test.tsx}`, `src/components/Editor/WorkflowPanel/__tests__/GhaWorkflowWorkbench.test.tsx`, `src/lib/ghaWorkflow/__tests__/detection.test.ts` (Windows separators included); e2e `e2e/journeys/13-workflow-split-pane.mjs`
- notes: The view slice's selection is a singleton, so two workbenches in a document split share one canvas selection.

- capabilities: `@actions/workflow-parser` wrapper; trigger normalization (string / array / mapping, one trigger per cron); permissions normalization; matrix Cartesian × include × exclude with a combination cap; `needs:` edge derivation and cycle detection; per-key source ranges; diagnostic prefixes `GHA-PARSE`/`JOB`/`NEEDS`/`STEP`/`EXPR`/`MATRIX`/`SEC` (plus `GHA-ACTIONLINT` rows appended by `gha-actionlint`)

### Workflow DAG canvas (xyflow)

### Structured workflow forms editor

### CST-preserving workflow save
- id: gha-workflow-cst-save
- feature: GHA workflow viewer
- summary: Save applies the queued form patches through the `yaml` CST so comments, anchors, key order, formatting and CRLF line endings survive, writes the file to disk when it has a path, and clears exactly the patches that were written.
- capabilities: `parseAsCst`/`stringifyCst`; 15 patch kinds (`workflow.set`, `job.set`, `step.set`, `with.set/remove`, `needs.add/remove`, `trigger.setFilters`, `job.create/delete`, `step.insert/delete/move`, `workflow.permissions.set`, `workflow.concurrency.set`); edit queue collapses an earlier patch to the same target but never collapses renumbering patches (step insert/delete/move), and a step edit replaces an earlier edit to the same step, followed back through that job's queued renumberings (one rule shared by queueing and reverting a field); per-document patch stashing with rename on Save As; discriminated serialize result (applied / unchanged / parse-failed / apply-failed / wrong-document) logged when nothing applies; serialization runs on LF-canonical text (`canonicalizeLineEndings`), so a no-op edit on a CRLF file reports `unchanged`, and `saveToPath` writes the document's recorded line ending back; alias-expansion bound on the reformat path; disk write FIRST via `saveToPath`, then editor update only if the user did not type meanwhile; a failed write keeps the queue; untitled documents are updated in the editor only; Discard drops the queue; single-flight per tab; outcome toasts
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `advanced.workflowEditorPreserveYamlFormatting = true` (reformat via `yaml.stringify` is the opt-in); the toggle is shown only when `advanced.developerMode = true` (default false)
- surfaces: Save / Discard controls in the forms header; settings pane (Settings → Advanced → Experimental, developer-gated)
- code: `src/services/workflow/saveGhaWorkflowDocument.ts`, `src/lib/ghaWorkflow/save/{cstParser.ts,mutators.ts}`, `src/stores/{workflowStore.ts,workflowEditQueue.ts,workflowSerialize.ts}`, `src/components/Editor/WorkflowEditor/SaveControls.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/AdvancedSettings.tsx`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/workflow-viewer.md` §"Saving edits", §"Preserving formatting"; `website/guide/settings.md` §"Workflow"
- tests: `src/services/workflow/saveGhaWorkflowDocument.test.ts`, `src/lib/ghaWorkflow/save/__tests__/{cstParser.test.ts,mutators.test.ts,mutators.crud.test.ts,corpusRoundtrip.test.ts}`, `src/stores/{workflowStore.test.ts,workflowEditQueue.test.ts,workflowSerialize.test.ts}`, `src/pages/settings/AdvancedSettings.workflowViewer.test.tsx`
- notes: none

### actionlint integration
- id: gha-actionlint

### MCP `workflow` tool (`apply_patch` / `validate`)
- id: mcp-workflow-tool
- feature: GHA workflow viewer
- summary: Lets an AI client make CST-safe edits to an open workflow tab and run actionlint over it, without a raw rewrite that would drop comments.
- capabilities: `apply_patch` accepts 8 kinds (`workflow.set`, `job.set`, `step.set`, `with.set`, `with.remove`, `needs.add`, `needs.remove`, `trigger.setFilters`); optimistic concurrency via `expected_revision` → `STALE` with `current_revision`; `NOT_WORKFLOW` for tabs failing path/content detection; `INVALID_TAB`; `INVALID_PATCH`; a no-op batch bumps no revision and pushes no checkpoint; checkpoint with a one-line batch description; `validate` returns `{ok, diagnostics, binaryAvailable, error}` and short-circuits when `advanced.workflowActionlint` is off
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on (not gated by `advanced.workflowEngine`)
- surfaces: MCP operations `vmark.workflow.apply_patch`, `vmark.workflow.validate`
- code: `src/services/mcpBridge/v2/workflow.ts`, `src/services/mcpBridge/v2/dispatch.ts`, `server/mcp/src/tools/workflow.ts`, `server/mcp/src/bridge/operationSchemas.ts`

- docs: `website/guide/mcp-tools.md` §"`workflow`"
- tests: `src/services/mcpBridge/v2/__tests__/workflow.test.ts`, `server/mcp/__tests__/unit/tools/workflow.test.ts`
- notes: The structural kinds the forms use (`job.create/delete`, `step.*`, `workflow.permissions.set`, `workflow.concurrency.set`) are deliberately not exposed. `apply_patch` always uses the CST path, regardless of `advanced.workflowEditorPreserveYamlFormatting`.

### Bespoke YAML workflow engine (Run / Cancel)
- id: workflow-engine
- feature: Workflow engine (bespoke YAML)
- summary: VMark's own YAML workflow language — an ordered graph of genie and built-in action steps executed by a Rust runner. With the engine on, a workflow `.yml` opens in the split view with its step graph under a Run / Cancel toolbar, and each run belongs to the tab that started it.
- capabilities: engine-file detection (top-level `steps:` whose `uses:` names `genie/`, `action/` or `webhook/`, no top-level `jobs:`, not under `.github/workflows/`; read from the parsed document, counting only a step's own `uses:`, so malformed YAML is never claimed and opens as the plain YAML tree); yaml adapter `vmark-workflow` schema renderer — the run panel with the engine on, the plain YAML tree with it off, swapped live when the setting flips, chunk fetched only when on; in the split pane a tab whose run is live keeps the panel and its Cancel after the engine is switched off or the file stops parsing; the same panel as a resizable side panel in the markdown surface when the markdown Source editor edits a YAML file (parses the file it opens with); Run executes the tab's live document content; a panel's run is registered with its owning tab in one store write before `run_workflow` resolves (`setExecution(id, owner)` → `preview.runTabId`, through `src/services/workflow/dispatchWorkflowRun.ts`) — only the owning panel paints statuses (and only while the tab still holds the text that ran, `preview.runSource`) and offers Cancel, enabled once the run is registered; other panels in the window show "busy"; toolbar status (starting, running, needs a workspace, busy, completed / failed / cancelled); a refused start is shown as a toast with the typed error and gives back the previous run's outcome and restore offer; a refused cancel other than `not-found` is shown as a toast; topological sort on `needs:`; one run at a time app-wide (managed `WorkflowRunnerState`, claim and publish under an admission lock); caller-supplied, validated execution id; a workspace root containing the app-data workspace-grant list is refused before admission and again on the admitted canonical root; soft cancel matched against the running id; `webhook/*` rejected before the run; live per-step status via `workflow:step-update` / `workflow:complete` / `workflow:approval-request`, routed by the window's one subscription (`src/services/workflow/workflowRunEvents.ts`, held by the approval dialog) that every start waits for; completion keeps the step results and the outcome; bundled sample `triage-and-translate.yml`
- status: shipped-off
- gate: `advanced.workflowEngine = false`; enforced in Rust by `require_workflow_engine_enabled` on `run_workflow`, and in the UI by the yaml adapter's `vmark-workflow` wrapper and `WorkflowEngineSlot`; the toggle is visible only when `advanced.developerMode = true` (default false)
- surfaces: split-pane preview of a workflow `.yml` (toolbar Run ▶ / Cancel ◼ / Restore Files); markdown-surface side panel (`WorkflowSidePanel`); settings pane (Settings → Advanced → Experimental, developer-gated); no menu id, shortcut or palette entry; genie picker via Area 9 `workflow-genies`
- code: `src/lib/workflow/detection.ts`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/{yaml.tsx,yamlEngineRenderer.tsx,markdownSurface.tsx}`, `src/components/Editor/WorkflowPanel/{WorkflowRunPanel.tsx,useWorkflowRunControls.ts,WorkflowEngineSlot.tsx,WorkflowSidePanel.tsx}`, `src/hooks/useWorkflowExecution.ts`, `src/services/workflow/{dispatchWorkflowRun.ts,workflowRunEvents.ts,providerPayload.ts}`, `src/stores/{workflowStore.ts,workflowPreviewSlice.ts}`, `src/lib/workflow/{parser.ts,layout.ts}`, `src/plugins/workflowPreview/{WorkflowPreview.tsx,WorkflowNode.tsx}`, `src/plugins/codemirror/sourceWorkflowPreview.ts`, `src-tauri/resources/workflows/examples/triage-and-translate.yml`
- rust: `run_workflow`, `cancel_workflow` (`src-tauri/src/workflow/commands.rs`); `src-tauri/src/workflow/{runner.rs,state.rs,state_cancel.rs,launch.rs,prepare.rs,validate.rs,recent_ids.rs,types.rs}`
- docs: `website/guide/workflows.md` §"Workflow file or GitHub Actions file?", §"Running a workflow", §"Execution flow"; `website/guide/settings.md` §"Workflow"
- tests: `src/lib/workflow/__tests__/detection.test.ts`, `src/lib/formats/adapters/{yaml.test.ts,yamlEngineRenderer.test.tsx}`, `src/components/Editor/WorkflowPanel/__tests__/{WorkflowRunPanel.test.tsx,useWorkflowRunControls.test.tsx,WorkflowSidePanel.test.tsx}`, `src/components/Editor/WorkflowPanel/WorkflowEngineSlot.test.tsx`, `src/plugins/codemirror/sourceWorkflowPreview.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useWorkflowExecution.test.tsx`, `src/stores/{workflowStore.test.ts,workflowPreviewSlice.test.ts}`, `src/services/workflow/{dispatchWorkflowRun.test.ts,workflowRunEvents.test.ts,providerPayload.test.ts}`, `src/plugins/workflowPreview/__tests__/{WorkflowPreview.test.tsx,WorkflowNode.test.tsx}`, `src/lib/workflow/__tests__/{parser.test.ts,parser.edges.test.ts,layout.test.ts}`; Rust `src-tauri/src/workflow/{runner.test.rs,state.test.rs,commands.test.rs,launch.test.rs,prepare.test.rs,examples.test.rs}`; no e2e journey; the run-panel tests assert raw `workflow:` keys because the test i18n mock does not load that namespace
- notes: "Busy" is known only inside the window whose store holds the running id — a workflow file in another window keeps Run enabled, and the backend refuses the start with a toast. Rule 60 §12's 2026-09-27 re-verdict keeps the engine in every build behind the setting, so `pub mod workflow` and `.manage(WorkflowRunnerState::default())` in `src-tauri/src/lib.rs` are unconditional by design.

### Engine backend gate (`workflow_engine_policy`)
- id: workflow-engine-backend-gate
- feature: Workflow engine (bespoke YAML)
- summary: Not user-facing: the engine flag is pushed from the webview to Rust so a UI-less caller (MCP bridge, another window, a replayed invoke) cannot start a workflow the user switched off.
- capabilities: Rust state starts fail-closed; pushed at bootstrap and on every change; pushes serialized through a queue; a failed disable retried 3× (250/500 ms backoff) then toasted, a failed enable only warned; the `false` transition cancels an in-flight run under the same admission lock as `run_workflow`, matched on the published execution id, so a snapshot restore (which holds the runner flag but publishes none) is not cancelled; refusal is `feature-disabled` with `errors.workflow.engineDisabled`; a persisted `advanced.workflowViewer` is dropped by migration
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on (the setter is ungated — it is the gate's setter)
- surfaces: none (started by `src/services/runtimeWiring.ts`)
- code: `src/services/workflow/workflowEnginePolicySync.ts`, `src/services/featureFlags/workflowFeatureFlag.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore/migrations.ts`
- rust: `workflow_engine_policy` (`src-tauri/src/workflow/commands.rs`); `src-tauri/src/workflow/{guards.rs,state.rs}`
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"Workflow"
- tests: `src/services/workflow/workflowEnginePolicySync.test.ts`, `src/services/featureFlags/workflowFeatureFlag.test.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore/migrations.test.ts`; Rust `src-tauri/src/workflow/{guards.test.rs,state.test.rs}`
- notes: Only `run_workflow` is refused when off; `cancel_workflow`, `respond_workflow_approval`, `list_workflow_snapshots` and `restore_workflow_snapshot` stay callable so a user can stop or undo a run after switching the engine off. Rule 60 §12 records why the unauthenticated boolean setter is intended.

### Workflow approvals
- id: workflow-approvals
- feature: Workflow engine (bespoke YAML)
- summary: A step with `approval: ask` pauses the run and shows a modal with the step id, resolved model and a 500-character prompt preview; Approve runs it, Deny or Esc fails it.
- capabilities: Rust registry keyed `(execution_id, step_id)` over oneshot channels; wait bounded by min(step timeout, 10 minutes); timeout → `Approval timed out`, deny → `Approval denied by user`; dialog dismisses only on a successful verdict; re-entrancy guard; browser-view occluder while open; the dialog holds the window's one workflow-event subscription (`src/services/workflow/workflowRunEvents.ts`), and events are matched to the window's current execution id; a run's completion dismisses its pending approval
- status: shipped-off
- gate: `advanced.workflowEngine = false` gates the run that raises the prompt; `respond_workflow_approval` is ungated
- surfaces: modal dialog mounted unconditionally in `src/App.tsx`
- code: `src/components/WorkflowApproval/ApprovalDialog.tsx`, `src/stores/{workflowStore.ts,workflowApprovalSlice.ts}`, `src/hooks/useWorkflowExecution.ts`, `src/services/workflow/workflowRunEvents.ts`
- rust: `respond_workflow_approval` (`src-tauri/src/workflow/commands.rs`); `src-tauri/src/workflow/{approval.rs,step_config.rs}`
- docs: `website/guide/workflows.md` §"Approvals"
- tests: `src/components/WorkflowApproval/{ApprovalDialog.test.tsx,ApprovalDialog.a11y.test.tsx}`, `src/stores/workflowApprovalSlice.test.ts`, `src/services/workflow/workflowRunEvents.test.ts` (approval queued for the current run, dismissed on completion); 6 inline tests in `src-tauri/src/workflow/approval.rs`
- notes: Approval precedence is step → genie metadata → workflow `defaults` → `auto`.

### Workflow step types (built-in actions + genie steps)
- id: workflow-step-types
- feature: Workflow engine (bespoke YAML)
- summary: Each step is a markdown genie call or one of five built-in actions, all sandboxed to the workspace root.
- capabilities: `genie/<name>` (template fill with `with:` aliases, provider call, `text`/`json` output validation); `action/read-file`, `action/read-folder` (≤1000 files, 10 MB/file, 100 MB total, optional `with.accept` filter), `action/save-file`, `action/notify`, `action/copy`; one required-parameter table shared by runner and sample check; bounded reads from the open handle; a `save-file` `with.path` containing an expression is refused at admission so the pre-run snapshot knows its target; atomic save committed into a directory descriptor proved inside the workspace (Unix), parents created the same way; path sandbox rejecting absolute paths, `..` and escaping symlinks; untrusted-content nonce fencing; single-pass template substitution; unbound placeholders fatal before the provider call
- status: shipped-off, partial
- gate: `advanced.workflowEngine = false`
- surfaces: automatic (inside a run)
- code: none frontend
- rust: `src-tauri/src/workflow/{actions.rs,actions_folder.rs,commit.rs,commit_dir.rs,dir_fd.rs,ensure_dir.rs,genie_step.rs,template.rs,untrusted.rs,sandbox.rs,prepare.rs}`
- docs: `website/guide/workflows.md` §"Step types", §"Genie steps and `with:` aliasing", §"Structured outputs"
- tests: `src-tauri/src/workflow/{actions.test.rs,actions_folder.test.rs,commit.test.rs,ensure_dir.test.rs,prepare.test.rs}`; inline tests in `template.rs`, `untrusted.rs`, `sandbox.rs`, `genie_step.rs`
- notes: Partial — `webhook/*` is rejected; genie `output.type` `file`/`files`/`pipe` unsupported. Off Unix, save and directory creation fall back to path-based calls with a stated TOCTOU residual, and no Windows test runs. `untrusted.rs` calls the fencing defense in depth, not a hermetic boundary.

### Workflow expressions, conditions, and per-step settings
- id: workflow-expressions-conditions
- feature: Workflow engine (bespoke YAML)
- summary: `with:` values interpolate earlier step outputs and env vars; `if:` conditions skip a step; `model`/`approval`/`limits` resolve down a precedence chain.
- capabilities: `${{ steps.ID.outputs.FIELD }}`, `${{ steps.ID.output }}`, `${{ env.NAME }}`, legacy `${VAR}` and bare `stepId.output`; unknown refs fatal; condition grammar with `success()`/`failure()`/`always()`, literals, comparisons, `&&`/`||`/`!`/parens, capped depth; unparseable condition fails the step; timeout step → defaults → 300 s; 5 MB cap on one step's output; `max_tokens` enforced for REST providers only
- status: shipped-off
- gate: `advanced.workflowEngine = false`
- surfaces: automatic (inside a run)
- code: none frontend
- rust: `src-tauri/src/workflow/{expressions.rs,condition.rs,condition_lexer.rs,condition_status.rs,run_context.rs,step_preflight.rs,step_config.rs}`
- docs: `website/guide/workflows.md` §"Wiring steps together: expressions", §"Conditions", §"Per-step settings", §"Timeouts"
- tests: `src-tauri/src/workflow/condition.test.rs` (45), `src-tauri/src/workflow/expressions.test.rs` (25), `src-tauri/src/workflow/runner_flow.test.rs` (21 — status functions across `needs`, timeouts and cancel), `src-tauri/src/workflow/step_preflight.test.rs` (5); inline tests in `step_config.rs` (21)
- notes: GitHub Actions rules: `success()` is implied when an `if:` names no status function, and is false when a needed step did not complete; `failure()` is true once any earlier step failed; `always()` and `failure()` steps run after a needed step failed. A cancel is checked before the `if:` and skips every remaining step, `always()` included. A run with a failed step still ends failed, naming the first one.

### Workflow file snapshots and Restore Files
- id: workflow-snapshots
- feature: Workflow engine (bespoke YAML)
- summary: Before a run with `action/save-file` steps, the target files are snapshotted (a run whose snapshot fails is refused); after the run, the tab's run panel offers Restore Files, which puts the snapshotted files back and deletes the files the run created.
- capabilities: full relative paths preserved; 64 MB per file and 256 MB per snapshot; `MAX_SNAPSHOTS = 50` with pruning; id restricted to `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, ≤64 chars; a save target that cannot be snapshotted refuses the run; bounded, cancellable copy; files absent before the run recorded as created; the canonical workspace root recorded with the snapshot; `list_workflow_snapshots` (newest first) and `restore_workflow_snapshot` (id only — the root comes from the snapshot's record); restore holds the recorded root open for its whole length and writes through the same anchored commit as `save-file` (Unix); snapshot metadata is read bounded (1 MB) and must name its own snapshot; recorded paths that are relative, carry `..` or lie outside the root are skipped; a created file that is now a symlink or directory is not deleted, and a deletion unlinks through the parent's descriptor (Unix); per-file failures counted (restored / deleted / skipped), not fatal; restore holds the runner's `running` flag, so it is refused (`conflict`) during a run and a run is refused during a restore, but publishes no execution id, so switching the engine off does not cancel it; a snapshot a later run has superseded is refused (`conflict`, checked under the claim); a snapshot without a recorded root (older version) is refused; one restore at a time from the click; confirmation dialog naming the counts and that later edits are lost, the choice re-checked against the store afterwards (a superseded restore is refused with a toast); success or partial toast; offer withdrawn after a complete restore, kept after a partial one; a failed snapshot listing retried once, then reported
- status: shipped-off
- gate: `advanced.workflowEngine = false` gates the runs that take snapshots; `list_workflow_snapshots` / `restore_workflow_snapshot` are ungated
- surfaces: Restore Files button in the run panel toolbar once the owning tab's run has ended
- code: `src/components/Editor/WorkflowPanel/{useRunSnapshot.ts,workflowSnapshots.ts,WorkflowRunPanel.tsx}`
- rust: `list_workflow_snapshots`, `restore_workflow_snapshot` (`src-tauri/src/workflow/snapshot_commands.rs`); `src-tauri/src/workflow/{snapshots.rs,snapshot_copy.rs,snapshot_restore.rs,snapshot_write.rs,state_restore.rs,prepare.rs}`
- docs: `website/guide/workflows.md` §"Undoing a run"
- tests: `src/components/Editor/WorkflowPanel/__tests__/{useRunSnapshot.test.tsx,workflowSnapshots.test.ts,WorkflowRunPanel.test.tsx}`; Rust `src-tauri/src/workflow/{snapshots.test.rs,snapshot_restore.test.rs,snapshot_write.test.rs,snapshot_commands.test.rs,prepare.test.rs}`; 4 inline tests in `snapshot_copy.rs`
- notes: The UI finds only the snapshot of the run the tab just finished (`lastExecutionId` in the window's store): after a reload, a restart or from another window there is no way to reach an older snapshot, though up to 50 stay on disk. On Unix the restore opens the recorded root without following a link and writes and deletes through descriptors proved inside it (`src-tauri/src/workflow/snapshot_write.rs`); off Unix both are path-based — deletion is `symlink_metadata` plus a canonical-parent check, then `remove_file` — with the residual stated in that file.

### Coherence capture for workflow file writes
- id: workflow-coherence-capture
- feature: Workflow engine (bespoke YAML)
- summary: Not user-facing: an `action/save-file` write records a provenance edge in the coherence kernel, with inputs found by walking template references back to the `action/read-file` steps that fed it — as far as the capture-on-save setting allows.
- capabilities: transitive dataflow walk over `${{ steps.X… }}` and bare `X.output`; `steps.X` counted only inside `${{ … }}`; reachable reads become direct inputs; the run carries the webview's `general.coherenceCaptureOnSave` as `capture_policy` (`adopt` / `tracked-only`; absent = `tracked-only`) and captures go through `capture_with_policy` — off with no ledger records nothing and creates no `.vmark/`, off with a ledger records only documents it tracks (a registered path, or a file that carries its own `vmark:` id) without stamping; failures log and never fail the step
- status: shipped-off
- gate: `advanced.workflowEngine = false`; `general.coherenceCaptureOnSave = false` limits what is recorded
- surfaces: automatic (inside a run); results surface through the coherence feature
- code: `src/services/workflow/dispatchWorkflowRun.ts`, `src/services/coherence/capturePolicy.ts`
- rust: `src-tauri/src/workflow/{coherence_capture.rs,commands.rs,runner.rs}`, `src-tauri/src/coherence/capture_policy.rs`
- docs: `website/guide/workflows.md` §"Step types" (the paragraph after the table); `website/guide/settings.md` §"Saving" (Stamp identity block on save)
- tests: `src-tauri/src/workflow/{coherence_capture.test.rs,commands.test.rs}`, `src/hooks/useWorkflowExecution.test.tsx`, `src/services/workflow/dispatchWorkflowRun.test.ts`
- notes: In-process, no IPC; the policy is an argument the admitted run carries from `run_workflow` through `run_workflow_sequential` to each capture, not shared runner state.

## Area 13 — Settings, appearance, localization, shortcuts, menus, palette, updates, shell

Verified: `bf8b69ceb`

The Settings window is its own route (`src/pages/Settings.tsx`, panes under `src/pages/settings/`); the document window is composed in `src/App.tsx` on the `src/shell/AppShell.tsx` layout primitive; the native menu is built in Rust under `src-tauri/src/menu/` and routed back through `src/services/commands/menuListener.ts` into the CommandBus.

### Settings window
- id: settings-window
- feature: Settings UI & persistence
- summary: A separate singleton Tauri window (route `/settings`) with a leading nav card, per-section panes and a global search field. It can be opened deep-linked to a section, and on macOS it uses the same overlay-titlebar chrome as the document window.
- capabilities: singleton create-or-focus (unminimize, show, focus), with idempotent creation so two rapid opens focus one window instead of failing; 760×680 default size, 600×400 minimum; `?section=` URL param percent-encoded in Rust and validated in the page against an 11-id allowlist (unknown → Appearance); `settings:navigate` event jumps sections when the window already exists (status bar → Integrations, update toast → About, formats nudge → Formats, genie provider switcher → Integrations); Cmd+W closes it through `menu:close` filtered on `payload === "settings"`; own theme application (`useTheme`); two-way live sync of settings, shortcuts, AI providers and update state with document windows; macOS-only centred window title and drag regions laid out from `shellChromeVars`; off macOS the window is built with an empty native menu and its final geometry up front; falls back to Appearance during render when Advanced is hidden while on screen
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: menu id `preferences` (App menu on macOS, File menu elsewhere, handled in Rust); shortcut `preferences` = `Mod-,`; command `app.preferences` (command palette); `openSettingsWindow(section)` callers in the status bar, update toast, formats nudge and genie provider switcher
- code: `src/pages/Settings.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/panels.ts`, `src/pages/settings/SettingsNav.tsx`, `src/services/navigation/settingsWindow.ts`, `src/App.tsx`, `src/shell/shellChrome.ts`, `src-tauri/src/window_manager/settings_window.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/events/dispatch.rs`, `src-tauri/capabilities/settings.json`
- rust: `window_manager::open_settings_window` (`#[tauri::command(async)]`, emits `settings:navigate`); `show_settings_window_section`; `menu::events::dispatch::handle_preferences`
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` (page intro)
- tests: `src/pages/Settings.devSection.test.tsx` (drives `?section=` and the render-time fallback), `src/pages/settings/SettingsNav.test.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/SettingsSearch.test.tsx`, `src-tauri/src/window_manager/settings_window.test.rs` (URL encoding, second open focuses, lost build race reports success)
- notes: creation is deliberately NOT mutex-guarded — the non-macOS branch calls `Menu::new`, which blocks on the main thread, so a lock held across it could deadlock against the menu's own Preferences handler (`settings_window.rs` header). The window's capability (`src-tauri/capabilities/settings.json`) calls itself "UI-only, no filesystem" but grants `store:default`; tauri-plugin-store 2.4.5 resolves a webview-supplied store path by joining it onto the app data dir, so an absolute path escapes it and `save` writes a JSON object there.

### Cross-window settings sync
- id: settings-cross-window-sync
- feature: Settings UI & persistence
- summary: A setting changed in one window (including the Settings window) reaches every other window live through localStorage `storage` events, and a write carries only the sections the writing window changed.
- capabilities: `SYNC_GROUPS` derived from `initialState` (every object-valued section; `showDevSection` excluded by construction); per-group shape check before adoption; inbound groups passed through the same `reconcileSettings` boundary hydration uses; outbound writes wrapped by `createSectionMergingStorage` so a window writes only the sections it changed and drops echo writes; shortcut rebinds sync separately via `vmark-shortcuts` (`src/hooks/useShortcutsSync.ts`, `setState` with per-value string validation); AI providers sync over a Tauri event because that store persists to a JSON file
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic
- code: `src/hooks/useSettingsSync.ts`, `src/stores/persistedSectionMerge.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore/reconcile.ts`, `src/hooks/useShortcutsSync.ts`, `src/hooks/useAiProviderSync.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: none
- tests: `src/hooks/useSettingsSync.test.ts`, `src/stores/persistedSectionMerge.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useShortcutsSync.test.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/crossWindowPropagation.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useAiProviderSync.test.ts`
- notes: deliberate tradeoff — two windows changing the SAME section between reads: the later write wins that section.

### Settings persistence, guards and migrations
- id: settings-persistence
- feature: Settings UI & persistence
- summary: Settings live in localStorage key `vmark-settings` (persist schema version 1) and pass one zero-trust boundary — migrate, shape-sanitize, deep-merge, clamp, normalize — on hydration and on every cross-window adoption.
- capabilities: one ordered migration pipeline `PERSISTED_SETTINGS_MIGRATIONS`, run on the raw blob before sanitizing — (1) `appearance.paragraphSpacing → blockSpacing`, (2) `advanced.workspaceRailMode → general.workspaceRailMode`, (3) remove `terminal.inputGate`, (4) remove `appearance.autoHideStatusBar`, (5) remove `advanced.mcpServer.port`, (6) remove `advanced.workflowViewer`; a v2+ blob (after a downgrade) is dropped rather than merged; `sanitizePersistedSettings` validates leaf types against live defaults (finite numbers only, `null` defaults accept anything, unknown keys pass through); `normalizeBrowserSettings` coerces `aiSession` to `sandbox` and `aiAllowLoopback` to `false` when invalid; `CLAMP_RANGES` — `appearance.fontSize [8,48]`, `lineHeight [1,3]`, `blockSpacing [0,3]`, `editorWidth [0,200]`; `terminal.fontSize [8,32]`, `lineHeight [1,2.5]`, `scrollback [100,200000]`, `panelRatio [0.1,0.8]`, `minimumContrastRatio [1,21]`; `general.autoSaveInterval [5,3600]`, `historyMaxSnapshots [1,1000]`, `historyMaxAgeDays [0,3650]`, `historyMergeWindow [0,3600]`, `historyMaxFileSize [0,1048576]`, `tabSize [1,8]`, `coherenceCheckTau [0.5,1]` — applied on every programmatic set and at the persist boundary; hydration-only union of `advanced.customLinkProtocols` with shipped defaults, dropping non-string entries; live state is a `structuredClone` of `initialState`
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic
- code: `src/stores/settingsStore.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore/migrations.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore/persistGuards.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore/reconcile.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore/clamp.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore/defaults.ts`, `src/stores/settingsTypes/`
- rust: none
- docs: none
- tests: `src/stores/settingsStore/migrations.test.ts` (pins every exported `migrate*` into the pipeline), `src/stores/settingsStore/defaults.test.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore.test.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore.browser.test.ts`, `src/stores/__tests__/settingsStore.clamp.test.ts`, `src/stores/__tests__/settingsStore.fault.test.ts`, `src/stores/__tests__/settingsStore.i18n.test.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore/__tests__/unusedSettings.test.ts` (every settings leaf must have a production read site)
- notes: `settingsStore.ts` header lists its known limitations — no per-document or per-workspace overrides; `resetSettings()` has no per-section form.
