### Theme catalog
- id: theme-catalog
- feature: Themes, fonts & layout
- summary: Six built-in themes — White, Paper, Mint, Sepia (light) and Night, Solarized (dark) — defined as typed `ThemeTokens` and written to the page as CSS custom properties at runtime. Windows and Linux offer only White and Night.
- capabilities: `themes` barrel is the single source of `ThemeId`; per-theme `isDark` drives the `.dark-theme`/`.dark` root classes; `applyTheme` flattens tokens to `--*` CSS vars, memoized per theme object, with the `terminal` subtree left out (xterm reads it from JS); 16-role syntax palette emitted as `--syntax-*`; xterm theme built per theme (`buildXtermTheme`); neutral White/Night terminal theme while a browser tab is focused (`terminalThemeForBrowser`); legacy `--bg-color`-style variables derived from the catalog (`legacyModeColors`); off macOS the picker lists only `white` + `night`, and a stored theme is mapped to the one of the same polarity (Sepia becomes White, not Night)
- status: shipped-on, windows-linux-gap
- gate: always on; `selectableThemeIds(isMac)` / `coerceThemeId` narrow the set off macOS; `appearance.theme = "paper"`
- surfaces: settings pane `appearance` swatches; automatic at startup
- code: `src/theme/themes/index.ts`, `src/theme/themeAvailability.ts`, `src/theme/applyTheme.ts`, `src/theme/tokens.ts`, `src/theme/palettes.ts`, `src/theme/legacyModeColors.ts`, `src/theme/buildXtermTheme.ts`, `src/theme/terminalThemeForBrowser.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"Theme" (with the "Windows and Linux offer White and Night only" callout)
- tests: `src/theme/applyTheme.test.ts`, `src/theme/cssVars.test.ts`, `src/theme/emittedCssVars.regression.test.ts`, `src/theme/buildXtermTheme.test.ts`, `src/theme/buildXtermTheme.regression.test.ts`, `src/theme/themeAvailability.test.ts`, `src/theme/terminalThemeForBrowser.test.ts`, `src/theme/legacyModeColors.test.ts`, `src/theme/syntaxStaticFallback.test.ts`, `src/theme/themes/__acceptance__/contrast.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.test.ts`
- notes: `src/theme/themes/__acceptance__/highContrast.spike.ts` is an acceptance-test artifact and is not in the catalog. It cannot be selected.

### Follow-system light/dark
- id: follow-system-appearance
- feature: Themes, fonts & layout
- summary: When enabled, VMark switches between a chosen light theme and a chosen dark theme as the OS appearance flips.
- capabilities: `matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)")` watcher with legacy `addListener` fallback and a no-op when matchMedia is absent; non-persisted `systemAppearanceStore` seeded at module init so the first paint is already correct; pure `resolveEffectiveThemeId` plus platform narrowing; paired light/dark rows (`systemLightTheme` default Paper, `systemDarkTheme` default Night) replace the single theme row; `appearance.theme` is never written by an OS flip, so the manual choice comes back when the option is turned off; every themed window (document, Settings, PDF export) tracks it through `useTheme`
- status: shipped-off
- gate: `appearance.followSystemAppearance = false`
- surfaces: settings pane `appearance`; automatic
- code: `src/hooks/useEffectiveTheme.ts`, `src/hooks/useSystemAppearanceWatcher.ts`, `src/stores/systemAppearanceStore.ts`, `src/hooks/useIsDarkTheme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"Follow system appearance"
- tests: `src/hooks/useEffectiveTheme.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useSystemAppearanceWatcher.test.ts`, `src/stores/systemAppearanceStore.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useIsDarkTheme.test.ts`
- notes: `#1125`.

### Native chrome theming
- id: native-chrome-theme
- feature: Themes, fonts & layout
- summary: The resolved light/dark state is reported to Rust so that OS-drawn title bars (and the Windows menu bar) match the in-app theme on Windows and Linux.
- capabilities: `syncNativeTheme(isDark)` sends only a real flip across IPC and records delivery only on success, so a failure is retried on the next change; failures are swallowed so the app's own theming never breaks; Rust remembers the preference so windows opened later start with the right chrome; macOS is a deliberate no-op
- status: shipped-on, windows-linux-gap
- gate: always on; `#[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]` does the work
- surfaces: automatic (from the `useTheme` effect)
- code: `src/services/theme/nativeTheme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src-tauri/src/window_manager/native_theme.rs`
- rust: `set_native_theme` (`src-tauri/src/window_manager/native_theme.rs`, still `Result<(), String>`)
- docs: none
- tests: `src/services/theme/nativeTheme.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/window_manager/native_theme.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/window_manager/mod.test.rs`
- notes: Known limitation recorded in `native_theme.rs`: the Windows menu bar goes dark only when the OS-wide app dark mode is on (muda gates on `should_apps_use_dark_mode()`), while the title bar always follows. The preference lives in a process-global `AtomicBool`, and the module header justifies the exception to the `.manage()` rule.

### Editor typography and fonts
- id: typography-fonts
- feature: Themes, fonts & layout
- summary: Latin, CJK and monospace font pickers — each with a curated shortlist, every installed family, and a typed custom family — plus font size, line height, block spacing, CJK letter spacing and editor width, all applied live as CSS variables.
- capabilities: curated shortlists in one table shared with the PDF export sidebar — Latin (System Default, Athelas, Palatino, Georgia, Charter, Literata), CJK (System Default, PingFang SC, Songti SC, Kaiti SC, Noto Serif CJK, Source Han Sans), mono (System Default + 15: SF Mono, Monaco, Menlo, Consolas, DejaVu Sans Mono, Liberation Mono, Ubuntu Mono, Noto Sans Mono, Noto Sans Mono CJK SC, JetBrains Mono, Fira Code, SauceCodePro NFM, IBM Plex Mono, Hack, Inconsolata); an **Installed Fonts** group listing every family on the machine (macOS only, via `NSFontManager`; Windows and Linux get an empty list, which is not treated as an error); **Custom…** reveals a family-name field with `<datalist>` autocompletion that applies as soon as the name passes validation, keeps a rejected draft local and says why it was rejected, and never commits the "Custom…" choice on its own; custom values are stored as `custom:<family>` in the same setting, so `useTheme`, PDF export and the terminal's mono sync all pick them up; `sanitizeCustomFontFamily` refuses quotes, backslashes, `;{}()`, comment markers, commas and control/format characters, and caps the name at 64 characters, and enumerated names pass through the same check; the current value always appears in the list, so a select never renders blank; font size 14/16/18/20/22 (default 18); line height 1.4–2.2 (default 1.8); block spacing 0.5/1/1.5/2 (default 1); CJK letter spacing off–0.12em (default off); editor width 36/42/50/60/80em or unlimited (default 50); View zoom in/out/actual step font size by 2 within 12–32 (store clamp 8–48); `--font-mono` measured before it is written (see block `verified-mono-stack`, area 14)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `appearance.latinFont = "system"`, `appearance.cjkFont = "system"`, `appearance.monoFont = "system"`, `appearance.fontSize = 18`, `appearance.lineHeight = 1.8`, `appearance.blockSpacing = 1`, `appearance.cjkLetterSpacing = "0"`, `appearance.editorWidth = 50`
- surfaces: settings pane `editor` §Typography and §Display; zoom shortcuts `zoomIn` / `zoomOut` / `zoomActual` (commands `view.zoomIn`, `view.zoomOut`, `view.zoomActual`)
- code: `src/pages/settings/FontSettings.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/FontSettingRow.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/EditorSettings.tsx`, `src/utils/fontOptions.ts`, `src/utils/customFont.ts`, `src/utils/fontStacks.ts`, `src/services/fonts/systemFonts.ts`, `src/hooks/useSystemFontFamilies.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/services/commands/viewZoomCommands.ts`
- rust: `list_system_font_families` (`src-tauri/src/system_fonts.rs` — async, hops to the main thread on macOS; `#[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]` returns an empty list)
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"Typography", §"Using a font you installed yourself", §"Display"
- tests: `src/pages/settings/FontSettings.test.tsx`, `src/utils/customFont.test.ts`, `src/utils/fontStacks.test.ts`, `src/services/fonts/systemFonts.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useSystemFontFamilies.test.tsx`, `src-tauri/src/system_fonts.test.rs`, `src/hooks/useTheme.test.ts`, `src/services/commands/viewZoomCommands.test.ts`
- notes: `#1429`. Installed-family enumeration is macOS-only by design (DirectWrite and fontconfig paths are unimplemented), and the docs state this. Font labels are deliberately untranslated (proper nouns). The `@coordinates-with` headers in `src/pages/settings/FontSettingRow.tsx` and `src/services/fonts/systemFonts.ts` still point at `utils/fontStacks.ts` for the validator, which lives in `src/utils/customFont.ts`.

### Reduced-motion policy
- id: reduced-motion
- feature: Themes, fonts & layout
- summary: VMark honours the OS "reduce motion" preference. CSS animation collapses globally, and JS-driven smooth scrolling falls back to instant.
- capabilities: `prefersReducedMotion()` (false without matchMedia); `scrollBehavior()` returns `auto` under reduced motion and `smooth` otherwise; a global `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` block in `src/styles/index.css`; an ESLint `no-restricted-syntax` rule forbids a literal `behavior: "smooth"` outside the module; used by typewriter mode (both editors), AI suggestions, footnote popups and the inline TOC
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on (follows the OS preference)
- surfaces: automatic
- code: `src/utils/motion.ts`, `src/styles/index.css`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/features.md` §"Editing Details" (Reduced motion)
- tests: `src/utils/motion.test.ts`
- notes: OS setting only; there is no in-app reduced-motion setting.

### Localization (i18n)
- id: localization
- feature: Localization (10 languages)
- summary: The webview UI and the native menus ship in 10 languages: en, zh-CN, zh-TW, ja, ko, es, fr, de, it, pt-BR.
- capabilities: 14 webview namespaces per locale (`ai, breakdown, claims, commands, common, dialog, editor, export, menu, settings, sidebar, statusbar, workflow, workflowEditor`); lazy per-namespace loading via `import.meta.glob` + `i18next-resources-to-backend`; boot-critical namespaces `common, statusbar, commands, dialog` loaded synchronously (`initAsync: false`); fallback `zh-TW → zh-CN → en`, `pt-BR → en`, otherwise `en`; `load: "currentOnly"`; `document.documentElement.lang` set at init and on `languageChanged`; first-run language derived from the OS locale list (`resolveInitialLanguage`: `zh-Hans`/`zh-Hant`, regions `tw`/`hk`/`mo` → zh-TW, any `pt` → pt-BR, else `en`); flat-key bundles only (nested objects fail a test); Rust side has parallel `src-tauri/locales/*.yml` bundles (about 440 lines each, including the `workspaceAccess` folder-picker titles and the workflow-snapshot and workspace-grant error keys added on this branch) for `t!()` menu labels and localized `CommandError` messages; a startup native-menu rebuild runs when the saved language is not English; `pnpm lint:i18n` checks key parity, untranslated values (baseline empty) and copy conventions
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `general.language = resolveInitialLanguage()` (OS-derived)
- surfaces: settings pane `language`; automatic at startup
- code: `src/i18n.ts`, `src/utils/localeDetect.ts`, `src/locales/en/common.json`, `src/services/menu/startupMenuSync.ts`, `src-tauri/locales/en.yml`, `scripts/check-i18n-keys.ts`, `scripts/i18nIdenticalAllowlist.ts`
- rust: `set_locale` (`src-tauri/src/menu/localized.rs`, calls `rust_i18n::set_locale`)
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"Interface language"; `website/blog/2026-06-i18n-launch.md`
- tests: `src/i18n.test.ts`, `src/utils/localeDetect.test.ts`, `src/locales/__tests__/localeShape.test.ts`, `src/locales/__tests__/terminalI18nCoverage.test.ts`, `src/stores/__tests__/settingsStore.i18n.test.ts`, `src/pages/settings/__tests__/languagePicker.test.ts`
- notes: `startupMenuSync` is a side-effect import from `src/main.tsx`. 13 shortcut ids have no `shortcuts.label.*` key in any `src/locales/*/settings.json` (`graphvizDiagram, contentSearch, toggleSidebar, markdownSplit, readOnly, showInvisibles, validateMarkdown, lintNext, lintPrev, newBrowserTab, nextTab, prevTab, commandPalette`), so their Shortcuts-pane rows show the registry's English label in every language (see block `keyboard-shortcuts`).

### Auto-update
- id: auto-update
- feature: Auto-update
- summary: VMark checks for, downloads and installs its own updates through the Tauri updater plugin, with a user-set check frequency, optional auto-download, per-version skip, a progress card in About, status toasts, stall recovery and cross-window state sync.
- capabilities: `update.autoCheckEnabled`; `update.checkFrequency` `startup | daily | weekly | manual`; `update.autoDownload`; `update.lastCheckTimestamp`; `update.skipVersion` (set by the card's Skip button); 2 s startup delay; retry with exponential backoff (3 attempts, 5 s base) and a stable `update-retries-exhausted` toast id; 30 s check timeout (the download itself has no timeout); per-window single-flight guards with an explicit release valve; progress throttled to whole percents, or ~512 KB steps when the size is unknown; determinate / indeterminate / installing progress bar with `aria-valuetext`; restart-to-update via `restartWithHotExit`, with `update:restart-cancelled` resetting the button; stall detection (60 s check, 180 s transfer, 5 s tick) that raises a sticky "Update stalled" toast with a Retry action that resets the flow; status toasts under one id `status-update` — available (auto-dismissing, View), ready (sticky, Restart), transfer error (sticky, Retry); milestone logging via `update_log`; cross-window broadcast with JSON echo suppression; the updater sends an `X-Machine-Id` header (a hash)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `update.autoCheckEnabled = true`, `update.checkFrequency = "startup"`, `update.autoDownload = false`; updater endpoints in `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` (`https://log.vmark.app/update/latest.json` first, then the GitHub latest release), `createUpdaterArtifacts: true`, minisign pubkey
- surfaces: settings pane `about` (Check Now, Download, Skip, Restart to Update); status toasts; automatic check on startup
- code: `src/services/updates/updateFlows.ts`, `src/services/updates/updateSingleFlight.ts`, `src/services/updates/updateProgressThrottle.ts`, `src/services/updates/updateFlowLog.ts`, `src/hooks/useUpdateChecker.ts`, `src/hooks/useUpdateOperations.ts`, `src/hooks/useUpdateStall.ts`, `src/hooks/useUpdateSync.ts`, `src/hooks/useStatusToasts.ts`, `src/pages/settings/UpdateAvailableCard.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/AboutSettings.tsx`
- rust: `update_log` (`src-tauri/src/app_setup.rs`); updater is `tauri_plugin_updater`, registered in `src-tauri/src/app_plugins.rs`; `machine_id_hash` (`src-tauri/src/app_setup.rs`) supplies the `X-Machine-Id` header, attached in `src-tauri/src/app_plugins.rs`
- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"Updates", §"If an update gets stuck"; `website/guide/features.md` §"Auto-Update"; `website/guide/privacy.md` (what the update check sends, and how to block `log.vmark.app`)
- tests: `src/hooks/useUpdateChecker.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useUpdateChecker.comprehensive.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useUpdateOperations.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useUpdateStall.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useUpdateSync.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useStatusToasts.test.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/UpdateAvailableCard.test.tsx`, `src/services/updates/updateProgressThrottle.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/app_setup.test.rs` (`machine_id_hash` stable and matching its documented formula); no dedicated test for `src/services/updates/updateFlows.ts` or `src/services/updates/updateSingleFlight.ts`
- notes: `#1270`, `#1253`. There is no "Check for Updates…" native menu item or palette command. The entry points are the About pane and the startup timer. The claim that the updater fetches the same package format as the install (settings.md §"The update matches how you installed VMark") is unverified from source. The machine hash is SHA-256 of `vmark-machine-id-v1:` + hostname + OS + arch. `updater:default` is granted in both the default and the settings capability files, because Check Now runs inline in whichever window it was clicked from (#909).

### Homebrew cask publication
- id: homebrew-cask
- feature: Auto-update
- summary: Not user-facing: release CI pushes each new version to the `xiaolai/homebrew-tap` cask, so `brew upgrade vmark` tracks VMark releases.
- capabilities: `workflow_dispatch` with a version input validated as `X.Y.Z`; dispatched by `release.yml` after assets upload (continue-on-error there); serialized with `concurrency: update-homebrew-tap`, `cancel-in-progress: false`; downgrade/equal-version guard, re-checked against a freshly fetched tap right before the push; waits up to 15 minutes for DMGs; 25-minute job timeout; release cleanup in `release.yml` keeps the release the cask currently points at
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on (CI, needs the `HOMEBREW_TAP_TOKEN` secret)
- surfaces: automatic (CI); `brew install xiaolai/tap/vmark`
- code: `.github/workflows/update-homebrew.yml`, `.github/workflows/release.yml`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/download.md` §"Homebrew (Recommended)"
- tests: `scripts/check-release-dispatch.test.mjs` (asserts `release.yml` dispatches `update-homebrew.yml`); the tap-update steps themselves are not tested
- notes: `.github/workflows/` has no spine owner. This block sits under Auto-update because it is the other update channel.

### Shortcuts pane (rebinding)
- id: shortcuts-settings
- feature: Settings UI & persistence
- summary: A searchable, category-grouped list of every shortcut definition, each rebindable by pressing a chord, with a per-row reset, Reset All, and JSON import/export.
- capabilities: every definition is listed, including the 13 that ship unbound, which render as "Unassigned" and can be bound like any other row; search matches the English label, translated label, category, translated description, raw chord and display chord (or "Unassigned"); result count and a no-results line in search mode; six categories in fixed order — Formatting, Blocks, Navigation, Editing, View, File; per-row "customized" highlight plus a reset icon; Reset All behind a `confirmAction` warning dialog; Export writes `vmark-shortcuts.json` (`{version, customBindings}`) via a Blob download; Import from a hidden file input is atomic — every entry is validated (string key, known id) before anything is applied, so a file with one bad entry changes nothing
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: settings pane `shortcuts`
- code: `src/pages/settings/ShortcutsSettings.tsx`, `src/stores/settingsStore/shortcuts.ts`, `src/stores/settingsShortcutLabels.ts`
- rust: `menu::update_menu_accelerators` (via the store's debounced sync)
- docs: `website/guide/shortcuts.md` §"Customizing Shortcuts" (search, Unassigned rows, Assign / Escape, conflict and not-supported warnings, per-row reset, Reset All, import/export); `website/guide/settings.md` §"Shortcuts"
- tests: `src/pages/settings/ShortcutsSettings.confirm.test.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/ShortcutsSettings.conflict.test.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/ShortcutsSettings.unassigned.test.tsx`, `src/stores/settingsShortcuts.test.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore/shortcutsUnbind.test.ts`
- notes: import errors are shown with a raw `window.alert`, not the app's toast/dialog surface. The pane has no way to CLEAR a binding — the capture modal only assigns a chord, so unbinding a default is possible only through an imported JSON carrying `""` (the store honours `""` by presence). "Assign anyway" on a conflict does not unbind the previous owner, so both shortcuts keep the chord; for native-menu accelerators only one of the two then fires. Rows for the 13 ids without a `shortcuts.label.*` key show English in every locale (see *keyboard-shortcuts*).

### Key capture modal
- id: key-capture
- feature: Settings UI & persistence
- summary: A modal that records the next chord you press, shows how it will read, warns when another shortcut already owns it, and refuses chords the runtime resolver cannot map.
- capabilities: capture-phase window keydown with IME guard; Escape cancels; lone modifiers ignored; Space and arrow keys mapped to `Space`/`Up`/`Down`/`Left`/`Right`; on macOS Cmd records as `Mod` and Control as a separate literal `Ctrl`, so shipped `Ctrl-…` defaults stay re-enterable (off macOS both collapse to `Mod`); unmappable chords (e.g. a shifted symbol with no physical code token) are refused with a warning, validated by the same `canonicalizeChordString` the router uses; the conflict banner resolves against the chord just pressed and names the owning shortcut, and the confirm button reads "Assign Anyway"
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: settings pane `shortcuts` → click a key badge
- code: `src/pages/settings/KeyCapture.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/captureChord.ts`, `src/utils/keybinding/canonicalChord.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: `website/guide/shortcuts.md` §"Customizing Shortcuts" (the conflict and not-supported warnings)
- tests: `src/pages/settings/KeyCapture.test.tsx`, `src/pages/settings/captureChord.test.ts`, `src/utils/keybinding/canonicalChord.test.ts`, `src/pages/settings/ShortcutsSettings.conflict.test.tsx`
- notes: none

### Keyboard shortcut registry
- id: keyboard-shortcuts
- feature: Keyboard shortcuts
- summary: 132 named, rebindable shortcut ids in six categories; 113 are menu-backed (they carry a `menuId` and push their accelerator into the native menu) and 13 ship unbound.
- capabilities: Formatting (14; all menu-backed except `unlink`, `inlineMath`) — bold `Mod-b`, italic `Mod-i`, code `Mod-Shift-\``, strikethrough `Mod-Shift-x`, underline `Mod-u`, link `Mod-k`, unlink `Alt-Shift-k`, wikiLink `Alt-Mod-k`, bookmarkLink `Alt-Mod-b`, highlight `Mod-Shift-m`, inlineMath `Alt-Mod-m`, subscript `Alt-Mod-=`, superscript `Alt-Mod-Shift-=`, clearFormat `Mod-\`; Blocks (33, all menu-backed) — mathBlock `Alt-Mod-Shift-m`, diagram `Alt-Mod-Shift-d`, graphvizDiagram (unbound), mindmap `Alt-Mod-Shift-k`, heading1–6 `Mod-1..6`, paragraph `Mod-Shift-0`, increaseHeading `Mod-Alt-]`, decreaseHeading `Mod-Alt-[`, blockquote `Alt-Mod-q`, codeBlock `Alt-Mod-c`, bulletList `Alt-Mod-u`, orderedList `Alt-Mod-o`, taskList `Alt-Mod-x`, insertTable `Mod-Shift-t`, horizontalLine `Alt-Mod--`, insertToc (unbound), insertImage `Shift-Mod-i`, insertVideo (unbound), insertAudio (unbound), indent `Mod-]`, outdent `Mod-[`, formatTable `Alt-Mod-t`, insertNote `Alt-Mod-n`, insertTip `Mod-Alt-Shift-t`, insertWarning `Mod-Shift-w`, insertImportant `Mod-Alt-Shift-i`, insertCaution `Mod-Shift-u`, insertCollapsible `Alt-Mod-d`; Navigation (14; 8 menu-backed) — selectLine `Mod-l`, expandSelection `Ctrl-Shift-Up`, skipOccurrence `Mod-Shift-d`, selectAllOccurrencesInBlock `Alt-Mod-Shift-l` (#1418, sibling of the fixed document-wide `Mod-Shift-L`), softUndoCursor `Alt-Mod-z`, addCursorAbove `Mod-Alt-Up`, addCursorBelow `Mod-Alt-Down`, formatToolbar `Mod-Shift-b`, sourcePeek `F5`, findReplace `Mod-f`, findNext `Mod-g`, findPrevious `Mod-Shift-g`, useSelectionFind `Mod-e`, contentSearch `Mod-Shift-h`; Editing (19; 17 menu-backed) — formatCJKSelection `Mod-Shift-f`, formatCJKFile `Alt-Mod-Shift-f`, copyAsHTML `Mod-Shift-c`, pastePlainText `Mod-Shift-v`, toggleComment `Mod-/`, toggleQuoteStyle `Shift-Mod-'`, aiPrompts `Mod-y`, moveLineUp `Alt-Up`, moveLineDown `Alt-Down`, duplicateLine `Shift-Alt-Down`, deleteLine `Mod-Shift-k`, joinLines `Mod-j`, sortLinesAsc `F4`, sortLinesDesc `Shift-F4`, transformUppercase/Lowercase/TitleCase `Ctrl-Shift-u/l/t` (off macOS `Alt-Shift-u/l/t`), transformToggleCase (unbound), removeBlankLines (unbound); View (34; 27 menu-backed) — toggleSidebar `Ctrl-Shift-0` / off macOS `Alt-Shift-0` (no `menuId`), toggleOutline `Ctrl-Shift-1`, fileExplorer `Ctrl-Shift-2`, viewHistory `Ctrl-Shift-3`, knowledgeBase `Ctrl-Shift-4` (Developer Mode only), windowStatus `Ctrl-Shift-5`, sourceMode `F6`, markdownSplit `Shift-F6`, splitDocuments `Alt-Mod-\`, closePane `Alt-Mod-Shift-\`, focusOtherPane `Alt-Mod-Shift-o`, syncPaneScroll (unbound), toggleStatusBar `F7`, focusMode `F8`, typewriterMode `F9`, wordWrap `Alt-z`, lineNumbers `Alt-Mod-l`, toggleTerminal `Ctrl-\``, focusTerminal `Ctrl-Shift-\`` / off macOS `Alt-Shift-\``, diagramPreview `Alt-Mod-p`, fitTables (unbound), readOnly `F10`, showInvisibles `F3`, validateMarkdown `Alt-Mod-v`, lintNext `F2`, lintPrev `Shift-F2`, toggleHiddenFiles `Mod-Shift-.` / off macOS `Ctrl-h`, toggleAllFiles `Mod-Shift-a`, zoomActual `Mod-0`, zoomIn `Mod-=`, zoomOut `Mod--`, nextTab `Mod-Shift-]`, prevTab `Mod-Shift-[`, lastUsedTab `Ctrl-Tab`; File (18; 16 menu-backed) — newTab `Mod-t`, newBrowserTab `Alt-Mod-Shift-b`, newFile `Mod-n`, newWindow `Mod-Shift-n`, quickOpen `Mod-o`, commandPalette `Mod-Shift-p` (no `menuId`), openFile (unbound), openFolder `Mod-Shift-o`, save `Mod-s`, saveAs `Mod-Shift-s`, moveTo (unbound), closeFile `Mod-w`, reopenClosedTab (unbound), exportHTML (unbound), print `Mod-p` → menu `export-pdf`, exportPdf (unbound) → menu `export-pdf-native`, preferences `Mod-,`, saveAllQuit `Alt-Mod-Shift-q`
- status: shipped-on

- docs: `website/guide/shortcuts.md` (per-category tables plus F-key quick reference, Smart Tab Navigation, Table Editing, Popup Navigation, Math Block Editing, Terminal, Customizing Shortcuts)
- tests: `src/stores/settingsStore/shortcutDefinitions.test.ts`, `src/stores/settingsShortcuts.test.ts`, `src/stores/settingsStore/shortcutsUnbind.test.ts`, `src/utils/shortcutMatch.test.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/keybindingReferentialIntegrity.test.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/shortcutConsumerClosure.test.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/nativeOwnership.test.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/shortcutCanonicalization.test.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/bindingConflicts.test.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/crossWindowPropagation.test.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/undoRedoChords.test.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/splitPaneViewShortcut.test.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/mechanicExtractionGate.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/menu/accelerators.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/localized.test.rs`, `scripts/check-keybinding-manifest.test.mjs` (the `pnpm lint:keybinding-manifest` drift gate over registry / Rust menu / docs)

The `newBrowserTab` comment in `shortcutDefinitions.ts` still says the item "is disabled until `browser.enabled` is on" — it is hidden, not disabled (see *conditional-menu-items*). `toggleSidebar` has no `menuId` by design (a Rust accelerator would clash with `paragraph` off macOS); Alt-swapped off-macOS defaults exist for the transform trio, `toggleSidebar`, `focusTerminal` and `toggleHiddenFiles`.

### Binding registry and keybinding router
- id: keybinding-registry
- feature: Keyboard shortcuts
- summary: One declarative registry resolves every window-level chord against the active scope stack and the asking capture adapter, instead of scattered per-shortcut window listeners (ADR-018).
- capabilities: capture owners `window | prosemirror | codemirror | native-menu | wkwebview`; scope priorities `modal 60 > terminal 50 > editor-wysiwyg/editor-source 40 > input 30 > panel 20 > window 10`; per-binding `when`, `priority`, `windowPhase` (capture/bubble), `repeat`, `ime` (block / chord-exempt / editor-local), `consumption` (preventDefault / preventAndStop / passthrough); exact ties raise `AmbiguousBindingError` (logged, nothing fires) rather than first-wins; 38 bindings today — 22 view toggles (incl. `focusTerminal`, `windowStatus`), 13 global (palette, find-in-files, quick open, tabs, save, genie picker…), 2 file-explorer, and one `containment` binding (the `Mod-a` select-all guard outside text fields); every `command` binding dispatches through `executeCommand`; five native-owned bindings (`newBrowserTab`, `lastUsedTab`, `splitDocuments`, `closePane`, `focusOtherPane`) are indexed for conflict detection but executed only by the native menu accelerator, because a focused WKWebView consumes `window.keydown`
- status: shipped-on, partial
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic (one window keydown listener per document window, both phases)
- code: `src/services/keybinding/bindingRegistry.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/keybindingRegistry.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/keybindingDefinitions.ts`, `src/services/keybinding/bindingContext.ts`, `src/hooks/useKeybindingRouter.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: none (internal architecture)

### Native menu bar
- id: native-menus
- feature: Native menu bar & menu events
- summary: A fully localized native menu bar built in Rust — 8 top-level menus on macOS, 6 elsewhere, ~165 item ids — with per-item accelerators driven by the user's shortcut bindings.
- capabilities: VMark (`app-menu`, macOS only) — About, Settings…, Services, Hide/Hide Others/Show All, Save All and Quit, Quit; File (`file-menu`) — New, New Window, New Browser Tab (conditional), Quick Open, Open File…, Open Workspace…, Open Recent and Open Recent Workspace submenus (with Clear / empty placeholders), Close, Reopen Closed Tab, Close Workspace, Save, Save As, Move to…, Export submenu (Export HTML, Export PDF, Copy as HTML, Other Formats → Pandoc docx/epub/latex/odt/rtf/txt or a single install hint), Print (`export-pdf`, every platform), Document History submenu (Clear History / Clear Workspace History), and off macOS Settings, Save All and Exit, Quit; Edit (`edit-menu`) — Undo, Redo, Cut/Copy/Paste/Select All (predefined items on macOS and Linux, accelerator-free `edit-*` items on Windows), Find submenu (Find & Replace, Find Next, Find Previous, Use Selection for Find, Find in Files), Selection submenu (Select Word, Line, Block, Expand Selection), Lines submenu (Move Up/Down, Duplicate, Delete, Join, Remove Blank Lines, Sort Asc/Desc), Line Endings submenu (LF/CRLF), Genies submenu (built dynamically); Format (`format-menu`) — Bold, Italic, Underline, Strikethrough, Inline Code, Highlight, Subscript, Superscript, Clear Formatting, Headings / Lists / Blockquote / Transform submenus, Toggle Quote Style, CJK submenu, Text Cleanup submenu, Cleanup Images; Insert (`insert-menu`) — Links submenu, Image, Video, Audio, Table submenu (insert, rows/cols, delete, cell and column alignment, Format Table), Code Fences, Math Block, Diagram, Graphviz Diagram, Mindmap, Footnote, Horizontal Line, Collapsible Block, Table of Contents, Info Box submenu; View (`view-menu`) — WYSIWYG / Source / Markdown Split mode group, Split Documents, Close Pane, Focus Other Pane, Sync Pane Scroll, Last Used Tab, Focus Mode, Typewriter Mode, Zoom Actual/In/Out, Word Wrap, Universal Toolbar, Line Numbers, Diagram Preview, Fit Tables, Read-Only, Show Invisibles, Outline, File Explorer, History, Knowledge Base (conditional), Toggle Window Status, Toggle Terminal, Check Markdown, Next/Previous Issue; Window (`window-menu`, macOS only) — Minimize, Maximize, Coherence Breakdown, Bring All to Front; Help (`help-menu`) — VMark Help, Keyboard Shortcuts, Install CLI (macOS only), Report Issue, About (off macOS); every item carries an SF Symbol on macOS; off macOS the Settings and Export PDF windows are built with an empty menu so they do not inherit the application menu bar
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]` for the App menu, Window menu and Install CLI; `#[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]` tails for Settings/Exit/Quit/About; `#[cfg(target_os = "windows")]` for the accelerator-free clipboard items; conditional items per *conditional-menu-items*
- surfaces: native menu bar / accelerators
- code: `src-tauri/src/menu/localized.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/localized/*.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/mod.rs`, `src-tauri/src/macos_menu.rs`, `src-tauri/src/macos_menu_icons.rs`, `src-tauri/locales/en.yml`, `src/shared/menuIdExtraction.ts`, `src/shared/menu-ids.json`
- rust: `menu::rebuild_menu`, `menu::update_menu_accelerators`, `menu::set_locale`, `menu::update_recent_files`, `menu::update_recent_workspaces`, `menu::refresh_genies_menu`, `menu::hide_genies_menu`, `menu::sync_view_menu_state`, `menu::set_menu_item_visible`, `menu::debug_submenu_item_ids` (debug builds only)
- docs: `website/guide/index.md` §"Menu Bar"; `website/guide/shortcuts.md` (accelerators); `website/guide/export.md` (Pandoc submenu); `website/guide/knowledge-base.md` (Developer Mode gating); `website/guide/troubleshooting.md` §"Menu Bar Shows English After Language Change"
- tests: `src-tauri/src/menu/localized.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/localized/export_menu.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/accelerators.test.rs`, `src-tauri/src/macos_menu.test.rs`, `src/shared/menuIdExtraction.test.ts` (checked-in `menu-ids.json` vs Rust sources), `src/plugins/actions/actionRegistry.test.ts` (every Rust menu id mapped or excluded with a reason)
- notes: The `//!` header of `src-tauri/src/menu/localized/file_menu.rs` still says non-macOS "omits Print (macOS-only `pdf_export` backend)"; the code adds Print on every platform. `rebuild_menu` cannot distinguish a malformed user chord from a platform failure, so both report as `internal` (stated in `src-tauri/src/menu/commands.rs`). Windows clipboard items are accelerator-free because muda's predefined items desynced WebView2's modifier state (#1354). `src/shared/menuIdExtraction.ts` extracts ids only from literal `MenuItem::with_id(&app, "id", …)` call sites, so an id built from a variable is invisible to the `menu-ids.json` contract.

### Conditional menu items
- id: conditional-menu-items
- feature: Native menu bar & menu events
- summary: Native menu items whose feature is off are removed from the menu bar (not greyed) and restored in place when it turns on — today New Browser Tab and Knowledge Base.
- capabilities: one Rust table `CONDITIONAL_ITEMS` of `{id, anchor}` (`new-browser-tab` anchored on `new`, `knowledge-base` on `outline`); hiding removes the item and stashes it with its index, showing re-inserts it; desired visibility is kept independently of the menu tree and re-applied after every `rebuild_menu` (after the macOS icon pass, so a restored item keeps its SF Symbol) and after the first `set_menu` at startup; items are built disabled so a push that never lands leaves a greyed row, not a live dead one; reapply applies every item before reporting the first failure; frontend pusher recomputes on any settings change, pushes only on a difference, serialized latest-wins with backoff retry; predicates are the same ones the palette commands' `when` use — `browserAvailableHere` (macOS and `browser.enabled`) and `knowledgeBaseAvailableHere` (Developer Mode)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `browser.enabled = true` (macOS only) for New Browser Tab; `advanced.developerMode = false` for Knowledge Base
- surfaces: File → New Browser Tab, View → Knowledge Base
- code: `src-tauri/src/menu/conditional_items.rs`, `src/services/menu/conditionalMenuItemSync.ts`, `src/services/runtimeWiring.ts`
- rust: `menu::set_menu_item_visible`, `menu::debug_submenu_item_ids` (debug builds only)
- docs: `website/guide/shortcuts.md` tip "New Browser Tab"; `website/guide/knowledge-base.md`
- tests: `src-tauri/src/menu/conditional_items.test.rs` (parses the menu sources: anchor in the same submenu, no conditional anchor, built disabled), `src/services/menu/conditionalMenuItemSync.test.ts`
- notes: Tauri's `MenuItem` has no `set_visible`, which is why hiding is remove-and-restore.

### Differential accelerator updates
- id: menu-accelerator-diff
- feature: Native menu bar & menu events
- summary: Rebinding a shortcut mutates only the menu items whose accelerator actually changed, instead of rebuilding the whole menu tree.
- capabilities: `ACCEL_CACHE` baseline committed only after a successful full build; live-tree walk per diff; 100 ms trailing debounce on the frontend so Reset All / Import produce one update; single-flight promise chain; a malformed chord is `invalid-input` carrying the menu id and offending string in `detail`, everything else is `internal`
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic (on any shortcut edit, reset or import)
- code: `src-tauri/src/menu/accelerators.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/commands.rs`, `src/stores/settingsStore/shortcuts.ts`
- rust: `menu::update_menu_accelerators`
- docs: none
- tests: `src-tauri/src/menu/accelerators.test.rs`, `src/stores/settingsShortcuts.test.ts`
- notes: a rejected update is only written to the log (`shortcutsWarn`); the Shortcuts pane shows nothing. The capture modal's unmappable-chord refusal keeps most malformed chords from reaching Rust. Rationale for the diff path is #825 (a full rebuild froze the Settings window on Windows).

### View-menu state sync

- code: `src/hooks/useViewMenuStateSync.ts`, `src-tauri/src/menu/menu_state.rs`

- tests: `src/hooks/useViewMenuStateSync.test.ts`; inline `#[cfg(test)]` module in `src-tauri/src/menu/menu_state.rs` (5 tests)
- notes: `#1070`, `#1082`.

### Native menu rebuild pipeline
- id: menu-rebuild
- feature: Native menu bar & menu events
- summary: A full menu rebuild re-translates every label, re-applies accelerators, and re-populates the dynamic submenus a rebuild resets.
- capabilities: `rebuildNativeMenu()` invokes `rebuild_menu`, then `refresh_genies_menu`, then re-syncs recent files and recent workspaces; Rust side re-applies macOS menu fixes/icons, then conditional-item visibility; startup rebuild (after `set_locale`) when the persisted language is non-English
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic — startup (non-English), language change in Settings → Language (with revert on failure), Pandoc detect in Settings → Files & Images
- code: `src/services/menu/rebuildNativeMenu.ts`, `src/services/menu/startupMenuSync.ts`, `src-tauri/src/menu/commands.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/dynamic.rs`
- rust: `menu::rebuild_menu`, `menu::refresh_genies_menu`, `menu::update_recent_files`, `menu::update_recent_workspaces`, `menu::set_locale`
- docs: none
- tests: `src/services/menu/rebuildNativeMenu.test.ts`; no Rust tests for `commands.rs`; for `dynamic.rs` only the submenu layout (`src-tauri/src/menu/dynamic_layout.test.rs`)
- notes: `startupMenuSync` runs as a side-effect import from `src/main.tsx`.

### Dynamic menu items (recents, genies)
- id: menu-dynamic-items
- feature: Native menu bar & menu events
- summary: Open Recent, Open Recent Workspace and Edit → Genies are repopulated at runtime from frontend state without rebuilding the whole menu bar.
- capabilities: `update_recent_files` / `update_recent_workspaces` replace the submenu contents (filename per item, or an empty placeholder), then a separator and a Clear item that is disabled when the list is empty; `refresh_genies_menu` creates the Genies submenu inside Edit on demand — "Search Genies…" (accelerator from the user's `search-genies` binding, default `CmdOrCtrl+Y`), root-level genies flat and categorized ones as group submenus, then Reload Genies and Open Genies Folder; `hide_genies_menu` removes the submenu and its separator; a click resolves `recent-file-N` / `recent-workspace-N` / genie items by index against snapshot `Mutex`es captured at build time (`get_recent_file_path`, `get_recent_workspace_path`, `get_genie_path`)
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; the Genies submenu exists only while `src/hooks/useGenieShortcuts.ts` is mounted
- surfaces: File → Open Recent, File → Open Recent Workspace, Edit → Genies
- code: `src-tauri/src/menu/dynamic.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/dynamic_layout.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/mod.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/commands.rs`, `src/stores/workspaceStoreHelpers.ts`, `src/hooks/useGenieShortcuts.ts`, `src/services/menu/rebuildNativeMenu.ts`
- rust: `menu::update_recent_files`, `menu::update_recent_workspaces`, `menu::refresh_genies_menu`, `menu::hide_genies_menu`
- docs: `website/guide/ai-genies.md`; `website/guide/workspace-management.md` (recent lists)
- tests: `src-tauri/src/menu/events/dispatch.test.rs` (index parsing and classification of the dynamic ids); `src-tauri/src/menu/dynamic_layout.test.rs` (the ids, labels, Search Genies accelerator and genie grouping `dynamic.rs` builds from); building and inserting the native items in `src-tauri/src/menu/dynamic.rs` has no test
- notes: `rebuild_menu` resets all three submenus, so `src/services/menu/rebuildNativeMenu.ts` re-invokes `refresh_genies_menu` and re-syncs both recent lists after every rebuild (see *menu-rebuild*).

### Menu → command dispatch
- id: menu-command-dispatch
- feature: Native menu bar & menu events
- summary: Rust routes a menu click to the focused (or a fallback) document window as `menu:{id}`; one frontend dispatcher owns the whole `menu:*` space and routes each id to a CommandBus command or to the editor-action executor.
- capabilities: Rust side — pure `classify_menu_id`, readiness tracking with queue-until-ready and atomic flush, recent-file/workspace paths resolved from menu-build snapshots, Quit and Settings handled in Rust, focused → any-document-window fallback; frontend — single `mountMenuCommands` with a mount-time duplicate-rejection pass; binding groups MISC (23, incl. Windows `edit-*` clipboard ids and `bring-all-to-front`), EXPORT (5), WORKSPACE (2), RECENT_FILES (2), RECENT_WORKSPACES (2), VIEW (31), EDITOR_ACTION (87, from `MENU_TO_ACTION`), plus Pandoc format bindings discovered at runtime; a binding that cannot listen costs only its own item and readiness reports `false`; `menuCommandsReady` barrier signalled only from a live (non-StrictMode-cancelled) setup
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: native menu / automatic
- code: `src/hooks/useCommandBootstrap.ts`, `src/services/commands/menuListener.ts`, `src/services/commands/menuCommandsReady.ts`, `src/plugins/actions/menuMapping.ts`, `src-tauri/src/menu/events.rs`, `src-tauri/src/menu/events/dispatch.rs`
- rust: `menu::events::handle_menu_event` (not a command)
- docs: none
- tests: `src/services/commands/menuListener.test.ts`, `src/services/commands/menuListener.editorActions.test.tsx`, `src/services/commands/menuCommandsReady.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useCommandBootstrap.test.tsx`, `src/hooks/useCommandBootstrap.bindings.test.ts`, `src/hooks/menuRouting.test.ts`, `src-tauri/src/menu/events/dispatch.test.rs`
- notes: the generic fallback (`emit_generic` in `src-tauri/src/menu/events/dispatch.rs`) targets the focused DOCUMENT window, else any document window — a Windows menu click can momentarily defocus the webview, and document events must never reach the Settings window. `close` carries the target label in its payload because `window.emit` broadcasts.

### Command palette
- id: command-palette

- summary: `Mod+Shift+P` opens a ranked, searchable command list; an empty query browses every available command grouped by category, a query flattens to best-scoring matches.

### Command registry (CommandBus)
- id: command-registry
- feature: Command palette
- summary: One bus owns command registration, availability, ranking and execution; the menu dispatcher, keybinding router, palette, MCP bridge and programmatic callers all go through it.
- capabilities: ~81 declared command ids across 15 registrars (`misc, window, clipboard, export, workspace, recentFiles, recentWorkspaces, view` — which also registers pane, lint, explorer and zoom commands — `claim, format, browser, tab, file, genie`, and the editor bridge); the editor bridge lifts 85 `ActionId`s, projecting `setHeading` into six rows `editor.setHeading.1..6`; Pandoc format commands registered at runtime (one per discovered format); duplicate ids throw; owner batches (`registerCommands`) are replace-own and HMR-safe; `registerAllCommands` snapshots the registry and restores it exactly if any registrar throws, so the remount retry starts clean; definitions are `readonly`; `LocalizedString` titles resolved at display time; `commandText` folds and scores labels and survives a throwing title getter; `reportCommandFailure` is the one "log it and tell the user" path for contained command failures
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: command palette / native menu / keybindings / MCP
- code: `src/services/commands/CommandBus.ts`, `src/services/commands/commandText.ts`, `src/services/commands/commandFailure.ts`, `src/services/commands/registerAllCommands.ts`, `src/services/commands/editorCommandBridge.ts`, `src/services/commands/actionAvailability.ts`, `src/services/commands/commandContext.ts`, `src/plugins/actions/actionRegistry.ts`
- rust: none
- docs: none (`.claude/adr/ADR-012-command-bus-as-single-intent-path.md`)
- tests: `src/services/commands/CommandBus.test.ts`, `src/services/commands/commandText.test.ts`, `src/services/commands/commandFailure.test.ts`, `src/services/commands/registerAllCommands.test.ts`, `src/services/commands/registrationFlag.test.ts`, `src/services/commands/editorCommandBridge.test.ts`, `src/services/commands/actionAvailability.test.ts`, `src/services/commands/commandContext.test.ts`, plus one test file per command group under `src/services/commands/`
- notes: none

### App shell (mounted surfaces)
- id: app-shell
- feature: App chrome (titlebar/statusbar/welcome)
- summary: Not user-facing: `AppShell` is a pure four-slot layout primitive (chrome / sidebar / primary / overlays); `src/App.tsx` is the composition root that fills it, and a checked-in identity list pins exactly which surfaces are mounted.
- capabilities: chrome slot typed `ReactElement | null` and reserved only when filled, so off macOS no 40px band is held; `--chrome-height` and `--shell-side-width` published from the shell root; sidebar stack composing the WorkspaceRail (when `general.workspaceRailMode` is on in a document window) and the resizable Sidebar; `EditorArea` composes editor + 40px bottom-bar lane + terminal panel at top/bottom/left/right + a full-height right `sidePanel` (Knowledge Base) that displaces rather than occludes; root modifier classes via `appShellClassName` (focus-mode, typewriter-mode, find-bar-open, browser-workspace-active, workspace-rail-visible); routes `/` (MainLayout), `/settings`, `/pdf-export`, each non-root route and the editor/terminal wrapped in `FeatureErrorBoundary` under an app-level `ErrorBoundary`; shell-slot identity list (`scripts/shell-slots-baseline.json`, 20 surfaces): ApprovalDialog, AppTitleBar, BottomBar, BrowserApprovalDialog, CoherenceOverlays, CommandPalette, ContentSearch, DocumentSplitContainer, DropOverlay, EditorContextMenu, GeniePickerOverlay, KnowledgeBaseOverlay, QuickLookOverlay, QuickOpen, Sidebar, SidebarResizeHandle, TerminalPanel, WindowStatusOverlay, WorkspaceApprovalDialog, WorkspaceRail
- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on; `usesOverlayTitleBar()` decides whether the chrome slot is filled
- surfaces: automatic
- code: `src/shell/AppShell.tsx`, `src/shell/EditorArea.tsx`, `src/shell/appShellClassName.ts`, `src/shell/app-shell.css`, `src/App.tsx`, `scripts/shell-slots-baseline.json`, `scripts/check-shell-slots.mjs`
- rust: none
- docs: none (`.claude/adr/ADR-007-shell-as-composition-root.md`)
- tests: `src/shell/AppShell.test.tsx`, `src/shell/AppShell.a11y.test.tsx`, `src/shell/EditorArea.test.tsx`, `src/shell/appShellClassName.test.ts`, `src/App.chrome.test.tsx` (the platform→chrome ternary in App.tsx), `scripts/check-shell-slots.test.ts`; the gate runs as `pnpm lint:shell-slots` inside `check:static`
- notes: the baseline is a two-way ratchet — a mounted surface missing from the list fails, and a listed surface no longer mounted fails until its entry is deleted; entries are only removed. Excluded by definition: Suspense/FeatureErrorBoundary/Fragment wrappers and the three named bindings `EditorArea`, `DocumentWindowMount`, `MainWindowRunners`. There is no slot-registration mechanism; a new surface is an edit to `src/App.tsx`.

### Shell chrome geometry
- id: shell-chrome
- feature: App chrome (titlebar/statusbar/welcome)
- summary: Not user-facing: one module owns every chrome measurement — bar height, traffic-light clearance, card inset and radius, workspace-rail width — and publishes them as CSS variables that both the document window and the Settings window inherit.
- capabilities: `BAR_HEIGHT = 40` (title bar, status bar, horizontal terminal bar and chrome strip share it); `WORKSPACE_RAIL_WIDTH = 30`; `WINDOW_RADIUS = 21` (measured; tao has no corner-radius API); `SHELL_CARD_INSET = 8`, `SHELL_CARD_RADIUS = 13` (derived concentrically); `SHELL_TOP_INSET = max(CHROME_HEIGHT, TRAFFIC_LIGHTS_CLEARANCE)`; `shellSideWidth()` shared with the terminal's layout maths; `shellChromeVars(overlayTitleBar)` publishes `--bar-height`, `--workspace-rail-width`, `--shell-top-inset`, `--traffic-lights-zone`, `--traffic-lights-centre`, `--shell-card-inset`, `--shell-card-radius` — the light-derived values collapse to 0 off macOS, the card values do not; `trafficLights.ts` derives top, centre, clearance, reach and zone from one `TRAFFIC_LIGHT_POSITION = {x: 19, y: 28}`
- status: shipped-on
- gate: `usesOverlayTitleBar()` for the traffic-light values; always on otherwise
- surfaces: automatic
- code: `src/shell/shellChrome.ts`, `src/shell/trafficLights.ts`, `src/pages/Settings.tsx`, `src/styles/index.css`
- rust: `window_manager::traffic_lights` (`TRAFFIC_LIGHT_POSITION`, `apply_traffic_light_position`, macOS-only) used by the document, settings and PDF-export window builders; `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` `trafficLightPosition` for the main window; needs the `macos-private-api` tauri feature
- docs: none
- tests: `src/shell/shellChrome.test.ts`, `src/shell/trafficLights.test.ts` (reads `tauri.conf.json`, `traffic_lights.rs` and the TS constant and fails if they disagree), `src/shell/barHeight.test.ts`, `src/components/TitleBar/titleBarShellOffset.test.ts`
- notes: the position is declared in three places no compiler joins; the test above is the only link. `:root` in `src/styles/index.css` carries static defaults so consumers without a fallback still resolve before the shell mounts.

### Frontend bootstrap
- id: frontend-bootstrap

- id: quick-look
- feature: App chrome (titlebar/statusbar/welcome)
- summary: Finder-style spacebar preview: select a file in the explorer tree, press Space, and a full-window overlay previews it, with arrow keys stepping through the tree's visible files.

- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on

- code: `src/components/QuickLook/QuickLookOverlay.tsx`, `src/stores/quickLookStore.ts`, `src/components/Sidebar/FileExplorer/useQuickLookHotkey.ts`, `src/components/Editor/MediaView/MediaView.tsx`

The static asset scope in `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` is now the fs roots (`$HOME/**`, `/Volumes/**`, `/mnt/**`, `/media/**`, plus `C:\` to `F:\` via `src-tauri/tauri.windows.conf.json`), so a media file outside them previews only through that grant — which any media file anywhere can obtain (see the media area). The hotkey hook is owned by the Sidebar feature (area 4).

### Resilient UI chrome (boundaries, lazy retry, toasts)
- id: resilient-chrome

- status: shipped-on
- gate: always on
- surfaces: automatic; toast actions (Restart, Retry on a stalled or failed update, open settings)
- code: `src/components/FeatureErrorBoundary.tsx`, `src/components/RetryableLazy.tsx`, `src/components/ThemedToaster.tsx`, `src/components/toastIcons.tsx`, `src/hooks/useStatusToasts.ts`, `src/App.tsx`

- docs: `website/guide/settings.md` §"If an update gets stuck" (the sticky "Update stalled" notification and its Retry button)
- tests: `src/components/FeatureErrorBoundary.test.tsx`, `src/components/RetryableLazy.test.tsx`, `src/components/ThemedToaster.test.tsx`, `src/components/toastIcons.test.tsx`, `src/hooks/useStatusToasts.test.tsx`
